📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期乙型肝炎病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 喀麦隆 HBV 总体 患病率 为 9.98%(95% 置信区间 [CI] 9.00-11.02),异质性较高 (I 2 = 98.10%)。相对较低风险和较高风险人群的 患病率 分别为 8.94% (95% CI 7.84-10.11) 和 12.70% (95% CI 10.28-15.32)。总体而言,喀麦隆的 HBV 患病率 仍然很高。
  • 在 199,143 名接受治疗的患者中(中位随访时间为 7.3 年),15,233 名患者发展为 HCC。调整后的年度 HCC 发病率 在不同风险层中逐渐增加:低风险组为 0.31% (95% CI 0.24–0.40),中风险组为 1.09% (0.84–1.41),高风险组为 1.85% (1.44–2.40)。
  • 使用探针捕获方法从病毒载量 > 1000 IU/mL、使用 PCR-Illumina 方法 > 200 IU/mL 和使用 PCR-Nanopore 方法 > 10 IU/mL 的样本中成功获得完整 HBV 基因组。宏基因组方法未能在任何样本中检测到 HBV。
  • 初选系列结束后一个月,所有三组的血清保护率都很高且相似。与 3 剂量组相比,2 剂量组的 GMT 较低。攻击剂量后,所有组的记忆反应相似,异源 2 剂量组显示的 GMT 在数值上高于 3 剂量组。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • Overall prevalence of HBV in Cameroon was 9.98% (95% confidence interval [CI] 9.00-11.02) with high heterogeneity (I 2 = 98.10%).
  • Among 199,143 treated patients (median follow-up, 7.3 years), 15,233 developed HCC.
  • Full HBV genomes were successfully obtained from samples with viral loads > 1000 IU/mL using probe-capture methods, > 200 IU/mL using PCR-Illumina methods, and > 10 IU/mL using PCR-Nanopore methods.
  • One month after the primary series, seroprotection rates were high and similar across all three groups. GMTs were lower in the 2-dose groups compared to the 3-dose group. [10.1093/infdis/jiag122].

📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 本期乙型肝炎病毒新闻集中呈现以下动态。
  • 一项回顾性 队列研究 研究发现,戒烟与 慢性乙型肝炎 患者患肝细胞癌的风险降低 22% 相关,但并未建立因果关系。该研究还指出,改用电子烟也显示出类似的风险降低,但戒烟的一致性较差。 [news-edc7d9e8d48dde39]。
  • 在埃塞俄比亚进行的一项研究表明,干血斑可以可靠地定量 HBV DNA 并检测 HBeAg,这是 乙型肝炎病毒 感染的两个关键标志物。Bioengineer.org 于 2026 年 9 月 3 日报道了该研究。该事件根据该研究结果得到证实。
  • GSK 获得全球首个监管部门批准 Hibsago(bepirovirsen)作为治疗 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒感染的 功能性治愈。此次批准基于 III 期试验数据,该数据显示 功能性治愈 患者中 功能性治愈 的发生率显着。
  • 《美国管理护理杂志》发表了一篇题为“如何推进乙型肝炎消除”的评论,作者为公共卫生硕士 Helen Nde。这篇文章是指导性讨论,而不是已确认的 暴发 或调查的报告。 [news-000c96dc6572e5c1]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following Hepatitis B virus news developments.
  • A retrospective cohort study found that quitting smoking was associated with a 22% lower risk of hepatocellular carcinoma in patients with chronic hepatitis B, though it did not establish a causal relationship.
  • A study conducted in Ethiopia demonstrated that dried blood spots can reliably quantify HBV DNA and detect HBeAg, two key markers for hepatitis B virus infection.
  • GSK received the world's first regulatory approval for Hibsago (bepirovirsen) as a functional cure for chronic hepatitis B virus infection.
  • The American Journal of Managed Care published a commentary titled “What It Will Take to Advance Hepatitis B Elimination” authored by Helen Nde, MPH.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: The Journal of infectious diseases  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-09-01  |  DOI: 10.1093/infdis/jiag122  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 234, 2, e243-e253
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

2-18月龄儿童接种2剂与3剂乙型肝炎疫苗疫苗的免疫原性:一项随机临床试验

Immunogenicity of Two Versus Three Doses of Hepatitis B Vaccine When Administered to Children Aged 2-18 Months: A Randomized Clinical Trial.
作者： Koffi Barnabé Kegbevi, Yossi Febriani, Chantal Sauvageau, Marie-Michelle Ursu, Caroline Quach, Michaël Desjardins, Julie Bestman-Smith, Vladimir Gilca, Nicholas Brousseau
摘要中文翻译背景:2剂乙型肝炎疫苗疫苗接种方案在≥1岁儿童中高度免疫原性,但婴儿期数据缺乏。本研究旨在比较2剂与3剂乙型肝炎疫苗疫苗接种方案在该人群中的免疫原性。方法:实验组儿童于2月龄时招募,随机分配接受同源(Infanrix-hexa/Infanrix-hexa)或异源(Infanrix-hexa/Twinrix)2剂方案,于2月龄和12月龄接种。对照组儿童于18月龄时招募,已接受同源3剂方案(Infanrix-hexa/Infanrix-hexa/Infanrix-hexa),于2月龄、4月龄和18月龄接种。所有组别均于3年后接种加强剂量(Twinrix)。结果:主要免疫程序完成后1个月,3组血清保护率(抗-HBs ≥10 mIU/mL)均较高且相似(异源2剂组91.7%,同源2剂组90.9%,3剂组94.2%)。几何平均滴度(GMT)在2剂组低于3剂组。加强剂量后,大多数受试者均表现出回忆应答,且3组间相似(异源2剂组97.2%,同源2剂组95.5%,3剂组92.7%)。GMT在异源2剂组(9380.5 mIU/mL)数值上高于3剂组(6900.6 mIU/mL)(P = .4)。异源2剂方案的局部反应发生率显著低于同源2剂方案。结论:2剂(2月龄和12月龄)与3剂(2月龄、4月龄和18月龄)乙型肝炎疫苗疫苗接种方案完成3年后的回忆应答相似。异源2剂方案较同源2剂方案更具免疫原性且反应原性更低。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在比较 2-18 个月儿童中 2 剂和 3 剂乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 方案的免疫原性,因为尽管 2 剂方案对年龄较大的儿童具有高免疫原性,但婴儿数据有限。
设计与对象
进行了一项随机临床试验,将两种 2 剂量方案(同源和异源)与 3 剂量方案进行比较,该试验涉及在不同年龄招募并按指定时间间隔接受 疫苗 的儿童。
核心方法
儿童在 2 个月和 12 个月时被随机分配接受同源或异源 2 剂方案,或在 2、4 和 18 个月时接受同源 3 剂方案。3 年后,所有组均接受攻击剂量 (Twinrix)。
主要结果
初选系列结束后一个月,所有三组的血清保护率都很高且相似。与 3 剂量组相比,2 剂量组的 GMT 较低。攻击剂量后,所有组的记忆反应相似,异源 2 剂量组显示的 GMT 在数值上高于 3 剂量组。
解释与创新
作者得出结论,2 剂量和 3 剂量方案之间的记忆反应相似,但异源 2 剂量方案比同源 2 剂量方案具有更高的免疫原性和更低的反应原性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,对于 2-18 个月的儿童,2 剂 疫苗接种 方案可能与 3 剂方案一样有效,这可能为婴儿的 疫苗接种 策略提供参考。
局限与证据强度
该研究的设计和结果因缺乏长期随访和可能存在混杂因素而受到限制,但由于随机临床试验的设置以及免疫原性和反应原性的直接比较,证据是强有力的。

Immunogenicity of Two Versus Three Doses of Hepatitis B Vaccine When Administered to Children Aged 2-18 Months: A Randomized Clinical Trial.

Authors: Koffi Barnabé Kegbevi, Yossi Febriani, Chantal Sauvageau, Marie-Michelle Ursu, Caroline Quach, Michaël Desjardins, Julie Bestman-Smith, Vladimir Gilca, Nicholas Brousseau
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: A 2-dose hepatitis B vaccination schedule is highly immunogenic in children aged ≥1 year, but data for infants are scarce. Our goal was to compare the immunogenicity of 2- and 3-dose hepatitis B vaccination schedules in this population. METHODS: Children in the experimental group were recruited at 2 months of age and randomized to receive a homologous (Infanrix-hexa/Infanrix-hexa) or heterologous (Infanrix-hexa/Twinrix) 2-dose schedule at 2 and 12 months. Children in the control group were recruited at 18 months and had received a homologous 3-dose schedule (Infanrix-hexa/Infanrix-hexa/Infanrix-hexa) at 2, 4, and 18 months. All groups received a challenge dose (Twinrix) 3 years later. RESULTS: One month after the primary series, seroprotection rates (anti-HBs ≥10 mIU/mL) were high and similar among the 3 groups (heterologous 2-dose, 91.7%; homologous 2-dose, 90.9%; 3-dose, 94.2%). Geometric mean titers (GMTs) were lower in the 2-dose groups than in the 3-dose group. Post-challenge dose, the majority exhibited an anamnestic response, similar across all 3 groups (heterologous 2-dose, 97.2%; homologous 2-dose, 95.5%; 3-dose, 92.7%). GMTs were numerically higher in the heterologous 2-dose group (9380.5 mIU/mL) than in the 3-dose group (6900.6 mIU/mL) (P = .4). The proportion exhibiting local reactions was significantly lower after the heterologous 2-dose schedule than after the homologous 2-dose schedule. CONCLUSIONS: The anamnestic response 3 years after 2-dose (2 and 12 months) and 3-dose (2, 4, and 18 months) hepatitis B vaccination schedules was similar. The heterologous 2-dose schedule was more immunogenic and less reactogenic than the homologous 2-dose schedule.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to compare the immunogenicity of 2- and 3-dose hepatitis B vaccination schedules in children aged 2-18 months, as data on infants are limited despite the high immunogenicity of 2-dose schedules in older children.
Design and population
A randomized clinical trial was conducted comparing two 2-dose schedules (homologous and heterologous) with a 3-dose schedule, involving children recruited at different ages and receiving vaccines at specified intervals.
Core methods
Children were randomized to receive either a homologous or heterologous 2-dose schedule at 2 and 12 months, or a homologous 3-dose schedule at 2, 4, and 18 months. All groups received a challenge dose (Twinrix) 3 years later.
Main results
One month after the primary series, seroprotection rates were high and similar across all three groups. GMTs were lower in the 2-dose groups compared to the 3-dose group. Post-challenge dose, anamnestic responses were similar across all groups, with the heterologous 2-dose group showing numerically higher GMTs than the 3-dose group.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that the anamnestic response was similar between 2-dose and 3-dose schedules, but the heterologous 2-dose schedule was more immunogenic and less reactogenic than the homologous 2-dose schedule.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that a 2-dose vaccination schedule may be as effective as a 3-dose schedule in children aged 2-18 months, potentially informing vaccination strategies for infants.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's design and results are limited by the lack of long-term follow-up and the potential for confounding factors, but the evidence is strong due to the randomized clinical trial setup and direct comparisons of immunogenicity and reactogenicity.

ALG-020572,一种针对 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒感染的 治疗 的反义寡核苷酸,因药物引起的肝损伤而停产。

ALG-020572, an Antisense Oligonucleotide for the Treatment of Chronic Hepatitis B Virus Infection Discontinued for Drug-Induced Liver Injury.
作者： Kosh Agarwal, Dan Forton, Yoh Zen, Min Wu, Tse-I Lin, Kha Le, Sushmita Chanda, Lawrence Blatt, John Fry, Ed Gane
摘要中文翻译目前针对慢性 HBV 感染的 治疗 方案并不理想,因为它们无法抑制 HBsAg 水平。ALG-020572 是一种反义寡核苷酸,旨在通过降解 HBV mRNA 来减少病毒蛋白合成。ALG-020572-401 是一项双盲、随机、安慰剂对照试验,由两部分组成。第 1 部分(单剂量递增)评估了单剂量 ALG-020572 或安慰剂在健康参与者中的药代动力学、安全性和耐受性。在第 2 部分(多次给药)中,患有非肝硬化 HBeAg 阴性、病毒学抑制的慢性 HBV 感染的参与者服用最多 7 剂 ALG-020572,以评估安全性、药代动力学和 抗病毒药物 活性。在第 1 部分中,32 名参与者被随机分配至 ALG 020572 或安慰剂组。单剂量 ALG-020572 高达 480 mg 的耐受性良好。最常见的 治疗 突发不良事件是注射部位反应。ALG-020572 被迅速吸收,血浆暴露量随剂量增加而增加。在第 2 部分中,招募了 8 名非肝硬化 HBeAg 阴性病毒学抑制的 慢性乙型肝炎 感染参与者,并接受了最多 7 剂 ALG-020572。在 4 名参与者出现谷丙转氨酶显着升高(随后归因于药物引起的肝损伤)后,该研究提前终止。单剂量的 ALG-020572 在健康参与者中表现出良好的药代动力学和安全性。出乎意料的是,ALG-020572在慢性乙型肝炎感染的参与者中耐受性较差,导致该研究提前终止,并因特异质药物引起的肝损伤而进一步开发ALG-020572,表明此类药物的开发需要谨慎。试验注册:在 ClinicalTrials.gov 注册:NCT0500102。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
目前针对慢性 HBV 感染的 治疗 方案并不理想,因为它们无法抑制 HBsAg 水平。该研究旨在评估 ALG-020572 在慢性 HBV 感染患者中的安全性和 抗病毒药物 活性。
设计与对象
ALG-020572-401 是一项双盲、随机、安慰剂对照试验,由两部分组成。第 1 部分评估了 32 名健康参与者的单剂量,第 2 部分涉及 8 名非肝硬化 HBeAg 阴性、病毒学抑制的慢性 HBV 感染参与者。
核心方法
该研究通过单次递增剂量和多次给药对慢性 HBV 感染参与者评估 ALG-020572 的药代动力学、安全性、耐受性和 抗病毒药物 活性。
主要结果
报告的最常见 治疗 相关新发不良事件为注射部位反应。单次剂量的 ALG-020572 在健康受试者中显示出良好的药代动力学和安全性特征。然而,在4名受试者因药物诱导性肝损伤出现显著丙氨酸氨基转移酶升高后,研究被提前终止。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,慢性 HBV 感染参与者对 ALG-020572 的耐受性较差,导致提前终止,并强调开发此类药物需要谨慎。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果提醒人们在开发针对乙型肝炎病毒的反义寡核苷酸时需谨慎,因其存在药物诱导性肝损伤风险,这对未来药物设计和安全性评估具有重要意义。
局限与证据强度
由于安全问题,该研究提前终止,这限制了全面评估长期疗效和安全性的能力。由于样本量有限且提前终止,证据不充分。

ALG-020572, an Antisense Oligonucleotide for the Treatment of Chronic Hepatitis B Virus Infection Discontinued for Drug-Induced Liver Injury.

Authors: Kosh Agarwal, Dan Forton, Yoh Zen, Min Wu, Tse-I Lin, Kha Le, Sushmita Chanda, Lawrence Blatt, John Fry, Ed Gane
Original abstract (English)
Current treatment options for chronic HBV infection are suboptimal in that they fail to suppress HBsAg levels. ALG-020572 is an antisense oligonucleotide designed to reduce viral protein synthesis through degradation of HBV mRNA. ALG-020572-401 was a double-blind, randomized, placebo-controlled trial consisting of two parts. Part 1 (single-ascending doses) evaluated the pharmacokinetics, safety and tolerability of single doses of ALG-020572 or placebo in healthy participants. In Part 2 (multiple dosing), participants with non-cirrhotic HBeAg-negative, virologically suppressed chronic HBV infection were administered up to 7 doses of ALG-020572 to evaluate safety, pharmacokinetics and antiviral activity. In Part 1, 32 participants were randomized to ALG 020572 or placebo. Single doses of ALG-020572 up to 480 mg were well tolerated. The most common treatment-emergent adverse event reported was injection site reaction. ALG-020572 was rapidly absorbed and plasma exposures increased with dose. In Part 2, 8 participants with non-cirrhotic HBeAg-negative virologically suppressed chronic hepatitis B infection were enrolled and received up to 7 doses of ALG-020572. The study was prematurely discontinued after 4 participants experienced significant alanine aminotransferase elevations that were subsequently attributed to drug-induced liver injury. Single doses of ALG-020572 demonstrated a favourable pharmacokinetic and safety profile in healthy participants. Unexpectedly, ALG-020572 was poorly tolerated in participants with chronic HBV infection, resulting in the early termination of the study and further development of ALG-020572 due to idiosyncratic drug-induced liver injury, suggesting caution is required in the development of this class of drugs. Trial Registration: Registered at clinicaltrials.gov: NCT0500102.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Current treatment options for chronic HBV infection are suboptimal in that they fail to suppress HBsAg levels. The study aimed to evaluate the safety and antiviral activity of ALG-020572 in patients with chronic HBV infection.
Design and population
ALG-020572-401 was a double-blind, randomized, placebo-controlled trial consisting of two parts. Part 1 evaluated single doses in 32 healthy participants, while Part 2 involved 8 participants with non-cirrhotic HBeAg-negative, virologically suppressed chronic HBV infection.
Core methods
The study assessed pharmacokinetics, safety, tolerability, and antiviral activity of ALG-020572 through single-ascending doses and multiple dosing in participants with chronic HBV infection.
Main results
The most common treatment-emergent adverse event reported was injection site reaction. Single doses of ALG-020572 demonstrated a favourable pharmacokinetic and safety profile in healthy participants. However, the study was prematurely discontinued after 4 participants experienced significant alanine aminotransferase elevations attributed to drug-induced liver injury.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that ALG-020572 was poorly tolerated in participants with chronic HBV infection, leading to early termination and highlighting the need for caution in developing this class of drugs.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results caution against the development of antisense oligonucleotides for HBV treatment due to the risk of drug-induced liver injury, which has implications for future drug design and safety assessments.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was prematurely discontinued due to safety concerns, which limits the ability to fully assess long-term efficacy and safety. The evidence is moderate due to the limited sample size and early termination.

Journal: Journal of medical virology  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-09-01  |  DOI: 10.1002/jmv.71130  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 98, 9, e71130
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

敏感乙型肝炎病毒 基因组 分析的下一代测序方法:一项欧洲研究。

Next-Generation Sequencing Methods for Sensitive Hepatitis B Viral Genome Analysis: A European Study.
作者： Michael X. Fu, Maria F. Perdomo, Sheila F Lumley, Johan Ringlander, Kai Kean, Kaitlin Reid, Richard Mayne, Oscar Enrique Torres Montaguth, Leysa Forrest, Sarah Buddle
摘要中文翻译这项多中心研究调查了下一代测序 (NGS) 在低病毒载量(0.2 至 6207 IU/mL)样本中检测和生成 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 基因组 的效用。通过 6 个欧洲实验室的 9 种既定 NGS 方法对 23 份基因型 A-E 的 HBV DNA 阳性血浆样本和一份 HBV 阴性对照样本进行了盲法检测。方法包括非靶向宏基因组学、通过探针捕获进行预富集,然后进行 Illumina 测序,以及 HBV 特异性 PCR 预扩增,然后使用 Nanopore 或 Illumina 进行测序。仅使用探针捕获方法从病毒载量 > 1000 IU/mL、使用 PCR-Illumina 方法 > 200 IU/mL、使用 PCR-Nanopore 方法从病毒载量 > 10 IU/mL 的样本中获得完整 HBV 基因组,并且没有使用宏基因组方法的样本。在基于 PCR 的方法中,在阴性对照和病毒载量极低的样品中观察到污染。探针捕获和宏基因组方法检测到在献血中未常规筛查的其他病毒,包括多瘤病毒和疱疹病毒;PCR结果证实呈阳性。总之,如果有 PCR 预扩增步骤的支持,NGS 可以在低病毒载量下描绘出完整的 基因组 序列。探针捕获方法无需预扩增即可可靠地检测 HBV,但对低病毒载量样本的 基因组 覆盖范围有限;它们还可以检测多种血源性病毒。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估下一代测序 (NGS) 在检测低病毒载量样本中的 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 基因组 方面的有效性,解决在病毒载量极低的临床环境中准确识别 HBV 的挑战。
设计与对象
一项在 6 个欧洲实验室进行的多中心研究使用各种 NGS 方法评估了 23 份基因型 A-E 的 HBV DNA 阳性血浆样本和一份 HBV 阴性对照样本。
核心方法
该研究采用了非靶向宏基因组学、探针捕获后进行 Illumina 测序,以及 HBV 特异性 PCR 预扩增,并结合使用 Nanopore 或 Illumina 技术进行测序。
主要结果
使用探针捕获方法从病毒载量 > 1000 IU/mL、使用 PCR-Illumina 方法 > 200 IU/mL 和使用 PCR-Nanopore 方法 > 10 IU/mL 的样本中成功获得完整 HBV 基因组。宏基因组方法未能在任何样本中检测到 HBV。
解释与创新
该研究表明,在 PCR 预扩增步骤的支持下,NGS 可以在低病毒载量下有效生成 HBV 的全 基因组 序列,强调了这种方法在提高检测准确性方面的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现对于提高低病毒载量情况下 HBV 基因组 分析的敏感性具有重要意义,这可以提高诊断准确性并为更好的 监测 和 治疗 策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究结果受限于PCR方法在阴性对照和低病毒载量样本中观察到的污染,表明此类样本的结果解读可能面临挑战。

Next-Generation Sequencing Methods for Sensitive Hepatitis B Viral Genome Analysis: A European Study.

Authors: Michael X. Fu, Maria F. Perdomo, Sheila F Lumley, Johan Ringlander, Kai Kean, Kaitlin Reid, Richard Mayne, Oscar Enrique Torres Montaguth, Leysa Forrest, Sarah Buddle
Original abstract (English)
This multicentre study investigated the utility of next-generation sequencing (NGS) to detect and generate hepatitis B virus (HBV) genomes in samples of low viral load (from 0.2 to 6207 IU/mL). 23 HBV DNA-positive plasma samples of genotypes A-E and one HBV-negative control sample were assayed blindly via 9 established NGS methods from 6 European laboratories. Methods included untargeted metagenomics, pre-enrichment by probe-capture followed by Illumina sequencing, and HBV-specific PCR pre-amplification followed by sequencing with Nanopore or Illumina. Full HBV genomes were obtained only from samples with viral loads > 1000 IU/mL using probe-capture methods, > 200 IU/mL using PCR-Illumina methods, > 10 IU/mL using PCR-Nanopore methods, and in no samples using metagenomic methods. Contamination was observed in the negative control and samples with very low viral loads in PCR-based methods. Probe-capture and metagenomic methods detected additional viruses not routinely screened in blood donations, including polyomaviruses and herpesviruses; positive results were confirmed by PCR. In conclusion, NGS may delineate whole-genome sequences at low viral loads if supported by a PCR pre-amplification step. Probe-capture methods also reliably detect HBV without pre-amplification but show limited genome coverage for samples with low viral loads; they may additionally detect a wide range of blood-borne viruses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the effectiveness of next-generation sequencing (NGS) in detecting hepatitis B virus (HBV) genomes in samples with low viral loads, addressing the challenge of accurately identifying HBV in clinical settings where viral load is minimal.
Design and population
A multicentre study conducted across 6 European laboratories assessed 23 HBV DNA-positive plasma samples of genotypes A-E and one HBV-negative control sample using various NGS methods.
Core methods
The study employed untargeted metagenomics, probe-capture followed by Illumina sequencing, and HBV-specific PCR pre-amplification combined with sequencing using either Nanopore or Illumina technologies.
Main results
Full HBV genomes were successfully obtained from samples with viral loads > 1000 IU/mL using probe-capture methods, > 200 IU/mL using PCR-Illumina methods, and > 10 IU/mL using PCR-Nanopore methods. Metagenomic methods failed to detect HBV in any samples.
Interpretation and novelty
The study demonstrated that NGS can effectively generate whole-genome sequences of HBV at low viral loads when supported by a PCR pre-amplification step, highlighting the importance of this approach in improving detection accuracy.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings have important implications for enhancing the sensitivity of HBV genome analysis in low viral load scenarios, which can improve diagnostic accuracy and inform better surveillance and treatment strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's results are limited by the fact that contamination was observed in negative controls and low viral load samples using PCR-based methods, suggesting potential challenges in interpreting results from such samples.

二氧化氯对乙型肝炎病毒的杀病毒活性评价

Evaluation of the Virucidal Activity of Chlorine Dioxide against Hepatitis B Virus
作者： Ming Chen, Caroline Donzeli Pereira, Koichi Watashi, Takashi Shibata, Takanori Miura
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 仍然是全球主要的公共卫生问题,有大量医院和职业 暴发 报告。
设计与对象
本研究评估了二氧化氯 (ClO2) 对 HBV 的杀病毒活性,并将其与次氯酸钠 (NaClO) 进行了比较。
核心方法
Pretreatment of HBV with ClO2 at 10, 100, and 250 ppm (mg/L) for 1 min was performed, and the effects on hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) release, hepatitis B core (HBc)-positive cells, intracellular HBV RNA, and extracellular HBV DNA were measured.
主要结果
Pretreatment of HBV with ClO2 at 10, 100, and 250 ppm (mg/L) for 1 min reduced hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) release to 65.5%, 21.9%, and 7.0%; hepatitis B core (HBc)-positive cells to 59.1%, 27.0%, and 6.6%; intracellular HBV RNA to 71.5%, 31.6%, and 3.0%; and extracellular HBV DNA to 82.2%, 42.6%, and 14.1%, respectively.
解释与创新
这些结果表明,在测试的浓度范围和条件下,ClO2 表现出比 NaClO 更强的体外抗 HBV 活性。
科研与公卫意义
在有或没有牛血清白蛋白和羊红细胞的情况下,ClO2 的抗 HBV 效力比 NaClO 强约 2.2 倍。
局限与证据强度
在存在有机物的情况下,没有观察到 ClO2 对抗 HBV 的活性显着降低。

Evaluation of the Virucidal Activity of Chlorine Dioxide against Hepatitis B Virus

Authors: Ming Chen, Caroline Donzeli Pereira, Koichi Watashi, Takashi Shibata, Takanori Miura
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) remains a major global public health concern, with numerous nosocomial and occupational outbreaks reported. Preventing indirect HBV transmission through effective disinfection is crucial. This study evaluated the virucidal activity of chlorine dioxide (ClO2) against HBV and compared it with sodium hypochlorite (NaClO). Pretreatment of HBV with ClO2 at 10, 100, and 250 ppm (mg/L) for 1 min reduced hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) release to 65.5%, 21.9%, and 7.0%; hepatitis B core (HBc)-positive cells to 59.1%, 27.0%, and 6.6%; intracellular HBV RNA to 71.5%, 31.6%, and 3.0%; and extracellular HBV DNA to 82.2%, 42.6%, and 14.1%, respectively. At identical concentrations, NaClO produced smaller reductions. In the presence of organic matter, no significant reduction in ClO2 activity against HBV was observed. ClO2 demonstrated approximately 2.2-fold greater anti-HBV potency than NaClO with and without bovine serum albumin and sheep erythrocytes. Neutralizer, cell-permissiveness, and ultrafiltration controls supported direct viral inactivation. These findings indicate that ClO2 showed stronger in vitro anti-HBV activity than NaClO within the tested concentration range and conditions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) remains a major global public health concern, with numerous nosocomial and occupational outbreaks reported.
Design and population
This study evaluated the virucidal activity of chlorine dioxide (ClO2) against HBV and compared it with sodium hypochlorite (NaClO).
Core methods

Main results
Pretreatment of HBV with ClO2 at 10, 100, and 250 ppm (mg/L) for 1 min reduced hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) release to 65.5%, 21.9%, and 7.0%; hepatitis B core (HBc)-positive cells to 59.1%, 27.0%, and 6.6%; intracellular HBV RNA to 71.5%, 31.6%, and 3.0%; and extracellular HBV DNA to 82.2%, 42.6%, and 14.1%, respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings indicate that ClO2 showed stronger in vitro anti-HBV activity than NaClO within the tested concentration range and conditions.
Scientific and public-health significance
ClO2 demonstrated approximately 2.2-fold greater anti-HBV potency than NaClO with and without bovine serum albumin and sheep erythrocytes.
Limitations and evidence strength
In the presence of organic matter, no significant reduction in ClO2 activity against HBV was observed.

HIV 治疗 网站的乙型和丙型肝炎预防、筛查和诊断服务:评估艾滋病的国际 流行病学 数据库。

Hepatitis B and C Prevention, Screening, and Diagnostic Services at HIV Treatment Sites: International Epidemiology Databases to Evaluate AIDS.
作者： Mark H. Kuniholm, Gad Murenzi, Niharika Samala, Evy Yunihastuti, Gilles Wandeler, H Nina Kim, Marie K Plaisy, Hugo Perazzo, Christella Twizere, Francesca Odhiambo
摘要中文翻译背景:乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 和丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 的预防、筛查和诊断服务可以预防接受 HIV 护理的人群发病和 死亡率。然而,有关全球艾滋病毒诊所提供的乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎服务的信息有限。方法:国际 流行病学 艾滋病评估数据库 (IeDEA) 对来自 7 个地区的参与 HIV 治疗 中心的服务提供和实践进行了调查。我们使用 2023 年调查数据来衡量 HBV 疫苗接种、HBV 和 HCV 筛查、HBV 表面抗原 (HBsAg)、HBV DNA、HCV 抗体和 HCV RNA 检测的可用性。使用多变量逻辑回归模型来测试站点特征与 HBV 和 HCV 服务的关联。结果:204 个 HIV 治疗 站点中 67.7% 的 HBV 疫苗接种 可现场获得。据报告,72.1% 和 50% 的机构在 HIV 护理登记时进行了 HBV 和 HCV 筛查。HBsAg、HBV DNA、HCV 抗体和 HCV RNA 现场检测率分别为 77%、47.6%、61.8% 和 44.6%。主要服务于农村(相对于城市)人口的站点不太可能报告 HBV DNA 的现场可用性 [比值比 (OR):0.07;95%置信区间:0.01至0.68;P = 0.02]、HCV 抗体(OR = 0.18;95% CI:0.04 至 0.92;P = 0.04)和 HCV RNA(OR = 0.10;95% CI:0.01 至 0.90;P = 0.04)检测。结论:参与 IeDEA 网络的大多数 HIV 治疗 站点均可现场提供 HBV 疫苗接种、HBsAg 和 HCV 抗体检测等挽救生命的服务。农村地区的可用性较低表明,扩大服务范围对于消除乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎作为接受艾滋病毒护理的人群的公共卫生问题非常重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 和丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 的预防、筛查和诊断服务可以预防接受 HIV 护理的人群发病和 死亡率。然而,有关全球艾滋病毒诊所提供的乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎服务的信息有限。

核心方法
使用多变量逻辑回归模型来测试站点特征与 HBV 和 HCV 服务的关联。
主要结果
204 个 HIV 治疗 站点中的 67.7% 可以现场获得 HBV 疫苗接种。据报告,72.1% 和 50% 的机构在 HIV 护理登记时进行了 HBV 和 HCV 筛查。HBsAg、HBV DNA、HCV 抗体和 HCV RNA 现场检测率分别为 77%、47.6%、61.8% 和 44.6%。

局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计的局限性或证据的强度。

Hepatitis B and C Prevention, Screening, and Diagnostic Services at HIV Treatment Sites: International Epidemiology Databases to Evaluate AIDS.

Authors: Mark H. Kuniholm, Gad Murenzi, Niharika Samala, Evy Yunihastuti, Gilles Wandeler, H Nina Kim, Marie K Plaisy, Hugo Perazzo, Christella Twizere, Francesca Odhiambo
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Prevention, screening, and diagnostic services for hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis C virus (HCV) can prevent morbidity and mortality in people receiving HIV care. However, there is limited information about the availability of HBV and HCV services at HIV clinics globally. METHODS: The International epidemiology Databases to Evaluate AIDS (IeDEA) conducted surveys of service delivery and practices at participating HIV treatment centers from 7 regions. We used 2023 survey data to measure availability of HBV vaccination, HBV and HCV screening, HBV surface antigen (HBsAg), HBV DNA, HCV antibody, and HCV RNA testing. Multivariable logistic regression models were used to test associations of site characteristics with HBV and HCV services. RESULTS: HBV vaccination was available on-site at 67.7% of 204 HIV treatment sites. Screening for HBV and HCV at HIV care enrollment was reported by 72.1% and 50% of sites, respectively. HBsAg, HBV DNA, HCV antibody, and HCV RNA testing were available on-site at 77%, 47.6%, 61.8%, and 44.6% of sites, respectively. Sites serving predominately rural (vs. urban) populations were less likely to report on-site availability of HBV DNA [odds ratio (OR):0.07; 95% confidence interval: 0.01 to 0.68; P = 0.02], HCV antibody (OR = 0.18; 95% CI: 0.04 to 0.92; P = 0.04), and HCV RNA (OR = 0.10; 95% CI: 0.01 to 0.90; P = 0.04) testing. CONCLUSION: Life-saving services such as HBV vaccination, HBsAg, and HCV antibody testing were available on-site at most HIV treatment sites participating in the IeDEA network. Lower availability at rural sites suggests that expansion of services is important to eliminate HBV and HCV as public health problems in people receiving HIV care.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Prevention, screening, and diagnostic services for hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis C virus (HCV) can prevent morbidity and mortality in people receiving HIV care. However, there is limited information about the availability of HBV and HCV services at HIV clinics globally.
Design and population
The International epidemiology Databases to Evaluate AIDS (IeDEA) conducted surveys of service delivery and practices at participating HIV treatment centers from 7 regions. We used 2023 survey data to measure availability of HBV vaccination, HBV and HCV screening, HBV surface antigen (HBsAg), HBV DNA, HCV antibody, and HCV RNA testing.
Core methods
Multivariable logistic regression models were used to test associations of site characteristics with HBV and HCV services.
Main results
HBV vaccination was available on-site at 67.7% of 204 HIV treatment sites. Screening for HBV and HCV at HIV care enrollment was reported by 72.1% and 50% of sites, respectively. HBsAg, HBV DNA, HCV antibody, and HCV RNA testing were available on-site at 77%, 47.6%, 61.8%, and 44.6% of sites, respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
Life-saving services such as HBV vaccination, HBsAg, and HCV antibody testing were available on-site at most HIV treatment sites participating in the IeDEA network.
Scientific and public-health significance
Lower availability at rural sites suggests that expansion of services is important to eliminate HBV and HCV as public health problems in people receiving HIV care.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report on the limitations of the study design or the strength of the evidence.

慢性乙型肝炎 患者发生肝脏脂肪变性的代谢危险因素:罗马尼亚国家 队列研究

Metabolic Risk Factors of Developing Liver Steatosis among Patients with Chronic Hepatitis B: A National Cohort from Romania
作者： Anca Trifan, Robert Nastasa, Carol Stanciu, Ermina Stratina, Tudor Cuciureanu, Cristina Maria Muzica, Sebastian Zenovia, Remus Stafie, Adrian Rotaru, Ana-Maria Singeap
摘要中文翻译背景与目的:随着全球肥胖和2型糖尿病(T2DM)流行病的发展,慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)患者中肝脂肪变性的共存现象日益受到关注。本研究旨在识别罗马尼亚CHB患者中脂肪性肝病(SLD)相关的代谢风险因素。方法:本项前瞻性研究纳入罗马尼亚LIVE(RO)2全国筛查项目(2021-2023年)的320,000名受试者。大多数患者接受了控制性衰减参数(CAP)瞬时弹性成像检查以诊断肝脂肪变性,且属于脆弱人群。排除标准包括重度饮酒、任何与CHB无关的其他肝病以及缺乏CAP评估。结果:筛查项目中发现5,321名个体(1.67%)患有CHB。最终分析纳入1,970名患者,平均年龄为55.87±13.84岁;其中57.9%为男性,平均体质指数(BMI)水平为27.26 kg/m2,61.9%的患者属于脆弱人群。SLD的患病率在脆弱个体中为42.2%,而在非脆弱参与者中为26.4%。此外,脆弱条件患者的T2DM(11.5%)、高血压(41.3%)和BMI≥25 kg/m2(71.3%)患病率更高。本研究中,脆弱受试者HBeAg阳性率为3%,并被发现是肝脂肪变性发展的保护性风险因素(校正优势比[aOR]=0.397)。这些患者重度肝纤维化(≥F3)的患病率也更高,为17.1%,而非脆弱者为9.7%。此外,HBeAg阳性的存在可使重度肝纤维化进展风险提高6倍。结论:本研究发现,在未经治疗的CHB脆弱患者中,肝脂肪变性的患病率为42.2%,重度纤维化的患病率为17.1%。HBeAg阳性被发现是肝脂肪变性的保护性风险因素,但会加速重度纤维化的发展,且T2DM在CHB脆弱人群中更为常见。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
随着全球肥胖和 2 型糖尿病 (T2DM) 的流行,慢性乙型病毒性肝炎 (CHB) 患者肝脏脂肪变性的共存变得越来越重要。本研究的目的是确定罗马尼亚慢性乙型肝炎患者中与脂肪变性肝病 (SLD) 相关的代谢危险因素。
设计与对象
本项前瞻性研究纳入罗马尼亚LIVE(RO)2全国筛查项目(2021-2023年)的320,000名受试者。最终分析纳入1,970名患者,平均年龄为55.87±13.84岁;其中57.9%为男性,平均体质指数(BMI)水平为27.26 kg/m2,61.9%的患者属于脆弱人群。
核心方法
大多数患者接受了使用受控衰减参数(CAP)的瞬时弹性成像来诊断肝脏脂肪变性,他们属于易受影响的类别。HBeAg 阳性被发现是肝脂肪变性的一个保护性危险因素,但会加速严重纤维化以及 T2DM 的发展,这在易感 CHB 队列研究 中更为常见。
主要结果
筛查计划中发现 5,321 人 (1.67%) 患有慢性乙型肝炎。弱势群体中的 SLD 的 患病率 为 42.2%,而非弱势参与者的 SLD 的 患病率 为 26.4%。此外,病情脆弱的患者患有T2DM(11.5%)、高血压(41.3%)和BMI水平≥25 kg/m2(71.3%)的患病率较高。在我们的研究中,易感受试者的乙型肝炎病毒 e抗原(HBeAg)阳性率较高,为3%,并且发现它是肝脂肪变性发展的保护性危险因素(aOR=0.397)。此外,与非易感患者相比,这些患者的严重肝纤维化(≥ F3)患病率 较高,为 17.1%,患病率 为 9.7%。
解释与创新
在我们的研究中,我们发现在易受治疗的慢性乙型肝炎患者的 队列研究 中,肝脂肪变性的 患病率 为 42.2%,而严重纤维化的 患病率 为 17.1%。
科研与公卫意义
该研究结果可为弱势慢性乙型肝炎患者代谢并发症的针对性 监测 和风险评估策略提供信息。他们还强调了监测这些人群的 T2DM 和高血压对于降低肝纤维化进展风险的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其依赖瞬时弹性成像来诊断肝脏脂肪变性,并且排除了重度饮酒或其他肝脏疾病的个体。由于研究的观察性质和潜在的混杂因素,证据的强度中等。

Metabolic Risk Factors of Developing Liver Steatosis among Patients with Chronic Hepatitis B: A National Cohort from Romania

Authors: Anca Trifan, Robert Nastasa, Carol Stanciu, Ermina Stratina, Tudor Cuciureanu, Cristina Maria Muzica, Sebastian Zenovia, Remus Stafie, Adrian Rotaru, Ana-Maria Singeap
Original abstract (English)
Background and Aims: The coexistence of liver steatosis in patients with chronic viral hepatitis B (CHB) is growing relevant as the global epidemic of obesity and type 2 diabetes mellitus (T2DM). The aim of this study was to identify the metabolic risk factors linked to steatotic liver disease (SLD) in Romanian patients with CHB. Methods: In this prospective study, we evaluated 320,000 individuals in the LIVE(RO)2 nationwide screening program in Romania from 2021 to 2023. The majority of the patients had undergone transient elastography using controlled attenuation parameter (CAP) to diagnose liver steatosis, and they belonged to vulnerable categories. Exclusion criteria included heavy alcohol consumption, any other liver disease unrelated to CHB, and the lack of CAP evaluation. Results: 5,321 individuals (1.67%) were found with CHB in the screening program. In the final analysis we included 1,970 patients, with a mean age of 55.87±13.84 years; 57.9% of them were males, with a mean body mass index (BMI) level of 27.26 kg/m2, and 61.9% of the patients were vulnerable. The prevalence of SLD was 42.2% in the group of vulnerable individuals, compared with non-vulnerable participants, who had a prevalence of SLD of 26.4%. Moreover, patients with vulnerable conditions have a higher prevalence of T2DM (11.5%), hypertension (41.3%), and raised BMI levels ≥25 kg/m2 (71.3%). In our study, vulnerable subjects had a higher prevalence of hepatitis B virus e antigen (HBeAg) positivity of 3%, and it was found to be a protective risk factor for hepatic steatosis development (aOR=0.397). Also, these patients had a higher prevalence of severe liver fibrosis (≥ F3) of 17.1%, compared with those non-vulnerable, with a prevalence of 9.7% of severe liver fibrosis. More than that, we find that the presence of HBeAg-positive increases the risk of severe liver fibrosis progression 6 times higher. Conclusions: In our study we found a prevalence of hepatic steatosis of 42.2% in the cohort of vulnerable untreated patients with CHB, and the prevalence of severe fibrosis was 17.1%. Presence of HBeAg-positive was found to be a protective risk factor for hepatic steatosis but accelerates the development of severe fibrosis along with T2DM, which is more common in the vulnerable CHB cohort.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The coexistence of liver steatosis in patients with chronic viral hepatitis B (CHB) is growing relevant as the global epidemic of obesity and type 2 diabetes mellitus (T2DM). The aim of this study was to identify the metabolic risk factors linked to steatotic liver disease (SLD) in Romanian patients with CHB.
Design and population
In this prospective study, we evaluated 320,000 individuals in the LIVE(RO)2 nationwide screening program in Romania from 2021 to 2023. The final analysis included 1,970 patients, with a mean age of 55.87±13.84 years; 57.9% of them were males, with a mean body mass index (BMI) level of 27.26 kg/m2, and 61.9% of the patients were vulnerable.
Core methods
The majority of the patients had undergone transient elastography using controlled attenuation parameter (CAP) to diagnose liver steatosis, and they belonged to vulnerable categories. Presence of HBeAg-positive was found to be a protective risk factor for hepatic steatosis but accelerates the development of severe fibrosis along with T2DM, which is more common in the vulnerable CHB cohort.
Main results
5,321 individuals (1.67%) were found with CHB in the screening program. The prevalence of SLD was 42.2% in the group of vulnerable individuals, compared with non-vulnerable participants, who had a prevalence of SLD of 26.4%. Moreover, patients with vulnerable conditions have a higher prevalence of T2DM (11.5%), hypertension (41.3%), and raised BMI levels ≥25 kg/m2 (71.3%). In our study, vulnerable subjects had a higher prevalence of hepatitis B virus e antigen (HBeAg) positivity of 3%, and it was found to be a protective risk factor for hepatic steatosis development (aOR=0.397). Also, these patients had a higher prevalence of severe liver fibrosis (≥ F3) of 17.1%, compared with those non-vulnerable, with a prevalence of 9.7% of severe liver fibrosis.
Interpretation and novelty
In our study we found a prevalence of hepatic steatosis of 42.2% in the cohort of vulnerable untreated patients with CHB, and the prevalence of severe fibrosis was 17.1%.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted surveillance and risk assessment strategies for metabolic complications in vulnerable patients with CHB. They also highlight the importance of monitoring T2DM and hypertension in these populations to mitigate the risk of liver fibrosis progression.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on transient elastography for diagnosing liver steatosis and the exclusion of individuals with heavy alcohol consumption or other liver diseases. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational nature of the study and potential confounding factors.

使用亚洲 慢性乙型肝炎 患者的预测评分确定基于风险的肝细胞癌 监测 阈值:一项全国性 队列研究 研究

Risk-based hepatocellular carcinoma surveillance thresholds using a prediction score in Asian patients with chronic hepatitis B: a nationwide cohort study
作者： Won Sohn, Myeongcheol Lee, Danbee Kang, Ju-Yeon Cho, Jung Hee Kim, Soo Jin Park, Yong-Han Paik
摘要中文翻译背景/目的:本研究旨在根据经过验证的预测评分量化肝细胞癌 (HCC) 发病率,并定义接受 抗病毒药物 治疗的亚洲 监测 (CHB) 患者中与 监测 相关 发病率 阈值相对应的基于评分的截止值。方法:我们利用韩国国民健康保险服务数据库进行了一项全国范围内的 队列研究 研究。纳入 2012 年至 2023 年间开始核苷(酸)类似物治疗的 CHB 成人,排除病毒合并感染、既往恶性肿瘤或失代偿性肝硬化。使用 HCC-RESCUE 评分对 HCC 风险进行分层。HCC 事件是主要结局。使用多变量 Cox 比例风险模型估算年度 HCC 发病率 率和调整后的风险比。结果:在 199,143 名接受治疗的患者中(中位随访时间为 7.3 年),15,233 名患者发展为 HCC。调整后的年度 HCC 发病率 在不同风险层中逐渐增加:低风险组为 0.31% (95% CI 0.24-0.40),中风险组为 1.09% (0.84-1.41),高风险组为 1.85% (1.44-2.40)。与低风险组相比,中风险组和高风险组的 HCC 调整后风险比分别为 3.48 (95% CI 3.35-3.61) 和 5.90 (5.62-6.19)(均 p<0.001)。对应于年度 HCC 发病率 阈值 0.2%、1.5% 和 3.0% 的 HCC-RESCUE 评分分别为 47、66 和 90。结论:在大量接受 发病率 治疗的亚洲 CHB 人群中,基于预测评分的分层揭示了残余 HCC 风险的分级谱,并确定了与监测。这些发现支持 HCC 监测 策略的 发病率 知情细化,补充了 抗病毒药物 治疗 时代传统的基于肝硬化的标准。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 仍然是肝细胞癌 (HCC) 的主要原因,但 监测 策略通常基于肝硬化而不是个体化风险。该研究解决了通过经过验证的预测评分量化 HCC 发病率 的需要,并确定治疗的亚洲 CHB 患者中与 监测 相关的截止值。
设计与对象
使用韩国国民健康保险服务数据库进行了一项基于全国人口的 队列研究 研究。2012 年至 2023 年间开始接受核苷(酸)类似物治疗的慢性乙型肝炎成人患者被包括在内,而那些患有病毒合并感染、既往恶性肿瘤或失代偿性肝硬化的患者则被排除在外。
核心方法
使用 HCC-RESCUE 评分对 HCC 风险进行分层,HCC 事件是主要结局。使用多变量 Cox 比例风险模型估算年度 HCC 发病率 率和调整后的风险比。
主要结果
在 199,143 名接受治疗的患者中(中位随访时间为 7.3 年),15,233 名患者发展为 HCC。调整后的年度 HCC 发病率 在不同风险层中逐渐增加:低风险组为 0.31% (95% CI 0.24–0.40),中风险组为 1.09% (0.84–1.41),高风险组为 1.85% (1.44–2.40)。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证明了 抗病毒药物 治疗的 CHB 患者中残留 HCC 风险的分级谱,并确定了与 发病率 相关的 发病率 对齐阈值。新颖之处在于将 HCC-RESCUE 分数阈值与 发病率 指导的特定年度 发病率 截止值联系起来。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持 HCC 监测 策略的 发病率 知情细化,补充了 抗病毒药物 治疗 时代传统的基于肝硬化的标准,有可能改善早期检测和资源分配。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括它依赖于单一的国家数据库,并且排除了病毒合并感染或既往恶性肿瘤的患者,这可能会限制普遍性。由于 队列研究 较大、统计方法稳健且风险分层清晰,该证据被评为高等级。

Risk-based hepatocellular carcinoma surveillance thresholds using a prediction score in Asian patients with chronic hepatitis B: a nationwide cohort study

Authors: Won Sohn, Myeongcheol Lee, Danbee Kang, Ju-Yeon Cho, Jung Hee Kim, Soo Jin Park, Yong-Han Paik
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUNDS/AIMS: This study aimed to quantify hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) incidence according to a validated prediction score and to define score-based cut-offs corresponding to surveillance-relevant incidence thresholds in Asian patients with chronic hepatitis B (CHB) receiving antiviral therapy. METHODS: We conducted a nationwide population-based cohort study using the Korean National Health Insurance Service database. Adults with CHB initiating nucleos(t)ide analogue therapy between 2012 and 2023 were included, hose with viral coinfections, prior malignancy, or decompensated cirrhosis were excluded. HCC risk was stratified using the HCC-RESCUE score. Incident HCC was the primary outcome. Annual HCC incidence rates and adjusted hazard ratios were estimated using multivariable Cox proportional hazards models. RESULTS: Among 199,143 treated patients (median follow-up, 7.3 years), 15,233 developed HCC. Adjusted annual HCC incidence increased progressively across risk strata: 0.31% (95% CI 0.24-0.40) in the low-risk group, 1.09% (0.84-1.41) in the intermediate-risk group, and 1.85% (1.44-2.40) in the high-risk group. Compared with the low-risk group, adjusted hazard ratios for HCC were 3.48 (95% CI 3.35-3.61) and 5.90 (5.62-6.19) in the intermediate- and high-risk groups, respectively (all p<0.001). HCC-RESCUE scores corresponding to annual HCC incidence thresholds of 0.2%, 1.5%, and 3.0% were 47, 66, and 90. CONCLUSIONS: In a large antiviral-treated Asian CHB population, prediction score-based stratification revealed a graded spectrum of residual HCC risk and identified incidence-aligned thresholds relevant to surveillance. These findings support incidence-informed refinement of HCC surveillance strategies that complement traditional cirrhosis-based criteria in the antiviral treatment era.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) remains a leading cause of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC), yet surveillance strategies are often based on cirrhosis rather than individualized risk. The study addressed the need to quantify HCC incidence by validated prediction scores and to define surveillance-relevant cut-offs in treated Asian patients with CHB.
Design and population
A nationwide population-based cohort study was conducted using the Korean National Health Insurance Service database. Adults with CHB initiating nucleos(t)ide analogue therapy between 2012 and 2023 were included, while those with viral coinfections, prior malignancy, or decompensated cirrhosis were excluded.
Core methods
HCC risk was stratified using the HCC-RESCUE score, and incident HCC was the primary outcome. Annual HCC incidence rates and adjusted hazard ratios were estimated using multivariable Cox proportional hazards models.
Main results
Among 199,143 treated patients (median follow-up, 7.3 years), 15,233 developed HCC. Adjusted annual HCC incidence increased progressively across risk strata: 0.31% (95% CI 0.24–0.40) in the low-risk group, 1.09% (0.84–1.41) in the intermediate-risk group, and 1.85% (1.44–2.40) in the high-risk group.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as demonstrating a graded spectrum of residual HCC risk in antiviral-treated CHB patients and identified incidence-aligned thresholds relevant to surveillance. The novelty lies in linking HCC-RESCUE score thresholds to specific annual incidence cut-offs for surveillance guidance.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support incidence-informed refinement of HCC surveillance strategies that complement traditional cirrhosis-based criteria in the antiviral treatment era, potentially improving early detection and resource allocation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s limitations include its reliance on a single national database and the exclusion of patients with viral coinfections or prior malignancy, which may limit generalisability. The evidence is rated as high due to the large cohort, robust statistical methods, and clear risk stratification.

治疗 初治、HBeAg 阳性慢性 HBV 感染儿童 HBV 整合的基因组和转录组特征

Genomic and transcriptomic features of HBV integration in treatment-naïve, HBeAg-positive children with chronic HBV infection
作者： Na Wu, Yan Han, Jing Tang, Zhiwei Chen, Jiaying Wu, Yingzhi Zhou, Yunan Chang, Xiaorong Peng, Aoxue Tan, Yi Xiang
摘要中文翻译背景:乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 整合是治愈 HBV 的主要障碍;然而,慢性 HBV 感染儿童中 HBV 整合和局部 免疫应答 与转录活性病毒整合的情况仍不清楚。在此,我们的目的是阐明这一人群的这种情况。方法:使用基于探针的捕获策略对 18 名慢性 HBV 感染儿童和 28 名成人进行基因组分析。对来自我们 队列研究 的 12 名儿童和来自公共数据库的 3 名成人进行了空间转录组学 (ST)。结果:所有患者均为乙型肝炎 e 抗原 (HBeAg) 阳性且未接受过 治疗 治疗。从基因组角度来看,尽管独特断点计数相当,但儿童的 HBV 整合肝细胞克隆扩增水平显着低于成人。调整混杂变量后,年龄被确定为独特积分断点总频率的独立风险因素(b = 3.22,P = 0.005)。在空间上,ST 显示具有转录活性病毒整合的斑点分布稀疏,并且在儿童所有斑点中所占比例较低。值得注意的是,在这些点,儿童表现出适应性免疫细胞(例如 CD8+ T 细胞)减少,但先天成分(骨髓细胞、库普弗细胞、活化树突细胞)和 APC 共刺激增加,而成人表现出免疫细胞群的一致减少。解释:与成人相比,儿童表现出较低的 HBV 整合肝细胞克隆扩增和响应转录活性病毒整合的独特免疫特征,为了解其不同的临床病程提供了新的见解。资金来源:传染病分子生物学教育部重点实验室。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
儿童中的 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (HBV) 感染对治疗提出了独特的挑战,但该人群中 HBV 整合和局部 免疫应答 与转录活性病毒整合的情况仍知之甚少。
设计与对象
该研究使用基于探针的捕获策略对 18 名患有慢性 HBV 感染的儿童和 28 名成人进行基因组分析,并对来自 队列研究 的 12 名儿童和来自公共数据库的 3 名成人进行空间转录组学。
核心方法
使用基于探针的捕获策略进行基因组分析,并进行空间转录组学以评估转录活性和免疫细胞概况。
主要结果
尽管独特的断点数量相当,但儿童的 HBV 整合肝细胞克隆扩增明显低于成人,而且转录活性病毒整合点分布稀疏,占儿童所有点的比例较低。
解释与创新
儿童表现出适应性免疫细胞减少,但转录活性整合位点的先天成分和 APC 共刺激增加,与表现出免疫细胞群均匀减少的成年人形成鲜明对比,这为 HBV 整合和 免疫应答 中与年龄相关的差异提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了乙型肝炎病毒整合和免疫微环境的年龄依赖性差异,这可能为监测和治疗儿童慢性乙型肝炎病毒感染的策略提供参考。
局限与证据强度
年龄被确定为独特积分断点总频率的独立风险因素,表明超出研究的 队列研究 的潜在混淆和限制普遍性。

Genomic and transcriptomic features of HBV integration in treatment-naïve, HBeAg-positive children with chronic HBV infection

Authors: Na Wu, Yan Han, Jing Tang, Zhiwei Chen, Jiaying Wu, Yingzhi Zhou, Yunan Chang, Xiaorong Peng, Aoxue Tan, Yi Xiang
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Hepatitis B virus (HBV) integration represents a major obstacle to curing HBV; however, the landscape of HBV integration and local immune response to transcriptionally active viral integration in children with chronic HBV infection remain unclear. Herein, we aimed to elucidate this landscape in this population. METHODS: Genomic analyses using a probe-based capture strategy were performed on 18 children and 28 adults with chronic HBV infection. Spatial transcriptomics (ST) was performed on 12 children from our cohort and 3 adults from a public database. FINDINGS: All patients were hepatitis B e antigen (HBeAg)-positive and treatment-naïve. Genomically, children exhibited significantly lower clonal expansion level of HBV-integrated hepatocytes than adults, despite comparable unique breakpoint counts. After adjusting for confounding variables, age was identified as an independent risk factor for total frequency of unique integration breakpoints (b = 3.22, P = 0.005). Spatially, ST revealed that spots with transcriptionally active viral integration exhibited a sparse distribution and accounted for a low proportion of all spots in children. Notably, at these spots, children showed reduced adaptive immune cells (e.g., CD8+ T cells) but increased innate components (myeloid cells, Kupffer cells, activated dendritic cells) and APC co-stimulation, whereas adults exhibited a uniform reduction of immune cell populations. INTERPRETATION: Compared with adults, children exhibit lower clonal expansion of HBV-integrated hepatocytes and distinct immune profiles in response to transcriptionally active viral integration, offering new insights into their differing clinical course. FUNDING: Key Laboratory of Molecular Biology for Infectious Diseases (Ministry of Education).
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection in children poses unique challenges for cure, yet the landscape of HBV integration and local immune responses to transcriptionally active viral integration in this population remains poorly understood.
Design and population
The study performed genomic analyses using a probe-based capture strategy on 18 children and 28 adults with chronic HBV infection, and spatial transcriptomics on 12 children from the cohort and 3 adults from a public database.
Core methods
Genomic analyses were conducted using a probe-based capture strategy, and spatial transcriptomics was performed to assess transcriptional activity and immune cell profiles.
Main results
Children exhibited significantly lower clonal expansion of HBV-integrated hepatocytes than adults despite comparable unique breakpoint counts, and transcriptionally active viral integration spots were sparsely distributed and accounted for a low proportion of all spots in children.
Interpretation and novelty
Children showed reduced adaptive immune cells but increased innate components and APC co-stimulation at transcriptionally active integration spots, contrasting with adults who exhibited uniform reduction of immune cell populations, offering new insights into age-related differences in HBV integration and immune response.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight age-dependent differences in HBV integration and immune microenvironment, which may inform strategies for monitoring and treating chronic HBV infection in children.
Limitations and evidence strength
Age was identified as an independent risk factor for total frequency of unique integration breakpoints, indicating potential confounding and limiting generalisability beyond the studied cohort.

Journal: Journal of Virology  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-09-01  |  DOI: 10.1128/jvi.01063-26  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, e0106326
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 包膜蛋白中的自然免疫逃逸 突变 调节人丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 的组装和感染性

Natural immune escape mutations in the envelope proteins of hepatitis B virus (HBV) regulate the assembly and infectivity of human hepatitis delta virus (HDV)
作者： Oleksandra Chazova, Igor Zaiets, Severin O. Gudima
摘要中文翻译摘要:该研究探讨了位于包膜蛋白主要抗原环(MAL)中的 乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)的 35 个自然免疫逃逸 突变(IEM)如何调节 HBV 天然亚病毒因子——丁型肝炎病毒(HDV)的组装和感染性。所有 IEM 都允许 HDV 病毒颗粒的组装和分泌。在一到三种测试的 HBV 基因型(B、C 或 D)的背景下,只有 7 个 IEM 导致分泌的 HDV 病毒体水平显着降低。IEM 对 HDV 感染性的影响比对装配的影响要显着得多。13 个 IEM 显着抑制了 HDV 病毒粒子的感染性。它们都大大降低了感染细胞中积累的 HDV RNA 基因组 水平和 HDV 感染细胞的百分比。只有一种突变体 R(169)P 产生完全非传染性的 HDV 病毒体。因此,我们对 IEM 介导的 HDV 生命周期影响的分析确定了 MAL 中的许多至关重要的氨基酸残基,并有助于理解 HDV 组装和感染过程中 MAL 功能的机制。HDV组装和/或感染性的抑制有望抑制体内病毒传播和重复感染,从而减少受感染肝脏中的HDV 储存宿主。总体而言,生成的数据表明,抗 HBV 免疫应答 能够通过促进 MAL 中 IEM 的选择,从而显着下调伴随的 HDV 感染。该研究进一步加深了我们对 HBV-HDV 和 HDV-宿主 复杂相互作用机制的理解。重要性 该研究表明,通过选择 HBV 包膜蛋白(表面抗原,HBsAg)中的免疫逃逸 免疫应答 (IEM),抗 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 免疫应答 可以显着减少 HBsAg 包被的丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 病毒体的组装和/或感染性。IEM 可以主要通过影响 HDV 感染性来下调伴随的 HDV 感染,35 个 IEM 中有 13 个显着抑制了 HDV 感染性。HDV 传播/重复感染预计可通过 HBV 特异性 IEM 抑制 HDV 病毒粒子的组装和/或感染性来抑制,从而导致受感染肝脏中 HDV 储存宿主 减少。IEM 对 HDV 感染性抑制的检查显然表明 HBsAg 的主要抗原环 (MAL) 可能含有主要参与病毒附着/进入的残基,以及主要调节 HDV 病毒粒子进入细胞内后运输的残基。这些数据加深了我们对感染期间 MAL 功能以及通过抗 HBV 免疫应答 间接调节 HDV 生命周期的理解。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)包膜蛋白中的自然免疫逃逸突变(IEM)如何影响丁型肝炎病毒(HDV)的组装和感染性。这一背景强调了了解这些相互作用对于更好地理解免疫逃避机制及其对 HDV 的影响的重要性。
设计与对象
该研究分析了位于包膜蛋白主要抗原环(MAL)中的 35 个 HBV 自然免疫逃逸 突变(IEM)。这些 突变 在一到三种 HBV 基因型(B、C 或 D)的背景下进行了测试,以评估它们对 HDV 组装和感染性的影响。
核心方法
研究人员通过评估感染细胞中分泌的 HDV 病毒粒子水平和 HDV RNA 基因组 积累,研究了 IEM 对 HDV 病毒粒子组装和感染性的影响。他们还评估了在这些 突变 存在下 HDV 感染细胞的百分比和 HDV 病毒颗粒的感染性。
主要结果
只有 7 个 IEM 导致分泌型 HDV 病毒粒子的水平显着降低。13 个 IEM 显着抑制了 HDV 的感染性,大大降低了感染细胞中积累的 HDV RNA 基因组 和 HDV 感染细胞的百分比。一种突变体 R(169)P 产生完全非传染性的 HDV 病毒体。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,HBV 中的 IEM 可以显着下调 HDV 感染性,这比它们对 HDV 组装的影响更为明显。这为了解 MAL 在 HDV 生命周期调节中的作用以及抗 HBV 免疫应答 影响 HDV 感染的潜力提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果有助于对 HBV-HDV 相互作用的机制理解,并可能为通过 HBV 特异性 免疫应答 靶向 HDV 的策略提供信息,可能有助于开发减少受感染肝脏中 HDV 免疫应答 的疗法或 疫苗。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据基于对 HBV 基因型及其对 HDV 影响的体外分析,这可能会限制其在体内条件下的普遍适用性。由于缺乏直接的临床或流行病学数据,证据强度中等。

Natural immune escape mutations in the envelope proteins of hepatitis B virus (HBV) regulate the assembly and infectivity of human hepatitis delta virus (HDV)

Authors: Oleksandra Chazova, Igor Zaiets, Severin O. Gudima
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT The study examined how 35 natural immune escape mutations (IEMs) of hepatitis B virus (HBV) located in the major antigenic loop (MAL) of the envelope proteins regulate the assembly and infectivity of the natural sub-viral agent of HBV, hepatitis delta virus (HDV). All IEMs were permissive for the assembly and secretion of HDV virions. Only seven IEMs resulted in considerably decreased levels of secreted HDV virions in the context of one to three tested HBV genotypes, B, C, or D. The effects of IEMs on HDV infectivity were much more pronounced than on the assembly. Thirteen IEMs considerably inhibited the infectivity of HDV virions. All of them greatly reduced both the levels of HDV RNA genomes accumulated in the infected cells and the percentage of HDV-infected cells. Only one mutant R(169)P generated completely non-infectious HDV virions. Our analysis of the IEMs-mediated effects on the HDV life cycle therefore identified a number of critically important amino acid residues in the MAL, and aided the mechanistic understanding of the MAL functioning during the processes of HDV assembly and infectivity. The inhibition of either HDV assembly and/or infectivity is expected to suppress the viral spread and super-infection in vivo , and therefore to reduce the HDV reservoir in infected livers. Overall, the generated data showed that the anti-HBV immune response is capable of considerable down-regulation of the concomitant HDV infection by facilitating the selection for IEMs in the MAL. The study further advanced our understanding of the mechanisms of the complex HBV-HDV and HDV-host interactions. IMPORTANCE The study demonstrated that anti-hepatitis B virus (HBV) immune response via selection for the immune escape mutations (IEMs) in HBV envelope proteins (surface antigen, HBsAg) can considerably reduce the assembly and/or infectivity of hepatitis delta virus (HDV) virions coated with HBsAg. The IEMs could down-regulate concomitant HDV infection mostly via affecting HDV infectivity, which was considerably inhibited by 13 out of 35 IEMs. The HDV spread/super-infection, which are expected to be suppressed by inhibited via HBV-specific IEMs the assembly and/or infectivity of HDV virions, should result in a reduced HDV reservoir in infected livers. The examination of the inhibition of HDV infectivity by IEMs apparently indicated that the major antigenic loop (MAL) of HBsAg could contain the residues primarily involved in viral attachment/entry and also the residues mostly regulating intracellular post-entry trafficking of HDV virions. The data advance our understanding of the MAL functioning during infection and of the indirect regulation of the HDV life cycle through the anti-HBV immune response.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate how natural immune escape mutations (IEMs) in the envelope proteins of hepatitis B virus (HBV) affect the assembly and infectivity of hepatitis delta virus (HDV). The background highlights the importance of understanding these interactions to better comprehend the mechanisms of immune evasion and their impact on HDV.
Design and population
The study analyzed 35 natural immune escape mutations (IEMs) of HBV located in the major antigenic loop (MAL) of the envelope proteins. These mutations were tested in the context of one to three HBV genotypes, B, C, or D, to assess their effects on HDV assembly and infectivity.
Core methods
The researchers examined the effects of IEMs on HDV virion assembly and infectivity by assessing secreted HDV virion levels and HDV RNA genome accumulation in infected cells. They also evaluated the percentage of HDV-infected cells and the infectivity of HDV virions in the presence of these mutations.
Main results
Only seven IEMs resulted in considerably decreased levels of secreted HDV virions. Thirteen IEMs considerably inhibited HDV infectivity, greatly reducing both HDV RNA genomes accumulated in infected cells and the percentage of HDV-infected cells. One mutant, R(169)P, generated completely non-infectious HDV virions.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that IEMs in HBV can significantly down-regulate HDV infectivity, which is more pronounced than their effect on HDV assembly. This provides novel insights into the role of the MAL in HDV life cycle regulation and the potential of anti-HBV immune responses to influence HDV infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results contribute to the mechanistic understanding of HBV-HDV interactions and may inform strategies for targeting HDV through HBV-specific immune responses, potentially aiding in the development of therapies or vaccines that reduce HDV reservoirs in infected livers.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence is based on in vitro analysis of HBV genotypes and their effects on HDV, which may limit generalizability to in vivo conditions. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the absence of direct clinical or epidemiological data.

免疫接种与乙型肝炎病毒:欧洲儿童肝病学会(ESPGHAN)肝病学委员会的观点

Immunization and the hepatitis B virus: A view from the ESPGHAN Hepatology Committee
作者： Benno Kohlmaier, Giuseppe Indolfi, Ulrich Baumann, Cristina Gonçalves, Emmanuel Gonzales, Norman Junge, Emanuele Nicastro, Tudor Lucian Pop, Jesus Quintero, Xavier Stephenne
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a global health challenge, with more than 250 million people chronically infected worldwide and >2000 daily deaths from HBV-related disease. The study addresses the need to clarify the role of early-life immunization in preventing chronic HBV infection.
设计与对象
该研究回顾了现有文献和证据,重点关注人类 乙型肝炎病毒 感染、基因型和疾病。它包括有关生命早期感染乙肝病毒及其与慢性感染的关系的数据,以及婴儿普遍免疫接种的影响。
核心方法
作者对包括流行病学数据、临床研究和公共卫生报告在内的证据基础进行了审查,以评估生命早期 HBV 传播 的风险和免疫策略的有效性。
主要结果
生命早期获得性感染是慢性感染的主要驱动因素;高达 90% 的围产期感染婴儿会发展为慢性乙肝病毒感染,而年龄较大的儿童和成人的比率则明显较低。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为生命早期 HBV 传播 显着增加慢性感染风险的证据,他们认为这强调了免疫接种在预防此类结果中的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,有必要维持婴儿乙肝疫苗的普遍免疫,以减少慢性感染、肝硬化和肝细胞癌的发生,特别是在高危人群中。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于对现有文献的回顾,没有提供新数据,这可能会限制其证据强度。然而,它引用了大量研究支持生命早期乙型肝炎免疫的安全性和有效性。

Immunization and the hepatitis B virus: A view from the ESPGHAN Hepatology Committee

Authors: Benno Kohlmaier, Giuseppe Indolfi, Ulrich Baumann, Cristina Gonçalves, Emmanuel Gonzales, Norman Junge, Emanuele Nicastro, Tudor Lucian Pop, Jesus Quintero, Xavier Stephenne
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a global health challenge, with more than 250 million people chronically infected worldwide and >2000 daily deaths from HBV‐related disease. Early‐life acquisition is the primary driver of chronic infection; up to 90% of infants infected perinatally progress to chronic HBV infection compared with markedly lower rates in older children and adults. Universal infant immunization, including the birth‐dose, has been central to global elimination, resulting in a substantial reduction in chronic HBV infection, cirrhosis, and hepatocellular carcinoma. Recent statements from the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention question the evidence‐base for early‐life vaccination and recommend deferring HBV immunization until later in childhood. These claims reflect misconceptions about HBV epidemiology and reference a perceived association between infant vaccination and autism. This communication summarizes evidence demonstrating the risks of early‐life HBV transmission leading to chronic infection. We argue that delaying or foregoing immunization would leave infants at unnecessary risk. We reference two decades of research in which large‐scale studies have not identified an association between vaccination, including HBV vaccination, and autism.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a global health challenge, with more than 250 million people chronically infected worldwide and >2000 daily deaths from HBV-related disease. The study addresses the need to clarify the role of early-life immunization in preventing chronic HBV infection.
Design and population
The study is a review of existing literature and evidence, focusing on human hepatitis B virus infection, genotypes and disease. It includes data on early-life acquisition of HBV and its association with chronic infection, as well as the impact of universal infant immunization.
Core methods
The authors conducted a review of the evidence base, including epidemiological data, clinical studies and public health reports, to assess the risks of early-life HBV transmission and the effectiveness of immunization strategies.
Main results
Early-life acquisition is the primary driver of chronic infection; up to 90% of infants infected perinatally progress to chronic HBV infection compared with markedly lower rates in older children and adults.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as evidence that early-life HBV transmission significantly increases the risk of chronic infection, and they argue that this underscores the importance of immunization in preventing such outcomes.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the necessity of maintaining universal infant immunization against HBV to reduce the incidence of chronic infection, cirrhosis, and hepatocellular carcinoma, particularly in high-risk populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a review of existing literature and does not present new data, which may limit its evidence strength. However, it references a substantial body of research supporting the safety and efficacy of early-life HBV immunization.

乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎负担的演变:2021 年全球疾病负担研究的过去趋势和未来预测。

The Evolving Burden of Hepatitis B and C: Past Trends and Future Projections from the Global Burden of Disease Study 2021.
作者： Yue Chen, He-Hui Mao, Ming-Jing Zhang, Jin-Yan Zhang, Xue-Qin Chen, Wei-Feng Huang
摘要中文翻译背景:乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎继续对全球公共卫生构成重大挑战,本研究旨在评估其负担。方法:利用 2021 年全球疾病负担的数据,我们使用 Joinpoint 回归、年龄周期 队列研究 模型和分解分析,分析了 1990 年至 2021 年乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎的年龄标准化 死亡率 率 (ASMR) 和年龄标准化伤残调整生命年率 (ASDR)。应用贝叶斯年龄周期 队列研究 模型来预测截至 2036 年的未来趋势。此外,我们评估了可改变的风险因素造成的负担及其与社会经济指标的关联。结果:从1990年到2021年,全球乙型肝炎ASMR从每10万人12.19下降到7.57,丙型肝炎ASMR从每10万人8.18下降到6.10。尽管出现这些下降,但死亡人数和伤残调整生命年的绝对人数继续增加,这主要是由人口增长和老龄化推动的。2021 年,与女性相比,男性的乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎负担要高得多。此外,社会经济发展水平较低的国家面临着更大的负担。值得注意的是,大量饮酒占乙型肝炎 DALY 的 16.20%,而吸毒则占丙型肝炎 DALY 的 49.49%。到 2036 年,乙型肝炎 ASMR 预计将进一步下降至每 100,000 人 5.90,丙型肝炎则进一步下降至每 100,000 人 5.23。结论:尽管 ASMR 和 ASDR 有所下降,但乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎的全球负担仍然相当大。继续努力疫苗接种、抗病毒药物、治疗以及控制关键风险因素至关重要,特别是在高负担人群中。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究通过评估乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎随时间的全球负担并预测未来趋势,解决了乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎带来的持续公共卫生挑战。
设计与对象
作者利用《2021 年全球疾病负担》的数据,分析了 1990 年至 2021 年乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎的年龄标准化 死亡率 和伤残调整生命年率,并预测了截至 2036 年的未来趋势。
核心方法
应用连接点回归、年龄周期-队列研究 模型、分解分析和贝叶斯年龄周期-队列研究 模型来量化负担和预测趋势。
主要结果
From 1990 to 2021, global age-standardized mortality rates for hepatitis B declined from 12.19 to 7.57 per 100,000 and for hepatitis C from 8.18 to 6.10 per 100,000, yet absolute deaths and DALYs rose due to population growth and aging.
解释与创新
作者将尽管发病率下降但持续的负担解释为人口变化和风险因素暴露造成的,强调了持续干预的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了 疫苗接种、抗病毒药物 疗法以及有针对性地控制酒精和药物使用对于减少乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎负担的重要性,特别是在低社会经济环境中。
局限与证据强度
仅抽象的证据限制了对数据质量、模型假设和潜在偏差的详细评估,需要适度的置信度评级。

The Evolving Burden of Hepatitis B and C: Past Trends and Future Projections from the Global Burden of Disease Study 2021.

Authors: Yue Chen, He-Hui Mao, Ming-Jing Zhang, Jin-Yan Zhang, Xue-Qin Chen, Wei-Feng Huang
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Hepatitis B and C continue to pose significant challenges to global public health, and this study is aimed at evaluating their burden. METHODS: Leveraging data from the Global Burden of Disease 2021, we analyzed age-standardized mortality rates (ASMRs) and age-standardized disability-adjusted life year rates (ASDRs) for hepatitis B and C from 1990 to 2021 using Joinpoint regression, age-period-cohort models, and decomposition analysis. Bayesian age-period-cohort models were applied to project future trends up to 2036. Additionally, we assessed the burden attributable to modifiable risk factors and its association with socioeconomic indicators. RESULTS: From 1990 to 2021, the global ASMR for hepatitis B declined from 12.19 to 7.57 per 100,000, and the ASMR for hepatitis C decreased from 8.18 to 6.10 per 100,000. Despite these decreases, the absolute numbers of deaths and DALYs continued to increase, mainly driven by population growth and aging. In 2021, males carried a disproportionately higher burden of hepatitis B and C compared to females. Moreover, countries with low socioeconomic development faced a greater burden. Notably, high alcohol consumption accounted for 16.20% of hepatitis B DALYs, while drug use was responsible for 49.49% of hepatitis C DALYs. By 2036, ASMRs are projected to decline further to 5.90 per 100,000 for hepatitis B and 5.23 per 100,000 for hepatitis C. CONCLUSIONS: Although ASMRs and ASDRs have declined, the global burden of hepatitis B and C remains considerable. Continued efforts in vaccination, antiviral treatment, and control of key risk factors are crucial, especially in populations with a high burden.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the ongoing public-health challenge posed by hepatitis B and C by evaluating their global burden over time and projecting future trends.
Design and population
Using data from the Global Burden of Disease 2021, the authors analyzed age-standardized mortality and disability-adjusted life year rates for hepatitis B and C from 1990 to 2021 and projected future trends up to 2036.
Core methods
Joinpoint regression, age-period-cohort models, decomposition analysis, and Bayesian age-period-cohort models were applied to quantify burden and forecast trends.
Main results
From 1990 to 2021, global age-standardized mortality rates for hepatitis B declined from 12.19 to 7.57 per 100,000 and for hepatitis C from 8.18 to 6.10 per 100,000, yet absolute deaths and DALYs rose due to population growth and aging.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the persistent burden despite declining rates as driven by demographic shifts and risk-factor exposure, highlighting the need for sustained interventions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the importance of vaccination, antiviral therapy, and targeted control of alcohol and drug use to reduce hepatitis B and C burden, particularly in low-socioeconomic settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The abstract-only evidence limits detailed assessment of data quality, model assumptions, and potential biases, warranting a moderate confidence rating.

波兰首次乙型肝炎住院的合并症和死亡趋势(2012-2023 年)。

Trends in Comorbidities and Fatalities Among First-Time Hepatitis B Hospitalizations in Poland (2012-2023).
作者： Agnieszka Genowska, Krystyna Dobrowolska, Dorota Zarębska-Michaluk, Jerzy Jaroszewicz, Krzysztof Kanecki, Katarzyna Lewtak, Paweł Goryński, Aneta Nitsch-Osuch, Piotr Rzymski, Robert Flisiak
摘要中文翻译背景 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 住院患者的临床特征越来越受到共存疾病和死亡模式的影响。本研究旨在评估 2012 年至 2023 年间首次因 HBV 住院患者的合并症和死亡率。材料和方法 利用全国总医院发病率研究的数据,对波兰首次 HBV 住院患者进行了 12 年全国回顾性分析。结果消化系统疾病(15.6%)、心血管疾病(10.5%)以及内分泌和代谢紊乱(9.6%)是最常见的合并症。2012 年至 2023 年间,消化系统疾病 (12.3%-27.4%) 和代谢疾病 (8.5%-18.5%) 疾病显着增加,而循环系统疾病则从 7.4% 上升至 18.6%,2019 年后趋于稳定。免疫相关疾病和泌尿生殖系统疾病在 2019 年后呈现持续增长,而包括肿瘤和呼吸系统疾病在内的几个类别则呈现增长-下降模式。高血压和肝纤维化是最常见的个体诊断,并且随着时间的推移持续或逐步增加。年龄分层分析显示,年轻患者的感染相关疾病向老年患者的心脏代谢和晚期肝脏疾病转变。死亡率从每 1000 人住院 6.8-15.9 人(2012-2019 年)增加到每 1000 人住院 23.1-30.7 人(2020-2023 年),在 2020-2021 年达到峰值,其中男性 死亡率 较高。结论 乙型肝炎首次住院越来越多地表现为多重发病率和死亡率上升,特别是在老年患者中。这些发现凸显了日益增长的临床和公共卫生负担,强调需要针对年龄进行综合管理和改进 监测,以减少 死亡率 并优化 HBV 护理。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估 2012 年至 2023 年间首次因 HBV 住院的患者的合并症和死亡率。乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 住院患者的临床特征越来越受到共存疾病和死亡模式的影响。
设计与对象
利用全国综合医院发病率研究的数据,对波兰首次 HBV 住院患者进行了 12 年全国回顾性分析。该研究纳入了 2012 年至 2023 年间首次因 HBV 住院的患者。
核心方法
该研究使用全国综合医院发病率研究的数据来分析首次乙肝住院患者的合并症和死亡率。进行年龄分层分析以确定不同条件下 患病率 的趋势。
主要结果
消化系统疾病(15.6%)、心血管疾病(10.5%)以及内分泌和代谢紊乱(9.6%)是最常见的合并症。免疫相关疾病和泌尿生殖系统疾病在 2019 年后呈现持续增长,而包括肿瘤和呼吸系统疾病在内的几个类别则呈现增长-下降模式。
解释与创新
作者解释说,这些发现凸显了由于感染相关疾病向心脏代谢疾病和晚期肝脏疾病的转变,临床和公共卫生负担日益增加,尤其是老年患者。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调需要进行综合的、针对年龄的管理和改进的 监测,以减少 死亡率 并优化 HBV 护理。这些结果有助于了解乙型肝炎及其相关合并症不断变化的临床特征。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于回顾性数据,这可能会引入偏差并限制建立因果关系的能力。证据是中等的,因为它来自全国数据集,没有详细的实验室或临床程序。

Trends in Comorbidities and Fatalities Among First-Time Hepatitis B Hospitalizations in Poland (2012-2023).

Authors: Agnieszka Genowska, Krystyna Dobrowolska, Dorota Zarębska-Michaluk, Jerzy Jaroszewicz, Krzysztof Kanecki, Katarzyna Lewtak, Paweł Goryński, Aneta Nitsch-Osuch, Piotr Rzymski, Robert Flisiak
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND The clinical profile of patients hospitalized with hepatitis B virus (HBV) is increasingly influenced by coexisting conditions and death patterns. This study aimed to assess comorbidities and fatality rates among patients hospitalized for the first time with HBV between 2012 and 2023. MATERIAL AND METHODS A 12-year retrospective nationwide analysis of first-time HBV hospitalizations in Poland was conducted using data from the Nationwide General Hospital Morbidity Study. RESULTS Digestive diseases (15.6%), cardiovascular diseases (10.5%), and endocrine and metabolic disorders (9.6%) were the most prevalent comorbidities. Between 2012 and 2023, marked increases were observed in digestive (12.3%-27.4%) and metabolic (8.5%-18.5%) disorders, while circulatory diseases rose from 7.4% to 18.6% with post-2019 stabilization. Immune-related and genitourinary disorders demonstrated sustained post-2019 growth, whereas several categories, including neoplasms and respiratory diseases, showed growth-decline patterns. Hypertension and liver fibrosis were the most frequent individual diagnoses, with sustained or stepwise increases over time. Age-stratified analyses revealed a shift from infection-related conditions in younger patients toward cardiometabolic and advanced hepatic diseases in older groups. Fatality rates increased from 6.8-15.9 per 1000 hospitalizations (2012-2019) to 23.1-30.7 per 1000 (2020-2023), peaking during 2020-2021, with higher mortality in men. CONCLUSIONS First-time HBV hospitalizations are increasingly characterized by multimorbidity and rising fatality rates, particularly in older patients. These findings highlight a growing clinical and public health burden, underscoring the need for integrated, age-tailored management and improved surveillance to reduce mortality and optimize HBV care.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to assess comorbidities and fatality rates among patients hospitalized for the first time with HBV between 2012 and 2023. The clinical profile of patients hospitalized with hepatitis B virus (HBV) is increasingly influenced by coexisting conditions and death patterns.
Design and population
A 12-year retrospective nationwide analysis of first-time HBV hospitalizations in Poland was conducted using data from the Nationwide General Hospital Morbidity Study. The study included patients hospitalized for the first time with HBV between 2012 and 2023.
Core methods
The study used data from the Nationwide General Hospital Morbidity Study to analyze comorbidities and fatality rates among first-time HBV hospitalizations. Age-stratified analyses were performed to identify trends in the prevalence of different conditions.
Main results
Digestive diseases (15.6%), cardiovascular diseases (10.5%), and endocrine and metabolic disorders (9.6%) were the most prevalent comorbidities. Immune-related and genitourinary disorders demonstrated sustained post-2019 growth, whereas several categories, including neoplasms and respiratory diseases, showed growth-decline patterns.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as highlighting a growing clinical and public health burden, particularly in older patients, due to the shift from infection-related conditions to cardiometabolic and advanced hepatic diseases.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings underscore the need for integrated, age-tailored management and improved surveillance to reduce mortality and optimize HBV care. The results inform the understanding of the evolving clinical profile of HBV and its associated comorbidities.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on retrospective data, which may introduce biases and limit the ability to establish causality. The evidence is moderate, as it is derived from a nationwide dataset without detailed laboratory or clinical procedures.

血清 HBsAg 无法区分 共价闭合环状DNA 病毒感染中的 共价闭合环状DNA 活性和整合的 HBV DNA 表达。

Serum HBsAg cannot distinguish cccDNA activity from integrated HBV DNA expression in chronic hepatitis B virus infection.
作者： Aleksei Suslov, Matthias S Matter, Diego Calabrese, Athimed El Taher, Stefan Wieland, Markus H Heim
摘要中文翻译背景与目的:HBsAg 目前被用作定义 慢性乙型肝炎 或 慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)的关键血清生物标志物。HBsAg 下降通常被解释为 共价闭合环状DNA 消除或持久转录沉默的证据。然而,HBsAg 也可以由整合到 宿主 基因组 (iDNA) 中的转录活性 HBV DNA 产生,这可能使血清 HBsAg 成为 共价闭合环状DNA 活性或数量的不可靠指标。方法:我们结合肝内 HBsAg 免疫染色、定量血清 HBsAg 水平 (qHBsAg) 以及肝活检中基于数字微滴 PCR 的 HBV DNA 和 RNA 分析,以确定 HBsAg 表达主要由 共价闭合环状DNA 或 iDNA 驱动的患者。然后我们比较了这些组之间的 HBsAg 丰度、亚型组成、分泌和肝内染色模式。结果:两组的肝组织和血清中均可检测到所有三种包膜蛋白(L、M、S)。通过蛋白质印迹和免疫荧光评估,L 和 S 的相对丰度在 iDNA 驱动的病例和 共价闭合环状DNA 驱动的病例之间没有差异。在组合的 队列研究 中,肝内 HBsAg 负荷与血清 qHBsAg 相关,表明细胞内滞留和分泌行为相似。重要的是,所有已建立的肝内 HBsAg 染色模式(膜亚型和细胞质亚型)均出现在两组中,没有与任一来源唯一相关的模式。结论:因此,iDNA 可以维持 HBsAg 表达谱,该表达谱与活性 共价闭合环状DNA 产生的表达谱无法区分。因此,仅凭血清 HBsAg 可能无法识别已实现 共价闭合环状DNA 消除或持久沉默的患者。这些发现表明,在临床研究中使用血清 qHBsAg 作为 功能性治愈 终点的组成部分值得重新评估。影响和意义:血清乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 是用于定义和监测 慢性乙型肝炎 中“功能性治愈”的核心生物标志物。这项研究表明,在存在大量 HBV DNA 整合的患者中,HBsAg 主要由整合序列产生,并且整合衍生的 HBsAg 与 共价闭合环状DNA 衍生的 HBsAg 在异构体组成、分泌行为和组织学分布方面无法区分。长读长测序证实整合的序列产生全长包膜转录本。这些发现与病毒学家、肝病学家、临床试验人员以及制定和评估乙肝治疗策略的监管机构最为相关。他们表明,血清 HBsAg 测量无法报告 共价闭合环状DNA 负担或转录活性,并且关注 HBsAg 消失可能会错过具有良好临床结果的“部分治愈”状态。这需要重新评估 HBsAg 丢失作为主要 功能性治愈 终点,并支持使用补充或源特异性标记物来评估 治疗 反应。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨血清 HBsAg 是否能够可靠地区分 共价闭合环状DNA 病毒感染中的 共价闭合环状DNA 活性和整合的 HBV DNA 表达,因为 HBsAg 目前被用作定义 功能性治愈 的关键生物标志物。
设计与对象
该研究对 队列研究 病毒感染患者进行联合分析,分析肝内 HBsAg 负荷和血清 qHBsAg 水平,以评估其相关性和行为。
核心方法
研究人员使用肝内 HBsAg 免疫染色、定量血清 HBsAg 水平 (qHBsAg) 和数字微滴 PCR 来分析肝活检中的 HBV DNA 和 RNA。他们还进行了长读长测序,以确认从整合序列中生成了全长包膜转录本。
主要结果
在 共价闭合环状DNA 或整合的 HBV DNA 驱动的 HBsAg 表达患者的肝组织和血清中,所有三种包膜蛋白(L、M、S)均可检测到。L 和 S 蛋白的相对丰度在两组之间没有差异,并且所有已建立的 HBsAg 染色模式都发生在两组中,没有独特的关联。
解释与创新
作者解释说,整合的 HBV DNA 可以产生与活性 共价闭合环状DNA 无法区分的 HBsAg 谱,这表明单独的血清 HBsAg 水平可能无法准确反映 共价闭合环状DNA 状态或转录活性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,血清 HBsAg 测量可能不足以评估 共价闭合环状DNA 消除或持久沉默,强调在评估 治疗 对 慢性乙型肝炎 的反应时需要补充或来源特异性标记物。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象证据,设计不包括详细的统计分析或长期随访。由于依赖间接测量且缺乏直接因果结论,证据强度中等。

Serum HBsAg cannot distinguish cccDNA activity from integrated HBV DNA expression in chronic hepatitis B virus infection.

Authors: Aleksei Suslov, Matthias S Matter, Diego Calabrese, Athimed El Taher, Stefan Wieland, Markus H Heim
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND & AIMS: HBsAg is currently used as the key serum biomarker to define functional cure of chronic hepatitis B (CHB). Declining HBsAg is often interpreted as evidence of cccDNA elimination or durable transcriptional silencing. However, HBsAg can also be produced from transcriptionally active HBV DNA integrated into the host genome (iDNA), which can make serum HBsAg an unreliable indicator of the cccDNA activity or amount. METHODS: We combined intrahepatic HBsAg immunostaining, quantitative serum HBsAg levels (qHBsAg), and digital droplet PCR-based profiling of HBV DNA and RNA in liver biopsies to identify patients in whom HBsAg expression was predominantly driven by cccDNA or by iDNA. We then compared HBsAg abundance, isoform composition, secretion, and intrahepatic staining patterns between these groups. RESULTS: All three envelope proteins (L, M, S) were detectable in liver tissue and serum in both groups. The relative abundance of L and S, assessed by western blot and immunofluorescence, did not differ between iDNA- and cccDNA-driven cases. Across the combined cohort, intrahepatic HBsAg burden correlated with serum qHBsAg, indicating similar intracellular retention and secretion behavior. Importantly, all established intrahepatic HBsAg staining patterns (membranous and cytoplasmic subtypes) occurred in both groups, with no pattern uniquely associated with either source. CONCLUSIONS: Thus, iDNA can sustain HBsAg expression profiles that are indistinguishable from those generated by active cccDNA. Serum HBsAg alone may therefore fail to identify patients who have achieved cccDNA elimination or durable silencing. These findings suggest that the use of serum qHBsAg as a component of the functional cure endpoint in clinical studies warrants re-evaluation. IMPACT AND IMPLICATIONS: Serum hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) is the central biomarker used to define and monitor "functional cure" in chronic hepatitis B. This study shows that in patients with substantial HBV DNA integration, HBsAg is produced predominantly from integrated sequences, and that integration-derived HBsAg is indistinguishable from cccDNA-derived HBsAg in isoform composition, secretion behaviour, and histological distribution. Long-read sequencing confirmed that the integrated sequences generate full-length envelope transcripts. These findings are most relevant to virologists, hepatologists, clinical trialists, and regulators developing and evaluating HBV cure strategies. They indicate that serum HBsAg measurement cannot report cccDNA burden or transcriptional activity, and that focusing on HBsAg loss could miss "partial cure" states with favourable clinical outcomes. This warrants re-evaluation of HBsAg loss as a main functional cure endpoint, and supports the use of complementary or source-specific markers to assess treatment response.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate whether serum HBsAg can reliably distinguish between cccDNA activity and integrated HBV DNA expression in chronic hepatitis B virus infection, as HBsAg is currently used as a key biomarker for defining functional cure.
Design and population
The study was conducted on a combined cohort of patients with chronic hepatitis B virus infection, analyzing intrahepatic HBsAg burden and serum qHBsAg levels to assess their correlation and behavior.
Core methods
The researchers used intrahepatic HBsAg immunostaining, quantitative serum HBsAg levels (qHBsAg), and digital droplet PCR to profile HBV DNA and RNA in liver biopsies. They also performed long-read sequencing to confirm the generation of full-length envelope transcripts from integrated sequences.
Main results
All three envelope proteins (L, M, S) were detectable in both liver tissue and serum in patients with HBsAg expression driven by either cccDNA or integrated HBV DNA. The relative abundance of L and S proteins did not differ between the two groups, and all established HBsAg staining patterns occurred in both groups without unique associations.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that integrated HBV DNA can produce HBsAg profiles indistinguishable from those of active cccDNA, suggesting that serum HBsAg levels alone may not accurately reflect cccDNA status or transcriptional activity.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings indicate that serum HBsAg measurement may not be sufficient to assess cccDNA elimination or durable silencing, highlighting the need for complementary or source-specific markers in evaluating treatment response for chronic hepatitis B.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-only evidence, and the design does not include detailed statistical analyses or long-term follow-up. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the reliance on indirect measures and the absence of direct causal conclusions.
