孟加拉国大学生乙型肝炎意识、知识差距、寻求健康行为、风险认知和 疫苗接种 状况的综合评估:一项横断面研究。

A Comprehensive Evaluation of Hepatitis B Awareness, Knowledge Gaps, Health-Seeking Behaviour, Risk Perceptions, and Vaccination Status Among University Students in Bangladesh: A Cross-Sectional Study.
作者： Ali Ashraf Sakil, Md. Murad Hossain, Rayhan Ivan Rimon, Hasibur Rahaman, Rafaz Samnan Nahid, Md. Shohel Rana
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估孟加拉国大学生对乙型肝炎的认识、知识差距、寻求健康的行为、风险认知和 疫苗接种 状况,因为乙型肝炎病毒感染是全球重大的公共卫生问题。
设计与对象
该研究是在孟加拉国进行的为期 6 个月(2024 年 8 月 24 日至 2025 年 2 月 28 日)的横断面调查,涉及 440 名随机选择的大学生。
核心方法
使用分发给学生的预先设计的结构化问卷收集数据,并使用 Microsoft Excel 2016 和 Stata 17 进行统计分析。使用多变量逻辑回归分析来评估变量之间的关联。
主要结果
总体而言,受访者对乙型肝炎的认知度较高,大多数人认识到该疾病及其主要传播途径和健康后果。略超过一半的受访者对乙型肝炎表现出令人满意的知识、态度和做法。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了孟加拉国学生需要对乙型肝炎感染及其危险因素进行有针对性的教育,以及 疫苗接种 的重要性,因为 疫苗 相关知识一般,而 疫苗接种 的使用率仍然相对较低。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为公共卫生策略提供参考,以提高孟加拉国大学生乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 率和认识,强调教育和有针对性的干预措施的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横截面设计的限制,无法建立因果关系,并且依赖自我报告的数据,可能会引入偏差。由于这些限制,证据强度中等。

A Comprehensive Evaluation of Hepatitis B Awareness, Knowledge Gaps, Health-Seeking Behaviour, Risk Perceptions, and Vaccination Status Among University Students in Bangladesh: A Cross-Sectional Study.

Authors: Ali Ashraf Sakil, Md. Murad Hossain, Rayhan Ivan Rimon, Hasibur Rahaman, Rafaz Samnan Nahid, Md. Shohel Rana
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Background Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection is a significant public health problem globally. In Bangladesh, viral hepatitis is the most common liver disease, with approximately 10 million people infected with HBV. Methods The study was a cross‐sectional survey in which 440 respondents were randomly selected using Cochran's formula. The duration of the study was about 6 month (24th August 2024 to 28th February 2025). Data was gathered using a predesigned and structured questionnaire, which was distributed among the students. The collected data were then analysed utilizing Microsoft Excel 2016 and Stata 17 software for statistical processing and interpretation. Results Overall, the study found that respondents had a high level of awareness of hepatitis B, with most participants recognizing the disease and its major transmission routes and health consequences. Vaccine‐related knowledge was moderate, whereas hepatitis B vaccination uptake remained relatively low among the respondents and their family members. The mean (±SD) awareness, vaccine knowledge, and perception scores were 37.18 ± 11.16, 25.00 ± 10.88, and 22.38 ± 4.71 (each measured on a 50‐point scale), respectively. Multivariable logistic regression analysis showed that male participants were more likely to be vaccinated against hepatitis B than female participants (aOR = 1.74, 95% CI: 1.14–2.67). Participants with low awareness also had higher odds of being vaccinated than those with high awareness (aOR = 2.17, 95% CI: 1.09–4.36), while no significant association was observed for moderate awareness. Conclusion Less than half of the respondents had received the hepatitis B vaccine, with the most common reason for non‐vaccination being a lack of awareness of their vaccination status. Slightly over half of the respondents demonstrated satisfactory knowledge, attitudes, and practices regarding hepatitis B. These findings underscore the need for targeted education on hepatitis B infection, its risk factors, and the importance of vaccination among students in Bangladesh.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate hepatitis B awareness, knowledge gaps, health-seeking behavior, risk perceptions, and vaccination status among university students in Bangladesh, as HBV infection is a significant public health problem globally.
Design and population
The study was a cross-sectional survey conducted over 6 months (24th August 2024 to 28th February 2025) in Bangladesh, involving 440 randomly selected university students.
Core methods
Data was collected using a predesigned and structured questionnaire distributed among students, and statistical analysis was performed using Microsoft Excel 2016 and Stata 17. Multivariable logistic regression analysis was used to assess associations between variables.
Main results
Overall, respondents had a high level of awareness of hepatitis B, with most recognizing the disease and its major transmission routes and health consequences. Slightly over half of the respondents demonstrated satisfactory knowledge, attitudes, and practices regarding hepatitis B.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the need for targeted education on hepatitis B infection, its risk factors, and the importance of vaccination among students in Bangladesh, as vaccine-related knowledge was moderate and vaccination uptake remained relatively low.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results can inform public health strategies to improve hepatitis B vaccination rates and awareness among university students in Bangladesh, emphasizing the importance of education and targeted interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which cannot establish causality, and the reliance on self-reported data, which may introduce bias. The evidence strength is moderate due to these limitations.

艾伯塔省 慢性乙型肝炎 和丁型肝炎检测的地理空间差异:基于人群的空间分析。

Geospatial Discrepancy of Chronic Hepatitis B and Hepatitis D Testing in Alberta: A Population-Based Spatial Analysis.
作者： Bryce Tkachuk, Isabelle Couloigner, Carla S Coffin, Jason Jiang, Stefania Bertazzon, Abdel-Aziz Shaheen
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 和丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 双重感染是肝硬化和肝细胞癌的主要原因。CHB 的地理分布以及抗 HDV 检测与 CHB 负担的一致性仍然不明确。
设计与对象
We aimed to examine if anti-HDV testing and seropositivity align with areas of highest CHB burden. Among 8317 persons with CHB, the provincial age- and sex-standardized prevalence was 1.70 per 1000 population (95% CI, 1.66-1.74). Prevalence ranged from 2.15 per 1000 in metropolitan areas to 0.40 per 1000 in remote regions and was higher in Calgary than Edmonton (2.53 vs.
核心方法
使用省级实验室数据库中捕获 HBV/HDV 血清学 的经过验证的血清学算法来识别患有 CHB 的成人(2014-2022 年)。病例被地理定位到与艾伯塔省卫生服务区和城乡连续体水平相关的聚合传播区 (ADA)。使用描述性映射、全局空间自相关和局部热点分析来评估空间聚类。
主要结果
总体而言,17.5% 的慢性乙型肝炎患者接受了抗 HDV 检测,其中 4.1% 的患者抗 HDV 呈阳性。CHB 患病率 热点集中在大都市 ADA,而抗 HDV 检测和阳性的热点仅与这些高负担地区部分重叠。
解释与创新
艾伯塔省的慢性乙型肝炎负担在地理上集中,特别是在大都市的特定社区内,而抗丁型肝炎病毒检测仍然很少,并且仅部分与高负担地区一致。反射或系统性抗丁型肝炎病毒检测,与地理信息丰富的外展服务相结合,可能会改善病例检测。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为抗 HDV 检测和 Z​​XQTERMIQXZ 的地理针对性策略提供信息,以更好地识别和管理高负担地区的 HDV 合并感染。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告与研究设计或数据收集方法相关的局限性。

Geospatial Discrepancy of Chronic Hepatitis B and Hepatitis D Testing in Alberta: A Population-Based Spatial Analysis.

Authors: Bryce Tkachuk, Isabelle Couloigner, Carla S Coffin, Jason Jiang, Stefania Bertazzon, Abdel-Aziz Shaheen
Original abstract (English)
Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) and hepatitis D virus (HDV) coinfection are major causes of cirrhosis and hepatocellular carcinoma. The geographic distribution of CHB and alignment of anti-HDV testing with CHB burden remain poorly defined. We aimed to examine if anti-HDV testing and seropositivity align with areas of highest CHB burden. Adults with CHB (2014-2022) were identified using a validated serologic algorithm in the provincial laboratory database capturing HBV/HDV serology. Cases were geolocated to Aggregate Dissemination Areas (ADAs), linked to Alberta Health Services zones and rural-urban continuum levels. Age- and sex-standardized CHB prevalence was calculated per ADA using the 2016 Canadian Census. Descriptive mapping, global spatial autocorrelation and local hotspot analysis were used to assess spatial clustering. Among 8317 persons with CHB, the provincial age- and sex-standardized prevalence was 1.70 per 1000 population (95% CI, 1.66-1.74). Prevalence ranged from 2.15 per 1000 in metropolitan areas to 0.40 per 1000 in remote regions and was higher in Calgary than Edmonton (2.53 vs. Overall, 17.5% of CHB patients were tested for anti-HDV, and 4.1% of those tested were anti-HDV positive. CHB prevalence hotspots were concentrated in metropolitan ADAs, whereas hotspots of anti-HDV testing and positivity showed only partial overlap with these high-burden areas. CHB burden in Alberta is geographically clustered, particularly within specific neighbourhoods of metropolitan cities, whereas anti-HDV testing remains infrequent and only partially aligned with high-burden areas. Reflex or systematic anti-HDV testing, paired with geographically informed outreach, may improve case detection.
View research seven-element analysis

The findings may inform geographically targeted strategies for anti-HDV testing and surveillance to better identify and manage HDV coinfection in high-burden areas.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations related to the study design or data collection methods.

单细胞乙型肝炎测序揭示了与重复感染一致的独特病毒感染事件。

Single-Cell Hepatitis B Sequencing Reveals Distinct Viral Infection Events Consistent With Superinfection.
作者： Monika Mani, Steven J Clipman, Justin R Bailey, Richard K Sterling, Mark S Sulkowski, Mark Anderson, Ana Olivo, Giulia Belluccini, Gavin Cloherty, Ruy M Ribeiro
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在研究个体肝细胞内 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 的遗传多样性及其对病毒复制和共价闭合环状 DNA (共价闭合环状DNA) 动力学的影响。
设计与对象
该研究使用四名 HIV/HBV 混合感染者(HB1、HB2、HB3、HB6)的肝活检,通过 RNA 测序分析肝细胞之间和肝细胞内的 HBV 多样性。
核心方法
研究人员开发了测序方案来表征 HBV 多样性,方法是从单独分离的肝细胞中提取 RNA,并对四个肝活检中 200 多个肝细胞中的 HBV 进行测序。
主要结果
Two biopsies (HB1 and HB6) showed HBV diversity between hepatocytes that met an experimentally identified threshold. In HB1, 70.2% of cells had haplotype a, 5.8% had haplotype b, and 24% had both haplotypes in the same cell. HB6 exhibited drug resistance mutations distributed among hepatocytes and had up to 3 different sequences, including wild-type and drug-resistant variants, within the same hepatocyte.
解释与创新
研究结果表明,单个肝细胞可能含有多个转录活性的 HBV 共价闭合环状DNA 分子,这些分子可能源自不同的感染事件,凸显了 HBV 复制和多样性的复杂性。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果提供了对 HBV 复制和多样性机制的见解,可以为未来关于病毒 进化、重复感染和靶向治疗的开发的研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于 HIV/HBV 合并感染个体的肝活检,这可能会影响普遍性。该证据基于少量活检的测序数据,结果与实验阈值一致,表明可信度中等。

Single-Cell Hepatitis B Sequencing Reveals Distinct Viral Infection Events Consistent With Superinfection.

Authors: Monika Mani, Steven J Clipman, Justin R Bailey, Richard K Sterling, Mark S Sulkowski, Mark Anderson, Ana Olivo, Giulia Belluccini, Gavin Cloherty, Ruy M Ribeiro
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: The high error rate of the hepatitis B virus (HBV) polymerase leads to a genetically diverse quasispecies in individuals with chronic hepatitis B. Data regarding the propagation of these variants in individual hepatocytes may provide insight into viral replication and diversity of covalently closed circular DNA (cccDNA), which is the template for HBV replication. METHODS: We developed sequencing protocols to characterize HBV diversity between and within hepatocytes using RNA extracted from individually isolated hepatocytes. We sequenced HBV in >200 hepatocytes in 4 liver biopsies from people with HIV/HBV (HB1, HB2, HB3, HB6). RESULTS: We found that 2 biopsies (HB1 and HB6) showed HBV diversity between hepatocytes that met an experimentally identified threshold. Specifically, in HB1, HBV sequences from 86 individual cells were from 2 different haplotypes of genotype D: 70.2% of cells with haplotype a, 5.8% with haplotype b, and 24% with both haplotypes in the same cell. Furthermore, within single hepatocytes, up to 3 different HBV sequences were present per cell, including some cells with both genotypes A and D. HB6, who received years of lamivudine monotherapy, had evidence of drug resistance mutations distributed among hepatocytes and demonstrated up to 3 different sequences, including wild-type and drug-resistant sequences, in the same hepatocyte. Modeling of infected hepatocytes did not reveal evidence of local HBV spread based on spatial proximity. CONCLUSIONS: Taken together, our findings demonstrate that individual hepatocytes may harbor multiple, transcriptionally active HBV cccDNA molecules, which likely arose from distinct infection events.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the genetic diversity of hepatitis B virus (HBV) within individual hepatocytes and its implications for viral replication and covalently closed circular DNA (cccDNA) dynamics.
Design and population
The study used liver biopsies from four individuals with HIV/HBV co-infection (HB1, HB2, HB3, HB6) to analyze HBV diversity between and within hepatocytes using RNA sequencing.
Core methods
The researchers developed sequencing protocols to characterize HBV diversity by extracting RNA from individually isolated hepatocytes and sequencing HBV in over 200 hepatocytes across the four liver biopsies.
Main results
Two biopsies (HB1 and HB6) showed HBV diversity between hepatocytes that met an experimentally identified threshold. In HB1, 70.2% of cells had haplotype a, 5.8% had haplotype b, and 24% had both haplotypes in the same cell. HB6 exhibited drug resistance mutations distributed among hepatocytes and had up to 3 different sequences, including wild-type and drug-resistant variants, within the same hepatocyte.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that individual hepatocytes may harbor multiple, transcriptionally active HBV cccDNA molecules, which likely originated from distinct infection events, highlighting the complexity of HBV replication and diversity.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide insights into the mechanisms of HBV replication and diversity, which could inform future studies on viral evolution, superinfection, and the development of targeted therapies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to liver biopsies from individuals with HIV/HBV co-infection, which may affect generalizability. The evidence is based on sequencing data from a small number of biopsies, and the findings are consistent with experimental thresholds, suggesting moderate confidence.

高病毒载量 慢性乙型肝炎 患者对 抗病毒药物 治疗的病毒学反应:一项真实世界的 队列研究 研究。

Virologic Response to Antiviral Therapy in Chronic Hepatitis B Patients With High Viral Load: A Real-World Cohort Study.
作者： Siwei Zheng, Xueyan Li, Hongli Liu, Tianhao Mao, You Ge, Wenjing Ji, Xixuan Wang, Yijun Bao, Yu Zhang, Anling Zhou
摘要中文翻译目前的 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 指南已逐步扩大了 抗病毒药物 治疗 的适应症,年龄 > 30 岁越来越多地被纳入一个重要标准。然而,关于 治疗 在这一扩大的人群中的结果的现实世界证据仍然有限。本研究评估了年龄 > 30 岁且基线病毒载量较高的 治疗 初治 CHB 患者的 48 周 治疗 反应及其独立预测因子。这项回顾性研究纳入了 808 名年龄 > 30 岁且基线 HBV DNA ≥ 2000 IU/mL 且接受一线核苷类似物 (NA) 单药治疗的 808 名 治疗 初治 CHB 患者。患者被分为中高病毒载量组(MH;≥ 2 × 10 3 至 7 IU/mL)和极高病毒载量组(EH;≥ 1 × 10 7 IU/mL)。应用倾向得分匹配 (PSM) 和 治疗 加权逆概率 (IPTW) 来调整基线不平衡。第 48 周时,总体病毒学应答 (VR) 率为 90.0% (727/808)。在基线时 HBeAg 阳性患者中,HBeAg 血清清除率和血清转换率分别为 14.0% (59/422) 和 11.8% (50/422)。与 MH 组相比,EH 组的 VR 发生率显着较低(81.8% vs. 95.5%,p 7 IU/mL 与 VR 可能性较低独立相关。PSM 和 IPTW 调整后,这些关联仍然强劲。一线 NA 治疗在年龄 > 30 岁、高病毒载量的 CHB 患者中实现了良好的 48 周 VR。然而,HBeAg 阳性和极高的病毒载量与次优反应相关,支持强化监控、个性化管理。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
目前的 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 指南已逐步扩大了 抗病毒药物 治疗 的适应症,年龄 > 30 岁越来越多地被纳入一个重要标准。该研究旨在评估年龄 > 30 岁且基线病毒载量较高的 治疗 初治 CHB 患者的 48 周 治疗 反应及其独立预测因子。
设计与对象
这项回顾性研究纳入了 808 名年龄 > 30 岁且基线 HBV DNA ≥ 2000 IU/mL 且接受一线核苷类似物 (NA) 单药治疗的 808 名 治疗 初治 CHB 患者。患者被分为中高病毒载量组(MH;≥ 2 × 10 3 至 7 IU/mL)和极高病毒载量组(EH;≥ 1 × 10 7 IU/mL)。
核心方法
应用倾向得分匹配 (PSM) 和 治疗 加权逆概率 (IPTW) 来调整基线不平衡。该研究评估了第 48 周时的病毒学应答 (VR) 率,并确定了 VR 的独立预测因素。
主要结果
第 48 周时,总体病毒学应答 (VR) 率为 90.0% (727/808)。与 MH 组相比,EH 组的 VR 发生率显着较低(HBeAg 阳性率为 81.8%),并且极高的病毒载量与 VR 可能性较低独立相关。
解释与创新
研究结果支持对 HBeAg 阳性和病毒载量极高的患者加强监测和个体化管理,因为这些因素与一线 NA 治疗反应欠佳相关。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为 30 岁以上高病毒载量慢性乙型肝炎患者的管理提供了重要见解,强调需要根据病毒载量和 HBeAg 状态制定定制方法。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和潜在的无法测量的混杂因素而受到限制。然而,PSM 和 IPTW 的使用强化了证据,并且总体 VR 率很高,表明一线 NA 治疗具有良好的结果。

Virologic Response to Antiviral Therapy in Chronic Hepatitis B Patients With High Viral Load: A Real-World Cohort Study.

Authors: Siwei Zheng, Xueyan Li, Hongli Liu, Tianhao Mao, You Ge, Wenjing Ji, Xixuan Wang, Yijun Bao, Yu Zhang, Anling Zhou
Original abstract (English)
Current guidelines for chronic hepatitis B (CHB) have progressively expanded antiviral treatment indications, with age > 30 years increasingly being incorporated as an important criterion. However, real-world evidence regarding treatment outcomes in this expanded population remains limited. This study evaluated the 48-week treatment response and its independent predictors in treatment-naïve CHB patients aged > 30 years with a high baseline viral load. This retrospective study included 808 treatment-naïve CHB patients aged > 30 years with baseline HBV DNA ≥ 2000 IU/mL who received first-line nucleos(t)ide analogue (NA) monotherapy. Patients were classified into moderately high (MH; ≥ 2 × 10 3 to 7 IU/mL) and extremely high (EH; ≥ 1 × 10 7 IU/mL) viral load groups. Propensity score matching (PSM) and inverse probability of treatment weighting (IPTW) were applied to adjust for baseline imbalance. At week 48, the overall virological response (VR) rate was 90.0% (727/808). Among HBeAg-positive patients at baseline, the HBeAg seroclearance and seroconversion rates were 14.0% (59/422) and 11.8% (50/422), respectively. Compared with the MH group, the EH group had significantly lower rates of VR (81.8% vs. 95.5%, p 7 IU/mL were independently associated with a lower likelihood of VR. These associations remained robust after PSM and IPTW adjustment. First-line NA therapy achieved a favourable 48-week VR in CHB patients aged > 30 years with a high viral load. However, HBeAg positivity and extremely high viral load were associated with suboptimal response, supporting intensified monitoring and individualised management.

Question and background
Current guidelines for chronic hepatitis B (CHB) have progressively expanded antiviral treatment indications, with age > 30 years increasingly being incorporated as an important criterion. The study aimed to evaluate the 48-week treatment response and its independent predictors in treatment-naïve CHB patients aged > 30 years with a high baseline viral load.
Design and population
This retrospective study included 808 treatment-naïve CHB patients aged > 30 years with baseline HBV DNA ≥ 2000 IU/mL who received first-line nucleos(t)ide analogue (NA) monotherapy. Patients were classified into moderately high (MH; ≥ 2 × 10 3 to 7 IU/mL) and extremely high (EH; ≥ 1 × 10 7 IU/mL) viral load groups.
Core methods
Propensity score matching (PSM) and inverse probability of treatment weighting (IPTW) were applied to adjust for baseline imbalance. The study assessed virological response (VR) rates at week 48 and identified independent predictors of VR.
Main results
At week 48, the overall virological response (VR) rate was 90.0% (727/808). Compared with the MH group, the EH group had significantly lower rates of VR (81.8% vs. HBeAg positivity and extremely high viral load were independently associated with a lower likelihood of VR.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings support intensified monitoring and individualised management for patients with HBeAg positivity and extremely high viral load, as these factors were associated with suboptimal response to first-line NA therapy.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide important insights for the management of CHB patients aged > 30 years with high viral load, highlighting the need for tailored approaches based on viral load and HBeAg status.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential for unmeasured confounding. However, the use of PSM and IPTW strengthens the evidence, and the overall VR rate was high, suggesting a favourable outcome with first-line NA therapy.

Differential Hepatitis B Surface Antigen Glycan Isomer Kinetics Between Tenofovir Alafenamide and Entecavir: Hepatocellular Carcinoma Risk Stratification in Hepatitis B e Antigen-Positive Patients.
作者： Yuji Kita, Ayato Murata, Hiroki Nago, Masahiro Yamaguchi, Yoko Kato, Rihwa Om, Yuichro Terai, Yuji Ikeda, Shunsuke Sato, Yuji Shimada
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
核苷类似物 (NA) 疗法可抑制 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) DNA,但残留肝细胞癌 (HCC) 风险仍然存在。
设计与对象
我们评估了替诺福韦艾拉酚胺 (TAF) 与恩替卡韦 (ETV) 对 48 周 HBsAgGi 动力学的影响及其在 90 名未接受 NA 治疗的患者中对 HCC 风险进行分层的效用(ETV:73;TAF:17)。

由于该检测针对基因型 C 特异性 O-糖基化,因此研究结果不能推广到基因型 A、B 或 D。

与 ETV 相比,TAF 独立预测了良好的反应(惩罚比值比 3.60,p = 0.030)。

Differential Hepatitis B Surface Antigen Glycan Isomer Kinetics Between Tenofovir Alafenamide and Entecavir: Hepatocellular Carcinoma Risk Stratification in Hepatitis B e Antigen-Positive Patients.

Authors: Yuji Kita, Ayato Murata, Hiroki Nago, Masahiro Yamaguchi, Yoko Kato, Rihwa Om, Yuichro Terai, Yuji Ikeda, Shunsuke Sato, Yuji Shimada
Original abstract (English)
Nucleos(t)ide analog (NA) therapy suppresses hepatitis B virus (HBV) DNA, but residual hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) risk persists. O-glycosylated hepatitis B surface antigen glycan isomer (HBsAgGi) uniquely reflects virion burden. We evaluated the impact of tenofovir alafenamide (TAF) versus entecavir (ETV) on 48-week HBsAgGi kinetics and its utility in stratifying HCC risk in 90 NA-naïve patients (ETV: 73; TAF: 17). A favourable response was defined as a reduction or maintenance of low HBsAgGi levels. TAF independently predicted a favourable response compared with ETV (penalised odds ratio 3.60, p = 0.030). A significant interaction occurred between HBeAg status and HBsAgGi response in relation to HCC development (p = 0.038). In HBeAg-positive patients, a poor response was associated with increased HCC risk (hazard ratio 7.34, 95% confidence interval [CI] 1.96-20.35, p = 0.001); however, the exact magnitude warrants cautious interpretation due to wide CIs. This association was absent in HBeAg-negative cases. Adding HBsAgGi response to the aMAP score improved long-term HCC prediction exclusively in the HBeAg-positive cohort. As the assay targets genotype C-specific O-glycosylation, the findings cannot be generalised to genotypes A, B, or D. In conclusion, on-treatment HBsAgGi response is a specific surrogate marker for stratifying residual HCC risk in HBeAg-positive patients, and TAF is more effective than ETV in inducing these favourable kinetics.

Question and background
Nucleos(t)ide analog (NA) therapy suppresses hepatitis B virus (HBV) DNA, but residual hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) risk persists.
Design and population
We evaluated the impact of tenofovir alafenamide (TAF) versus entecavir (ETV) on 48-week HBsAgGi kinetics and its utility in stratifying HCC risk in 90 NA-naïve patients (ETV: 73; TAF: 17).
Core methods
As the assay targets genotype C-specific O-glycosylation, the findings cannot be generalised to genotypes A, B, or D.
Main results
TAF independently predicted a favourable response compared with ETV (penalised odds ratio 3.60, p = 0.030).
Interpretation and novelty
A significant interaction occurred between HBeAg status and HBsAgGi response in relation to HCC development (p = 0.038).
Scientific and public-health significance
Adding HBsAgGi response to the aMAP score improved long-term HCC prediction exclusively in the HBeAg-positive cohort.
Limitations and evidence strength
In HBeAg-positive patients, a poor response was associated with increased HCC risk (hazard ratio 7.34, 95% confidence interval [CI] 1.96-20.35, p = 0.001); however, the exact magnitude warrants cautious interpretation due to wide CIs.

埃塞俄比亚 12-36 个月龄儿童 乙型肝炎病毒 疫苗 的覆盖范围和决定因素:对 2016 年埃塞俄比亚人口和健康调查的分析。

Coverage and determinants of hepatitis B virus vaccine uptake among children aged 12-36 months in Ethiopia: an analysis of the 2016 Ethiopian Demographic and Health Survey.
作者： Kathleen Kenyatta White, Tesfa Sewunet Alamneh, Josephine G Walker, Aaron G Lim
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
埃塞俄比亚面临着 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (HBV) 感染的高负担 (~6%),并且尚未达到婴儿 疫苗接种 目标,因此需要评估幼儿中 HBV 疫苗 摄取的覆盖率和决定因素。
设计与对象
使用 2016 年埃塞俄比亚人口和健康调查进行横断面分析,其中包括 3,855 名 12-36 个月龄儿童的全国代表性加权样本,以评估 HBV 疫苗接种 覆盖水平及其空间分布。
核心方法
HBV 疫苗接种 覆盖率被分类为无、不完整或完全,并使用序数逻辑回归来确定相关因素,以模拟社会人口统计学和产科决定因素。
主要结果
总体而言,51.2% 的儿童接受了完整的 HBV 疫苗接种,但在埃塞俄比亚各地观察到的覆盖率水平存在显着的区域差异。
解释与创新
该研究强调,埃塞俄比亚的 HBV 疫苗接种 覆盖率仍低于世卫组织的目标,并表现出明显的地理差异,强调了有针对性的干预措施的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,优先针对社会经济地位较低和家庭人口较多的母亲开展 疫苗接种 运动可以提高埃塞俄比亚儿童的 HBV 疫苗 吸收率。
局限与证据强度
横截面设计限制了因果推断,并且对调查数据的依赖可能会引入回忆或报告偏差,需要对所确定的关联进行谨慎解释。

Coverage and determinants of hepatitis B virus vaccine uptake among children aged 12-36 months in Ethiopia: an analysis of the 2016 Ethiopian Demographic and Health Survey.

Authors: Kathleen Kenyatta White, Tesfa Sewunet Alamneh, Josephine G Walker, Aaron G Lim
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: In Ethiopia, chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) prevalence is high (∼6%) and infant vaccination targets are unmet. We assessed HBV vaccination uptake and associations with parental sociodemographic and obstetric factors among children (aged 12-36 months) in Ethiopia. METHODS: We utilised the 2016 Ethiopian Demographic and Health Survey. We included a weighted sample of 3,855 children (aged 12-36 month) to assess HBV vaccination coverage level (none, incomplete, and complete) and its spatial distribution. Additionally, we identified associated factors of HBV vaccination coverage level using ordinal logistic regression. RESULTS: Overall, 51.2% of children received complete HBV vaccination, with wide regional variation. Older maternal age (adjusted odds ratio (AOR)=1.05, 95% confidence interval (CI): 1.02-1.07), better maternal education level (mean AOR=1.66-2.08), higher household wealth quantile (mean AOR=1.51-2.32), distance to a healthcare facility not a big problem (AOR=1.54, 95% CI: 1.25-1.89), and complete maternal continuum care utilisation (AOR=2.59, 95% CI: 1.25-5.30) were positively associated with HBV vaccination coverage, while higher number of children in the household (AOR=0.86, 95% CI: 0.79-0.92) was negatively associated. CONCLUSIONS: HBV vaccination coverage falls below World Health Organization (WHO) targets in Ethiopia and has substantial regional variation. Targeted vaccination campaigns for mothers in lower socioeconomic groups and larger families may improve vaccination coverage.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Ethiopia faces a high burden of chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection (~6%) and has not met infant vaccination targets, creating a need to assess coverage and determinants of HBV vaccine uptake among young children.
Design and population
A cross-sectional analysis was conducted using the 2016 Ethiopian Demographic and Health Survey, including a nationally representative weighted sample of 3,855 children aged 12–36 months to evaluate HBV vaccination coverage levels and their spatial distribution.
Core methods
HBV vaccination coverage was categorized as none, incomplete, or complete, and associated factors were identified using ordinal logistic regression to model sociodemographic and obstetric determinants.
Main results
Overall, 51.2% of children received complete HBV vaccination, with substantial regional variation in coverage levels observed across Ethiopia.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights that HBV vaccination coverage in Ethiopia remains below WHO targets and demonstrates marked geographic disparities, underscoring the need for targeted interventions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that prioritizing vaccination campaigns for mothers with lower socioeconomic status and larger families could improve HBV vaccine uptake among children in Ethiopia.
Limitations and evidence strength
The cross-sectional design limits causal inference, and reliance on survey data may introduce recall or reporting biases, warranting cautious interpretation of the associations identified.

异质病毒环境中衣壳和适应性 免疫应答 HBV 感染的 宿主 模型内广义扩散多重延迟中的复杂动力学行为

Complex dynamics behavior in a generalized diffusive multiple delays within-host model for HBV infection with capsids and adaptive immune response in a heterogeneous viral environment
作者： Séverin Foko, Jean M-S Lubuma
摘要中文翻译摘要:在本文中,我们通过异质和同质空间中环境驱动的感染研究了新的 慢性乙型肝炎 模型的动力学。该模型包括空间扩散、五个时间延迟和通用 发病率 函数。解的适定性,包括全局解的存在性、唯一性、非负性、最终有界性,均已成立。分别计算病毒感染的繁殖数、CTL 免疫应答、抗体免疫应答、CTL免疫竞争和抗体免疫竞争。利用Lyapunov泛函和线性化方法,得到了无感染平衡全局稳定性的阈值条件;并分别建立了无免疫平衡、抗体反应、CTL反应以及抗体和CTL反应平衡的统一持久性和整体稳定性的阈值条件。空间均匀平衡的 Hopf 分岔的发生也已成立。提供了应用程序,并进行了数值模拟来说明分析结果并验证猜想。我们的研究结果揭示了体内 HBV 感染动态的丰富而复杂的模式,包括规则和不规则的周期性振荡。不规则的振荡可能意味着免疫紊乱和过敏。此外,根据数值模拟,大多数乙型肝炎感染可以逃避慢性感染。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究通过异质和同质空间中的环境驱动感染来研究 慢性乙型肝炎 模型的动力学。该研究旨在了解体内 HBV 感染动态的复杂模式。
设计与对象
该研究采用了 HBV 感染的广义扩散多重延迟 -宿主 模型,结合了空间扩散、五次时间延迟和通用 发病率 函数。它专注于异质病毒环境中的乙型肝炎病毒感染。
核心方法
解的适定性,包括全局解的存在性、唯一性、非负性和最终有界性,均已成立。该模型使用李亚普诺夫函数和线性化方法来确定全局稳定性和均衡持续性的阈值条件。
主要结果
计算病毒感染的繁殖数、CTL 免疫应答、抗体免疫应答、CTL免疫竞争和抗体免疫竞争。该研究还确定了空间均匀平衡的霍普夫分岔的发生。
解释与创新
研究结果揭示了体内乙型肝炎病毒感染动态的丰富而复杂的模式,包括规则和不规则的周期性振荡。这些波动可能意味着免疫紊乱和过敏。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果提供了对 HBV 感染动态的深入了解,并可能为 免疫应答 的未来研究和治疗 HBV 感染的治疗策略的开发提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于数学模型和数值模拟,可能无法完全捕捉现实世界乙型肝炎病毒感染动态的复杂性。证据是温和的,因为它来自理论分析和模拟。

Complex dynamics behavior in a generalized diffusive multiple delays within-host model for HBV infection with capsids and adaptive immune response in a heterogeneous viral environment

Authors: Séverin Foko, Jean M-S Lubuma
Original abstract (English)
Abstract In this paper, we investigate the dynamics of a new chronic hepatitis B model through environmental-driven infection in heterogeneous and homogenous space. The model includes spatial diffusion, five time delays, and a general incidence function. The well-posedness of solutions, including the existence of global solutions, uniqueness, non-negativity, ultimate boundedness, is established. The reproduction numbers for virus infection, CTL immune response, antibody immune response, CTL immune competition and antibody immune competition, respectively, are calculated. By employing the Lyapunov functionals and linearization methods, the threshold conditions on the global stability of the infection-free equilibrium are obtained; and the threshold conditions on the uniform persistence and the global stability of the equilibria for immune-free, antibody response, CTL response and antibody and CTL responses, respectively, are established. The occurrence of Hopf bifurcation of the spatially homogeneous equilibria is also established. An application is provided, and numerical simulations are carried out to illustrate the analytical results and verify the conjectures. Our findings reveal the rich and complex patterns of HBV infection dynamics in vivo, including regular and irregular periodic oscillations. Irregular oscillations may signify immune disorders and allergies. Also, from numerical simulations, it follows that the majority of hepatitis B infection could escape chronic infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study investigates the dynamics of chronic hepatitis B model through environmental-driven infection in heterogeneous and homogenous space. The research aims to understand the complex patterns of HBV infection dynamics in vivo.
Design and population
The study employs a generalized diffusive multiple delays within-host model for HBV infection, incorporating spatial diffusion, five time delays, and a general incidence function. It focuses on HBV infection in a heterogeneous viral environment.
Core methods
The well-posedness of solutions, including the existence of global solutions, uniqueness, non-negativity, and ultimate boundedness, is established. The model uses Lyapunov functionals and linearization methods to determine threshold conditions for global stability and persistence of equilibria.
Main results
The reproduction numbers for virus infection, CTL immune response, antibody immune response, CTL immune competition, and antibody immune competition are calculated. The study also establishes the occurrence of Hopf bifurcation of the spatially homogeneous equilibria.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings reveal rich and complex patterns of HBV infection dynamics in vivo, including regular and irregular periodic oscillations. These oscillations may signify immune disorders and allergies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide insights into the dynamics of HBV infection and may inform future studies on immune responses and the development of therapeutic strategies for managing HBV infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a mathematical model and numerical simulations, which may not fully capture the complexities of real-world HBV infection dynamics. The evidence is moderate as it is derived from theoretical analysis and simulations.

死亡率 预测 5 年内乙型肝炎相关肝硬化导致的门静脉血栓形成的新列线图。

A New Nomogram for Mortality Prediction Within 5 Years in Portal Vein Thrombosis due to Hepatitis B-Related Cirrhosis.
作者： Yang Zhou, Feixiang Xiong, Jialiang Chen, Xiaoli Liu, Peipei Meng, Tong Wu, Hao Yu, Xiaomin Ji, Yuyong Jiang, Yixin Hou
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了乙型肝炎相关肝硬化并发门静脉血栓(PVT)患者对准确长期 死亡率 预测的未满足需求,PVT 是一种预后工具有限的高风险亚组。
设计与对象
回顾性招募2015年1月至2023年12月961例乙型肝炎相关肝硬化合并PVT患者的队列研究,并形成用于模型开发的训练队列研究。
核心方法
使用 Cox 回归确定了 5 年 死亡率 的独立危险因素,并根据这些因素构建了列线图;通过 ROC 曲线、C 指数、校准曲线和决策曲线分析来评估模型性能。
主要结果
该列线图在训练 队列研究 中实现了 0.906 (95% CI: 0.891–0.921) 的 AUROC 值,在验证 队列研究 中实现了 0.910 (95% CI: 0.885–0.927) 的 AUROC 值,优于 Child–Turcotte–Pugh 和 MELD 评分。
解释与创新
作者将列线图解释为该患者群体个体化风险分层的重大进步,强调与现有评分系统相比,其区分能力有所提高。
科研与公卫意义
列线图可以通过更早地识别高危患者来加强监测或治疗干预,从而为临床决策提供信息。
局限与证据强度
单中心、回顾性设计和缺乏外部验证限制了普遍性;由于潜在的选择和混杂偏差,证据强度被评估为中等。

A New Nomogram for Mortality Prediction Within 5 Years in Portal Vein Thrombosis due to Hepatitis B-Related Cirrhosis.

Authors: Yang Zhou, Feixiang Xiong, Jialiang Chen, Xiaoli Liu, Peipei Meng, Tong Wu, Hao Yu, Xiaomin Ji, Yuyong Jiang, Yixin Hou
Original abstract (English)
This study aims to develop a nomogram model intended for predicting long-term mortality in patients suffering from hepatitis B-related cirrhosis associated with Portal vein thrombosis (PVT). A total of 961 patients were recruited from January 2015 to December 2023, forming the training cohort. We analysed independent risk factors affecting 5-year mortality through COX regression, from which we constructed a nomogram. To assess the nomogram's utility, we employed the receiver operating characteristic (ROC) curve, C-index, calibration curves, and decision curve analysis (DCA). The independent prognostic factors retained in the final model for patients with hepatitis B-related cirrhosis complicated by PVT included age (HR = 1.09, 95% CI: 1.06-1.11; p < 0.001), leukocyte count (WBC) (HR = 1.09, 95% CI: 1.03-1.17; p = 0.002), and the neutrophil-to-lymphocyte ratio (NLR) (HR = 1.29, 95% CI: 1.22-1.35; p < 0.001), along with serum sodium (HR = 0.94, 95% CI: 0.90-0.98; p = 0.005). The AUROC values for the nomogram were 0.906 (95% CI: 0.891-0.921) in the training cohort and 0.910 (95% CI: 0.885-0.927) in the validation cohort, demonstrating improved performance and discriminatory ability compared to current models, including CTP and MELD scores. Age, leukocyte count (WBC), NLR, and serum sodium emerged as independent risk factors impacting prognosis for patients with hepatitis B cirrhosis and PVT within 5 years. The nomogram developed in this research holds significant potential for accurately predicting the outcomes of these patients within 5 years.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the unmet need for accurate long-term mortality prediction in patients with hepatitis B-related cirrhosis complicated by portal vein thrombosis (PVT), a high-risk subgroup with limited prognostic tools.
Design and population
A retrospective cohort of 961 patients with hepatitis B-related cirrhosis and PVT was recruited from January 2015 to December 2023 and formed the training cohort for model development.
Core methods
Independent risk factors for 5-year mortality were identified using Cox regression, and a nomogram was constructed from these factors; model performance was assessed with ROC curves, C-index, calibration curves, and decision curve analysis.
Main results
The nomogram achieved AUROC values of 0.906 (95% CI: 0.891–0.921) in the training cohort and 0.910 (95% CI: 0.885–0.927) in the validation cohort, outperforming Child–Turcotte–Pugh and MELD scores.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the nomogram as a significant advance in individualized risk stratification for this patient group, highlighting its improved discrimination compared to existing scoring systems.
Scientific and public-health significance
The nomogram could inform clinical decision-making by enabling earlier identification of high-risk patients for intensified monitoring or therapeutic interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The single-centre, retrospective design and lack of external validation limit generalisability; the evidence strength is assessed as moderate due to potential selection and confounding biases.

类风湿关节炎与慢性乙型肝炎相关的II型冷球蛋白血症性肾小球肾炎分型:一例病例报告

Mixed Cryoglobulinaemic Glomerulonephritis Section Type II in the Setting of Rheumatoid Arthritis and Chronic Hepatitis B: A Case Report
作者： Vijay Jeyachandran, Ram Pukar Bharat, Kapil Sejpal, Manish Balwani
摘要中文翻译混合型(II 型)冷球蛋白血症是一种全身性免疫复合物血管炎,通常与慢性感染尤其是丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 或自身免疫性疾病有关。慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (HBV) 相关的冷球蛋白血症很少见,其与类风湿关节炎 (RA) 共存尤其罕见。作者报告了一名 45 岁女性,患有长期服用甲氨蝶呤且患有慢性乙型肝炎的血清阳性 RA,并出现疲劳、关节痛、腿部明显紫癜和新发水肿。实验室检查显示血清肌酐升高、肾病范围蛋白尿、低蛋白血症、血尿和类风湿因子 (RF) 阳性。抗核抗体(ANA)和抗双链脱氧核糖核酸(抗 dsDNA)均为阴性。补体水平,即成分 3 (C3) 中度降低,成分 4 (C4) 严重降低,以及具有高免疫球蛋白 M (IgM) 冷比容的阳性混合冷球蛋白。HCV 和人类免疫缺陷病毒 (HIV) 血清学结果呈阴性。肾活检显示弥漫性毛细血管内增生性膜增生性肾小球肾炎(MPGN),伴有透明“假血栓”。免疫荧光显示 IgM、免疫球蛋白 G (IgG)(κ 和 lambda)、C3 和 C1q 的颗粒状毛细血管壁沉积,与 II 型冷球蛋白血症 GN 一致。治疗 包括大剂量皮质类固醇和 HBV 抗病毒药物 药物。尽管进行了 抗病毒药物 治疗和血浆置换,肾功能衰竭仍进展需要血液透析,并且患者在三周内因疾病相关并发症和医院感染而死亡。本病例强调 RA 和 HBV 共同构成冷球蛋白血症性肾小球肾炎。使用射频、补体和冷冻比容测试以及活检引导治疗进行早期识别至关重要。紫癜、RF 阳性和 C4/C3 低应提示考虑混合性冷球蛋白血症性血管炎。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
混合性冷球蛋白血症性肾小球肾炎 (GN) 是一种全身性免疫复合物血管炎,通常与丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 等慢性感染或自身免疫性疾病相关,但其在类风湿关节炎 (RA) 和 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (HBV) 背景下的发生罕见且不常见。
设计与对象
病例报告 描述了一名 45 岁女性,患有甲氨蝶呤血清阳性 RA 和慢性乙型肝炎,表现为疲劳、关节痛、可触及的紫癜和新发水肿。该研究重点关注这一独特病例的临床表现和诊断结果。
核心方法
该患者接受了实验室检查,包括 HCV 和 HIV 血清学检测,以及肾活检以评估肾小球肾炎的组织病理学特征。免疫荧光用于识别免疫复合物沉积物。
主要结果
实验室检查显示血清肌酐升高、肾病范围蛋白尿、低蛋白血症、血尿和类风湿因子 (RF) 阳性。补体水平中度降低(成分 3)和严重降低(成分 4),混合冷球蛋白呈阳性,免疫球蛋白 M (IgM) 冷容比较高。肾活检显示弥漫性毛细血管内增殖性、膜增殖性肾小球肾炎 (MPGN) 伴透明假血栓,免疫荧光证实 IgM、IgG(κ 和 lambda)、C3 和 C1q 颗粒状毛细血管壁沉积。
解释与创新
该病例强调 RA 和 HBV 可以共存并促进混合性冷球蛋白血症 GN 的发展,这是一种罕见且不寻常的关联。研究结果表明,对于患有重叠的自身免疫性疾病和病毒性疾病的患者,考虑这种情况的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
该病例强调了早期识别 RA 和 HBV 患者混合性冷球蛋白血管炎的必要性,强调了 RF、补体和冷比容测试以及活检引导治疗在管理此类复杂临床情况中的作用。
局限与证据强度
作为一项 病例报告,该研究缺乏统计能力和对照组来建立因果关系或概括结果。证据仅限于单个患者病例,它提供了描述性见解,但不支持更广泛的结论。

Mixed Cryoglobulinaemic Glomerulonephritis Section Type II in the Setting of Rheumatoid Arthritis and Chronic Hepatitis B: A Case Report

Authors: Vijay Jeyachandran, Ram Pukar Bharat, Kapil Sejpal, Manish Balwani
Original abstract (English)
Mixed (Type II) cryoglobulinaemia is a systemic immune-complex vasculitis often linked to chronic infections especially Hepatitis C Virus (HCV) or autoimmune disease. Chronic Hepatitis B Virus (HBV) associated cryoglobulinaemic is rare, and its coexistence with Rheumatoid Arthritis (RA) is especially unusual. The authors report a 45-year-old female with long-standing seropositive RA on methotrexate and chronic HBV presented with fatigue, arthralgias, palpable purpura on her legs, and new-onset oedema. Laboratory workup showed elevated serum creatinine, nephrotic-range proteinuria, hypoalbuminaemia, haematuria, and a positive Rheumatoid Factor (RF). Antinuclear Antibody (ANA) and Anti-double-stranded deoxyribonucleic acid (anti-dsDNA) were negative. Complement levels i.e., Component 3 (C3) moderately reduced, Component 4 (C4) severely low, and positive mixed cryoglobulins with a high Immunoglobulin M (IgM) cryocrit. HCV and Human Immunodeficiency Virus (HIV) serologies were negative. Renal biopsy showed a diffuse endocapillary proliferative, Membranoproliferative Glomerulonephritis (MPGN) with hyaline “pseudothrombi.” Immunofluorescence disclosed granular capillary wall deposits of IgM, Immunoglobulin G (IgG) (both kappa and lambda), C3, and C1q, consistent with Type II cryoglobulinaemic GN. Treatment included high-dose corticosteroids and HBV antiviral drugs. Despite antiviral therapy and plasmapheresis, renal failure progressed requiring haemodialysis, and the patient died within three weeks due to disease-related complications and nosocomial infection. The present case highlights that RA and HBV can together underlie cryoglobulinaemic GN. Early recognition using RF, complement, and cryocrit testing and biopsy-guided therapy are critical. Purpura, positive RF, and low C4/C3 should prompt consideration of mixed cryoglobulinaemic vasculitis.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Mixed cryoglobulinaemic glomerulonephritis (GN) is a systemic immune-complex vasculitis often associated with chronic infections like Hepatitis C Virus (HCV) or autoimmune diseases, but its occurrence in the context of Rheumatoid Arthritis (RA) and Chronic Hepatitis B Virus (HBV) is rare and unusual.
Design and population
This case report describes a 45-year-old female with seropositive RA on methotrexate and chronic HBV who presented with fatigue, arthralgias, palpable purpura, and new-onset oedema. The study focuses on the clinical presentation and diagnostic findings of this unique case.
Core methods
The patient underwent laboratory workup, including serological testing for HCV and HIV, and a renal biopsy to assess the histopathological features of the glomerulonephritis. Immunofluorescence was used to identify immune complex deposits.
Main results
Laboratory tests revealed elevated serum creatinine, nephrotic-range proteinuria, hypoalbuminaemia, haematuria, and a positive Rheumatoid Factor (RF). Complement levels were moderately reduced (Component 3) and severely low (Component 4), with positive mixed cryoglobulins and a high Immunoglobulin M (IgM) cryocrit. Renal biopsy showed diffuse endocapillary proliferative, membranoproliferative glomerulonephritis (MPGN) with hyaline pseudothrombi, and immunofluorescence confirmed granular capillary wall deposits of IgM, IgG (both kappa and lambda), C3, and C1q.
Interpretation and novelty
The case highlights that RA and HBV can coexist and contribute to the development of mixed cryoglobulinaemic GN, which is a rare and unusual association. The findings suggest the importance of considering this condition in patients with overlapping autoimmune and viral diseases.
Scientific and public-health significance
The case underscores the need for early recognition of mixed cryoglobulinaemic vasculitis in patients with RA and HBV, emphasizing the role of RF, complement, and cryocrit testing, as well as biopsy-guided therapy, in managing such complex clinical scenarios.
Limitations and evidence strength
As a case report, the study lacks the statistical power and control groups to establish causality or generalize findings. The evidence is limited to a single patient case, which provides a descriptive insight but does not support broader conclusions.

在赞比亚处理 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒感染的一线医护人员中验证肝炎护理点超声检查。

Validation of a Point-Of-Care Ultrasound for Hepatitis Among Front-Line Healthcare Workers Managing Chronic Hepatitis B Virus Infection in Zambia.
作者： Costanza Bertoni, Annie Kanunga, Edford Sinkala, Enock Syabbalo, Helen Chitundu, Alick Chikwezi, Carolyn Chibundi, Guy K Muula, Samuel Bosomprah, Nicolò Capra
摘要中文翻译为弥合资源有限环境中乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染患者护理实施中的差距,我们评估了一种新型肝脏床旁超声(POCUS)肝炎筛查方案(PUSH)在赞比亚一线非放射科医护人员中的准确性。在2024年3月至6月期间,于赞比亚卢萨卡大学教学医院,我们培训了4名护士和6名有HBV管理经验但无超声(US)经验的医生,采用PUSH方案,包括在三个窗口(上腹部、肋下和右侧肋间)观察肝脏,以识别提示肝硬化的特征及疑似肝细胞癌(HCC)的肝脏病变。随后,连续纳入仅患有乙型肝炎(PWHB)或HBV/HIV合并感染的成年参与者,进行PUSH检查,操作者对临床数据不知情。我们使用瞬时弹性成像(TE)作为参考标准(RST)评估PUSH对显著纤维化和肝硬化的准确性,并使用经验丰富放射技师进行的全面腹部超声作为可能HCC肝脏病变的RST。采用非参数法分析PUSH的受试者工作特征曲线(ROC),并按操作者类型(医生vs护士)、性别和HIV状态进行调整。在197名分析的PWHB中,69.5%正在接受HBV抗病毒药物治疗,27.9%为HBV/HIV合并感染。根据RST结果,17.7%的PWHB存在显著纤维化,11.6%存在肝硬化,3.5%存在肝脏病变。PUSH对显著纤维化的准确性较低,敏感性为8.3%(17.5-41.4),特异性为100.0%(96.1-100),曲线下面积(AUROC)为0.54(0.43-0.65);对肝硬化的准确性为中等,敏感性为60.9%(38.5-80.3),特异性为97.7%(94.2-99.4),AUROC为0.79(0.69-0.89);对肝脏病变的准确性较高,敏感性为85.7%(42.1-99.6),特异性为98.4%(95.5-99.7),AUROC为0.92(0.78-1.00)。在赞比亚,经过快速培训的前线护士和医生能够使用PUSH以中等准确性诊断肝硬化,以高准确性诊断肝脏病变,尽管性能估计的置信区间较宽。在缺乏实验室系统和TE的情况下,包含PUSH的肝脏POCUS可能有助于HBV管理。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究通过评估用于诊断赞比亚一线医护人员中 HBV 相关肝病的新型护理点超声 (POCUS) 方案 (PUSH),解决了在资源有限的环境中改善 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 护理的需要。
设计与对象
2024 年 3 月至 6 月,在赞比亚卢萨卡的大学教学医院,四名护士和六名具有乙肝管理经验但没有接受过超声培训的医生接受了 PUSH 方案培训,该方案在三个窗口中评估肝硬化提示特征和肝细胞癌 (HCC) 病变。
核心方法
单独感染 慢性乙型肝炎 (PWHB) 或 HBV/HIV 合并感染的连续成人参与者在不了解临床数据的操作员的情况下接受 PUSH,并使用瞬时弹性成像 (TE) 作为纤维化和肝硬化的参考标准以及由经验丰富的肝脏病变放射技师进行综合腹部超声评估准确性。
主要结果
197名感染者中,17.7%有明显纤维化,11.6%有肝硬化,3.5%有肝脏病变;PUSH 对显着纤维化的准确性较低(敏感性 8.3%,特异性 100.0%,AUROC 0.54),对肝硬化的准确性中等(敏感性 60.9%,特异性 97.7%,AUROC 0.79),对肝脏病变的准确性较高(敏感性 85.7%,特异性 98.4%,AUROC 0.92)。
解释与创新
作者解释说,经过快速培训后,PUSH 可以以中等准确度诊断肝硬化,并以较高准确度诊断肝脏病变,这突显了其在缺乏实验室系统和 TE 的 HBV 管理中的潜在用途。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,PUSH 可以通过使一线医护人员识别肝硬化和 HCC 病变来支持资源有限环境中的 HBV 护理,从而有可能改善早期 诊断 和转诊途径。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括表现估计的置信区间较宽,以及对单中心、短期训练的依赖,这可能会限制准确性指标的普遍性和精度。

Validation of a Point-Of-Care Ultrasound for Hepatitis Among Front-Line Healthcare Workers Managing Chronic Hepatitis B Virus Infection in Zambia.

Authors: Costanza Bertoni, Annie Kanunga, Edford Sinkala, Enock Syabbalo, Helen Chitundu, Alick Chikwezi, Carolyn Chibundi, Guy K Muula, Samuel Bosomprah, Nicolò Capra
Original abstract (English)
To close gaps in implementing care of people with hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection in resource-limited settings, we evaluated the accuracy of a novel liver point-of-care ultrasound (POCUS) protocol for hepatitis (PUSH) among front-line non-radiology healthcare workers in Zambia. At University Teaching Hospital in Lusaka, Zambia, from March to June 2024, we trained four nurses and six physicians with experience in HBV management, but not in ultrasound (US), in the PUSH protocol, which includes visualizing the liver in three windows (epigastric, subcostal and right transcostal) to identify cirrhosis-suggestive features and liver lesions suspicious for hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC). Then, consecutive adult participants with chronic hepatitis B (PWHB) alone or HBV/HIV coinfection underwent PUSH with operators blinded to clinical data. We evaluated the accuracy of PUSH for significant fibrosis and cirrhosis using transient elastography (TE) as the reference standard test (RST) and for liver lesions that were possible HCC using a comprehensive abdominal ultrasound by an experienced radiographer as the RST. Nonparametric analysis of the receiver operating curve (ROC) for PUSH was adjusted for operator type (doctor vs. nurse), sex and HIV status. Among 197 PWHBs analysed, 69.5% were taking HBV antivirals and 27.9% had HBV/HIV. According to RSTs, 17.7% of PWHBs had significant fibrosis, 11.6% had cirrhosis and 3.5% had liver lesions. PUSH had low accuracy for significant fibrosis, with sensitivity of 8.3% (17.5-41.4), specificity of 100.0% (96.1-100) and an area under the curve (AUROC) of 0.54 (0.43-0.65), moderate accuracy for cirrhosis, with sensitivity of 60.9% (38.5-80.3), specificity of 97.7% (94.2-99.4) and AUROC of 0.79 (0.69-0.89), and higher accuracy for liver lesions, with 85.7% (42.1-99.6) sensitivity, 98.4% (95.5-99.7) specificity, AUROC of 0.92 (0.78-1.00). After an expedited training, front-line nurses and physicians treating HBV in Zambia were able to use PUSH to diagnose cirrhosis with moderate accuracy and liver lesions with high accuracy, although confidence intervals around performance estimates were wide. Liver POCUS including PUSH may be useful in HBV management when laboratory systems and TE are lacking.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the need to improve hepatitis B virus (HBV) care in resource-limited settings by evaluating a novel point-of-care ultrasound (POCUS) protocol (PUSH) for diagnosing HBV-related liver disease among front-line healthcare workers in Zambia.
Design and population
At University Teaching Hospital in Lusaka, Zambia, from March to June 2024, four nurses and six physicians with HBV management experience but no ultrasound training were trained in the PUSH protocol, which assesses liver cirrhosis-suggestive features and hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) lesions in three windows.
Core methods
Consecutive adult participants with chronic hepatitis B (PWHB) alone or HBV/HIV coinfection underwent PUSH with operators blinded to clinical data, and accuracy was evaluated using transient elastography (TE) as the reference standard for fibrosis and cirrhosis and comprehensive abdominal ultrasound by an experienced radiographer for liver lesions.
Main results
Among 197 PWHBs, 17.7% had significant fibrosis, 11.6% had cirrhosis, and 3.5% had liver lesions; PUSH showed low accuracy for significant fibrosis (sensitivity 8.3%, specificity 100.0%, AUROC 0.54), moderate accuracy for cirrhosis (sensitivity 60.9%, specificity 97.7%, AUROC 0.79), and high accuracy for liver lesions (sensitivity 85.7%, specificity 98.4%, AUROC 0.92).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that PUSH can diagnose cirrhosis with moderate accuracy and liver lesions with high accuracy after expedited training, highlighting its potential utility in HBV management where laboratory systems and TE are lacking.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that PUSH could support HBV care in resource-limited settings by enabling front-line healthcare workers to identify cirrhosis and HCC lesions, potentially improving early diagnosis and referral pathways.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s limitations include wide confidence intervals around performance estimates and reliance on a single-center, short-duration training, which may limit generalisability and precision of accuracy metrics.

孟加拉国肝硬化:病因学、并发症和管理差距的系统评价

Liver cirrhosis in Bangladesh: a systematic review of etiology, complications and management gaps
作者： M. Sharmin Akther, Bismoy Saha, M. Toufique Khan, Mahbuba Yeasmin, Solaiman Pramanik
摘要中文翻译肝硬化是孟加拉国发病率和 死亡率 的一个重要且日益严重的原因,反映出低收入和中等收入国家慢性肝病负担日益增加。本系统综述旨在评估孟加拉国目前肝硬化的病因模式,总结其主要并发症和临床结果,并确定预防和管理方面的关键差距。从数据库建立到 2025 年 8 月 29 日,在 MEDLINE/PubMed、Scopus、Web of Science、Google Scholar 和 BanglaJOL 上进行了全面的文献检索。符合条件的研究包括涉及患有慢性肝病或肝硬化的孟加拉国人群的观察性研究、临床试验和国家报告。研究选择、数据提取和质量评估遵循 PRISMA 2020 指南。由于方法论上存在很大的异质性,研究结果以叙述方式综合。在 1,847 项已识别记录中,62 项研究符合资格标准。大多数是来自达卡三级中心的单中心医院横断面研究。乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 仍然是主要病因,占肝硬化病例的 30-60%,而丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 占 5-20%。最近的荟萃分析表明,普通人群中的 HBV 患病率 合并率为 4.0%(95% CI:3.0-5.1%)。非酒精性脂肪肝 (NAFLD/MASLD) 已成为一个迅速增加的病因,成人中 患病率 的比例为 25-35%,在具有代谢危险因素的个体中上升至 50% 以上。卫生系统面临的主要挑战包括 HBV/HCV 筛查不足、内窥镜检查和肝移植的机会有限、肝细胞癌 (HCC) 监测 不足以及缺乏结构化的 NAFLD/MASLD 管理途径。孟加拉国正在经历肝硬化从病毒性病因到代谢性病因的流行病学转变,因此需要加强病毒性肝炎控制、扩大代谢性疾病预防、改善专家服务以及建立标准化国家 监测 规划。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
肝硬化是孟加拉国发病率和 死亡率 的一个重要且日益严重的原因,反映出低收入和中等收入国家慢性肝病负担日益增加。
设计与对象
本系统综述旨在评估孟加拉国目前肝硬化的病因模式,总结其主要并发症和临床结果,并确定预防和管理方面的关键差距。
核心方法
研究选择、数据提取和质量评估遵循 PRISMA 2020 指南。
主要结果
在 1,847 项已识别记录中,62 项研究符合资格标准。大多数是来自达卡三级中心的单中心医院横断面研究。
解释与创新
乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 仍然是主要病因,占肝硬化病例的 30-60%,而丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 占 5-20%。
科研与公卫意义
孟加拉国正在经历肝硬化从病毒性病因到代谢性病因的流行病学转变,因此需要加强病毒性肝炎控制、扩大代谢性疾病预防、改善专家服务以及建立标准化国家 监测 规划。
局限与证据强度
由于方法论上存在很大的异质性,研究结果以叙述方式综合。

Liver cirrhosis in Bangladesh: a systematic review of etiology, complications and management gaps

Authors: M. Sharmin Akther, Bismoy Saha, M. Toufique Khan, Mahbuba Yeasmin, Solaiman Pramanik
Original abstract (English)
Liver cirrhosis represents a significant and growing cause of morbidity and mortality in Bangladesh, reflecting the increasing burden of chronic liver diseases in low- and middle-income countries. This systematic review aims to evaluate the current etiological patterns of cirrhosis in Bangladesh, summarize its major complications and clinical outcomes, and identify critical gaps in prevention and management. A comprehensive literature search was conducted across MEDLINE/PubMed, Scopus, Web of Science, Google Scholar, and BanglaJOL from database inception to August 29, 2025. Eligible studies included observational studies, clinical trials, and national reports involving Bangladeshi populations with chronic liver disease or cirrhosis. Study selection, data extraction, and quality assessment followed PRISMA 2020 guidelines. Due to substantial methodological heterogeneity, findings were synthesized narratively. Of 1,847 identified records, 62 studies met eligibility criteria. Most were single-center hospital-based cross-sectional studies from tertiary centers in Dhaka. Hepatitis B virus (HBV) remains the predominant etiology, accounting for 30-60% of cirrhosis cases, while hepatitis C virus (HCV) contributes 5-20%. Recent meta-analyses indicate a pooled HBV prevalence of 4.0% (95% CI: 3.0-5.1%) in the general population. Non-alcoholic fatty liver disease (NAFLD/MASLD) has emerged as a rapidly increasing cause, with community prevalence of 25-35% among adults, rising to over 50% in individuals with metabolic risk factors. Major health system challenges include inadequate HBV/HCV screening, limited access to endoscopy and liver transplantation, insufficient hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) surveillance, and absence of structured NAFLD/MASLD management pathways. Bangladesh is experiencing an epidemiological transition from viral to metabolic causes of cirrhosis, necessitating strengthened viral hepatitis control, expanded metabolic disease prevention, improved specialist services, and establishment of standardized national surveillance programs.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Liver cirrhosis represents a significant and growing cause of morbidity and mortality in Bangladesh, reflecting the increasing burden of chronic liver diseases in low- and middle-income countries.
Design and population

Core methods
Study selection, data extraction, and quality assessment followed PRISMA 2020 guidelines.
Main results
Of 1,847 identified records, 62 studies met eligibility criteria. Most were single-center hospital-based cross-sectional studies from tertiary centers in Dhaka.
Interpretation and novelty
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) remains the predominant etiology, accounting for 30-60% of cirrhosis cases, while hepatitis C virus (HCV) contributes 5-20%.
Scientific and public-health significance
Bangladesh is experiencing an epidemiological transition from viral to metabolic causes of cirrhosis, necessitating strengthened viral hepatitis control, expanded metabolic disease prevention, improved specialist services, and establishment of standardized national surveillance programs.
Limitations and evidence strength
Due to substantial methodological heterogeneity, findings were synthesized narratively.

功能性 FOXA2 3’UTR 变异 rs1055080 与 HBV 相关肝细胞癌风险和术后预后相关

Functional FOXA2 3’UTR variant rs1055080 is associated with HBV-related hepatocellular carcinoma risk and postoperative prognosis
作者： Pei-Gen Zhao, Yi-Fan Chen, Wenbin Liu, Wen-Wen Wang, Xu-Dong Cha, Jian-Rong Yin, Jun Zhao, Guangwen Cao
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染是肝细胞癌发生的主要驱动因素。尽管转录因子FOXA2已知可调控肝脏发育与代谢,但其遗传易感性对HBV相关肝细胞癌(HBV-HCC)的影响尚不清楚。本研究在中国4,921名参与者中开展了一项病例对照分析。对FOXA2中的两个候选功能性单核苷酸多态性(SNP)(启动子区域的rs1209523与3’UTR区域的rs1055080)进行基因分型。通过双荧光素酶报告基因检测评估FOXA2 SNP的功能。在367例HBV-HCC术后患者队列中评估FOXA2 SNP的预后价值。rs1055080-T等位基因频率与HBV-HCC发生风险显著降低相关。该关联在HBV-HCC患者与无症状HBV携带者及乙型肝炎病毒感染者中均显著。在报告基因检测中,rs1055080-T较rs1055080-C显示出更高的FOXA2 3’UTR报告基因活性。携带rs1055080-C等位基因的报告基因构建体较携带rs1055080-T者更易被miR-328-3p或miR-3615抑制。rs1055080-T与HBV-HCC发生风险显著相关的HBV变异频率降低相关。携带rs1055080-C构建体的报告基因活性在A1762T/G1764A存在时降低。rs1055080-CT+TT基因型与HBV-HCC患者总体生存期改善相关。研究结果提示,FOXA2 3’UTR变异可能是HBV感染者风险分层与预后评估的候选生殖系标志物。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
肝癌发生主要由 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (HBV) 感染驱动。尽管转录因子 FOXA2 已知可调节肝脏发育和代谢,但其遗传易感性对 HBV 相关肝细胞癌 (HBV-HCC) 的影响仍不清楚。
设计与对象
我们对 4,921 名中国参与者进行了病例对照分析。FOXA2 SNP 的预后价值在一项针对 367 名 HBV-HCC 患者的术后 队列研究 研究中进行了评估。
核心方法
对 FOXA2 中的两个候选功能性单核苷酸多态性 (SNP)(启动子区域的 rs1209523 和 3’UTR 区域的 rs1055080)进行了基因分型。通过双荧光素酶报告基因测定评估 FOXA2 SNP 的功能。
主要结果
rs1055080-T 等位基因的频率与 HBV-HCC 风险降低显着相关。当 HBV-HCC 患者与无症状 HBV 携带者和 慢性乙型肝炎 患者进行比较时,这种关联很明显。在报告基因检测中,rs1055080-T 显示出比 rs1055080-C 更高的 FOXA2 3’UTR 报告基因活性。
解释与创新
这些发现表明 FOXA2 3'UTR 变异是 HBV 感染受试者风险分层和预后评估的候选种系标记。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能有助于开发用于 HBV 感染者风险评估和预后的遗传标记,并可能有助于个性化 治疗 策略。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计或证据强度的具体限制。

Functional FOXA2 3’UTR variant rs1055080 is associated with HBV-related hepatocellular carcinoma risk and postoperative prognosis

Authors: Pei-Gen Zhao, Yi-Fan Chen, Wenbin Liu, Wen-Wen Wang, Xu-Dong Cha, Jian-Rong Yin, Jun Zhao, Guangwen Cao
Original abstract (English)
Hepatocarcinogenesis is predominantly driven by chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection. Although the transcription factor FOXA2 is known to regulate hepatic development and metabolism, the impact of its genetic predisposition on HBV-related hepatocellular carcinoma (HBV-HCC) remains unclear. We performed a case-control analysis in 4,921 Chinese participants. Two candidate functional single nucleotide polymorphisms (SNPs) (rs1209523 at the promoter region and rs1055080 at the 3’UTR region) in FOXA2 were genotyped. The functions of FOXA2 SNPs were evaluated by dual-luciferase reporter assays. The prognostic values of FOXA2 SNPs were evaluated in a postoperative cohort study of 367 patients with HBV-HCC. The frequency of the rs1055080-T allele was significantly associated with reduced risk of HBV-HCC. This association was evident when HBV-HCC patients were compared with asymptomatic HBV carriers and patients with chronic hepatitis B. In reporter assays, rs1055080-T showed higher FOXA2 3’UTR reporter activity than rs1055080-C. Reporter constructs carrying the rs1055080-C allele showed stronger repression by miR-328-3p or miR-3615 than those carrying rs1055080-T. rs1055080-T was significantly associated with lower frequencies of HCC-risk HBV mutations. Reporter activity of the rs1055080-C construct decreased in the presence of A1762T/G1764A. rs1055080-CT + TT was associated with better overall survival in HBV-HCC. These findings suggest that the FOXA2 3’UTR variant is a candidate germline marker for risk stratification and prognostic assessment in HBV-infected subjects.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatocarcinogenesis is predominantly driven by chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection. Although the transcription factor FOXA2 is known to regulate hepatic development and metabolism, the impact of its genetic predisposition on HBV-related hepatocellular carcinoma (HBV-HCC) remains unclear.
Design and population
We performed a case-control analysis in 4,921 Chinese participants. The prognostic values of FOXA2 SNPs were evaluated in a postoperative cohort study of 367 patients with HBV-HCC.
Core methods

Main results
The frequency of the rs1055080-T allele was significantly associated with reduced risk of HBV-HCC. This association was evident when HBV-HCC patients were compared with asymptomatic HBV carriers and patients with chronic hepatitis B. In reporter assays, rs1055080-T showed higher FOXA2 3’UTR reporter activity than rs1055080-C.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings suggest that the FOXA2 3’UTR variant is a candidate germline marker for risk stratification and prognostic assessment in HBV-infected subjects.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the development of genetic markers for risk assessment and prognosis in HBV-infected individuals, potentially aiding in personalized treatment strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations to the study design or evidence strength.

2011 年至 2021 年英国丁型肝炎病毒的特征。

Characterising Hepatitis D Virus in the UK From 2011 to 2021.
作者： Simmons R, Ijaz S, Jackson R, Jack K, Mulongeni R, Raghu R, Riddell A, Elsharkawy A, Uriel A, Koffas A
摘要中文翻译在英国,人们对 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 和丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 的双重感染知之甚少。建议对所有 HBV 感染者进行 HDV 筛查,因为合并感染会导致疾病进展加快。利用英国 11 个报告进行 HDV 检测的实验室共享的 HDV 检测数据以及全国 HBV 检测数据,我们建立了 HDV 检测个体的 队列研究,调查检测途径,描述 HDV-RNA 阳性的特征和结果,为检测、诊断 和护理联系的针对性干预措施提供信息。我们收集了 HDV-RNA 阳性个体的人口统计信息、临床评估、治疗 和 NHS 信托机构的最新实验室结果,并通过与国家医疗保健数据集的链接来收集。2011 年至 2021 年间,英格兰新诊断出的 HBV 患者中有 42% 与 HDV 检测有关。抗 HDV 阳性率为 5.0%,55.8% 进行过 HDV-RNA 检测,45.7% 曾经呈 HDV-RNA 阳性。在没有抗 HDV 结果的情况下,HDV-RNA 阳性率为 6.4%。在 HDV-RNA 阳性的个体中,94.3% 为非英国出生,报告有 24 种不同的主要语言,49% 有 治疗 证据,48% 有肝硬化证据。10%的人死于肝病,其中58.4%死于肝病。总之,我们的研究结果表明,在英国,针对 HBV 阳性个体的 HDV 检测相对于检测指南(包括抗 HDV 阳性者的 RNA 检测)来说并不是最佳的。需要做大量的工作来改善患者护理途径,以确保患者得到适当的诊断、联系和保留护理,并有足够的机会获得 治疗。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
在英国,人们对 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 和丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 的双重感染知之甚少。
设计与对象
利用英国 11 个报告进行 HDV 检测的实验室共享的 HDV 检测数据以及全国 HBV 检测数据,我们建立了 HDV 检测个体的 队列研究,调查检测途径,描述 HDV-RNA 阳性的特征和结果,为检测、诊断 和护理联系的针对性干预措施提供信息。
核心方法
我们收集了 HDV-RNA 阳性个体的人口统计信息、临床评估、治疗 和 NHS 信托机构的最新实验室结果,并通过与国家医疗保健数据集的链接来收集。
主要结果
2011 年至 2021 年间,英格兰新诊断出的 HBV 患者中有 42% 与 HDV 检测有关。抗 HDV 阳性率为 5.0%,55.8% 进行过 HDV-RNA 检测,45.7% 曾经呈 HDV-RNA 阳性。在没有抗 HDV 结果的情况下,HDV-RNA 阳性率为 6.4%。
解释与创新
总之,我们的研究结果表明,在英国,针对 HBV 阳性个体的 HDV 检测相对于检测指南(包括抗 HDV 阳性者的 RNA 检测)来说并不是最佳的。
科研与公卫意义
需要做大量的工作来改善患者护理途径,以确保患者得到适当的诊断、联系和保留护理,并有足够的机会获得 治疗。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的局限性。

Characterising Hepatitis D Virus in the UK From 2011 to 2021.

Authors: Simmons R, Ijaz S, Jackson R, Jack K, Mulongeni R, Raghu R, Riddell A, Elsharkawy A, Uriel A, Koffas A
Original abstract (English)
Little is known about hepatitis B virus (HBV) and Hepatitis D virus (HDV) coinfection in the UK. HDV screening is recommended among all individuals with HBV, with coinfection associated with increased disease progression. Using data on HDV testing, shared by 11 laboratories in the UK who reported undertaking HDV testing, and national testing data for HBV we established a cohort of individuals tested for HDV, investigating testing pathways, describing characteristics and outcomes of those HDV-RNA positive to inform targeted interventions for testing, diagnosis and linkage to care. Demographic information, clinical assessments, treatment and recent laboratory results from NHS trusts, and through linkage to national healthcare datasets was collected for HDV-RNA positive individuals. Between 2011 and 2021, 42% of individuals newly diagnosed with HBV in England were linked to an HDV test. Anti-HDV positivity was 5.0%, 55.8% were HDV-RNA tested and 45.7% were ever HDV-RNA positive. HDV-RNA positivity in the absence of an anti-HDV result was 6.4%. Among individuals who were HDV-RNA positive, 94.3% were non-UK born, with 24 different primary languages reported, 49% had evidence of treatment, and 48% had evidence of cirrhosis. 10% had died of which 58.4% died of liver disease. In conclusion, our findings indicate that HDV testing in HBV positive individuals in the UK is sub-optimal against testing guidelines, including RNA testing among those who are anti-HDV positive. Considerable work is needed to improve the patient care pathway, to ensure patients are appropriately diagnosed, linked and retained in care, with adequate access to treatment.

乙型肝炎表面抗原直接改变树突状细胞介导的自然杀伤细胞反应。

Hepatitis B surface antigen directly alters Dendritic cell-mediated Natural Killer cell responses.
作者： Lucile Dumolard, Marie-Noelle Hilleret, Mylene Pezet, Thomas Decaens, Philippe Saas, Evelyne Jouvin-Marche, Patrice N Marche, Caroline Aspord, Zuzana Macek Jilkova
摘要中文翻译背景和目的:由 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 引起的乙型肝炎是一个重大的全球健康问题,通常会导致无法安装有效 免疫应答 的个体出现 免疫应答。慢性乙型肝炎会大大增加患严重肝脏疾病的风险,包括肝硬化和肝细胞癌。树突状细胞 (DC) 和自然杀伤 (NK) 细胞在 免疫应答 感染 HBV 的早期过程中发挥着至关重要的作用,但它们的功能在 慢性乙型肝炎 患者中受到损害。我们研究了乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 如何影响 DC 亚群和 NK 细胞之间的相互作用。方法:将血源性 DC 亚群预先暴露于 HBsAg,然后在各种刺激条件下与 NK 细胞共培养。随后我们评估了 DC 的调节以及 NK 细胞的表型和功能反应。同样,DC 亚群暴露于具有不同水平 HBsAg 的 HBV 感染者的血清或直接从 HBV 患者中纯化的血清,以评估其刺激 NK 细胞反应的能力。结果:我们的研究结果表明,HBsAg 通过 Toll 样受体 (TLR)7/8 依赖性途径破坏 cDC2 介导的 NK 细胞活化,并通过 TLR3 依赖性机制破坏 cDC1 介导的 NK 细胞脱颗粒。pDC 暴露于 HBsAg 会通过 TLR9 依赖性途径改变 NK 细胞活化、表型和脱颗粒。在以 HBsAg 依赖性方式暴露于 HBV 感染患者的血清后,会重现这种损害。一致地,慢性 HBV 感染患者的循环 HBsAg 与 pDC 介导的 NK 细胞毒性受损有关。结论:这些结果揭示了 HBsAg 调节 pDC-NK 细胞相互作用的关键机制,揭示了 HBV 如何逃避先天 免疫应答 并导致免疫功能障碍。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
由 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 引起的乙型肝炎是一个重大的全球健康问题,通常会导致无法安装有效 免疫应答 的个体出现 免疫应答。
设计与对象
慢性乙型肝炎会大大增加患严重肝脏疾病的风险,包括肝硬化和肝细胞癌。树突状细胞 (DC) 和自然杀伤 (NK) 细胞在 免疫应答 感染 HBV 的早期过程中发挥着至关重要的作用,但它们的功能在 慢性乙型肝炎 患者中受到损害。
核心方法
血液来源的 DC 亚群预先暴露于 HBsAg,然后在各种刺激条件下与 NK 细胞共培养。随后我们评估了 DC 的调节以及 NK 细胞的表型和功能反应。同样,DC 亚群暴露于具有不同水平 HBsAg 的 HBV 感染者的血清或直接从 HBV 患者中纯化的血清,以评估其刺激 NK 细胞反应的能力。
主要结果
我们的研究结果表明,HBsAg 通过 Toll 样受体 (TLR)7/8 依赖性途径破坏 cDC2 介导的 NK 细胞活化,并通过 TLR3 依赖性机制破坏 cDC1 介导的 NK 细胞脱颗粒。pDC 暴露于 HBsAg 会通过 TLR9 依赖性途径改变 NK 细胞活化、表型和脱颗粒。在以 HBsAg 依赖性方式暴露于 HBV 感染患者的血清后,会重现这种损害。一致地,慢性 HBV 感染患者的循环 HBsAg 与 pDC 介导的 NK 细胞毒性受损有关。
解释与创新
这些结果揭示了 HBsAg 调节 pDC-NK 细胞相互作用的关键机制,揭示了 HBV 如何逃避先天 免疫应答 并导致免疫功能障碍。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为了解乙型肝炎免疫逃避和免疫功能障碍的机制提供了见解,这可以为未来免疫调节的研究和慢性乙型肝炎感染治疗策略的开发提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于体外实验,未考虑体内复杂性,可能限制研究结果的外推性。但证据较为充分,因其直接关联了乙型肝炎病毒表面抗原(HBsAg)与特定免疫细胞的相互作用及功能损伤。

Hepatitis B surface antigen directly alters Dendritic cell-mediated Natural Killer cell responses.

Authors: Lucile Dumolard, Marie-Noelle Hilleret, Mylene Pezet, Thomas Decaens, Philippe Saas, Evelyne Jouvin-Marche, Patrice N Marche, Caroline Aspord, Zuzana Macek Jilkova
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND & AIMS: Hepatitis B, caused by the Hepatitis B virus (HBV), is a significant global health concern, often leading to chronic hepatitis B in individuals unable to mount an effective immune response. Chronic HBV substantially increases the risk of severe liver diseases, including cirrhosis and hepatocellular carcinoma. Dendritic cells (DC) and Natural killer (NK) cells play crucial roles in the early immune response to HBV, but their function is compromised in chronic hepatitis B patients. We investigated how Hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) influences the interplay between DC subpopulations and NK cells. METHODS: Blood-derived DC subsets were pre-exposed to HBsAg and then co-cultured with NK cells under various stimulation conditions. We subsequently evaluated the modulations of DC, and the phenotypic and functional responses of NK cells. Similarly, DC subsets were exposed to serum from HBV-infected individuals with varying levels of HBsAg or directly purified from HBV patients to assess their ability to stimulate NK cell responses. RESULTS: Our findings show that HBsAg disrupts cDC2-mediated NK cell activation through Toll-like receptor (TLR)7/8-dependent pathways and cDC1-mediated NK cell degranulation via TLR3-dependent mechanisms. Exposure of pDC to HBsAg alters NK cell activation, phenotype and degranulation through TLR9-dependent pathways. This impairment was recapitulated following exposure to serum from HBV-infected patients in an HBsAg-dependent manner. Consistently, circulating HBsAg in patients with chronic HBV infection was associated with impaired pDC-mediated NK cell cytotoxicity. CONCLUSIONS: These results uncover key mechanisms by which HBsAg modulates pDC-NK cell interactions, shedding light on how HBV evades innate immune responses and contributes to immune dysfunction.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B, caused by the Hepatitis B virus (HBV), is a significant global health concern, often leading to chronic hepatitis B in individuals unable to mount an effective immune response.
Design and population
Chronic HBV substantially increases the risk of severe liver diseases, including cirrhosis and hepatocellular carcinoma. Dendritic cells (DC) and Natural killer (NK) cells play crucial roles in the early immune response to HBV, but their function is compromised in chronic hepatitis B patients.
Core methods
Blood-derived DC subsets were pre-exposed to HBsAg and then co-cultured with NK cells under various stimulation conditions. We subsequently evaluated the modulations of DC, and the phenotypic and functional responses of NK cells. Similarly, DC subsets were exposed to serum from HBV-infected individuals with varying levels of HBsAg or directly purified from HBV patients to assess their ability to stimulate NK cell responses.
Main results
Our findings show that HBsAg disrupts cDC2-mediated NK cell activation through Toll-like receptor (TLR)7/8-dependent pathways and cDC1-mediated NK cell degranulation via TLR3-dependent mechanisms. Exposure of pDC to HBsAg alters NK cell activation, phenotype and degranulation through TLR9-dependent pathways. This impairment was recapitulated following exposure to serum from HBV-infected patients in an HBsAg-dependent manner. Consistently, circulating HBsAg in patients with chronic HBV infection was associated with impaired pDC-mediated NK cell cytotoxicity.
Interpretation and novelty
These results uncover key mechanisms by which HBsAg modulates pDC-NK cell interactions, shedding light on how HBV evades innate immune responses and contributes to immune dysfunction.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide insights into the mechanisms of HBV immune evasion and immune dysfunction, which could inform future research on immune modulation and the development of therapeutic strategies for chronic HBV infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on in vitro experiments and does not account for in vivo complexities, which may limit the generalizability of the findings. The evidence is strong as it directly links HBsAg to specific immune cell interactions and impairments.

在不同的临床环境中检查乙型肝炎的结构和社会驱动因素 疫苗接种

Examining Structural and Social Drivers of Hepatitis B Vaccination Across Diverse Clinical Settings
作者： Alisa Vidwans, Anita Narkhede, Emily Coughlin, Lisa J. Sanders
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 在美国,乙型肝炎是肝癌的主要原因,并且还与非霍奇金淋巴瘤、胰腺癌、胆管癌和头颈癌的风险增加有关。尽管乙型肝炎 疫苗 在预防慢性感染和随后的癌症发展方面非常有效,但 疫苗接种 比率仍然很低:2021 年,乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 在 19 岁及以上成年人中的覆盖率为 34.2%。本研究评估青少年和年轻人在临床环境、人口统计和健康社会决定因素 (SDOH) 方面的乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 系列完成情况,以确定 疫苗 覆盖范围的差距,并减少乙型肝炎感染和相关癌症的 发病率。方法 我们使用来自南佛罗里达大学卫生系统内的青少年医学、儿科传染病、内科儿科和家庭医学诊所以及南佛罗里达大学儿科内的社区性健康诊所 Ybor Youth Clinic 的电子健康记录数据进行了回顾性横断面分析。该样本包括 456 名年龄在 16-24 岁之间的患者,他们在 2025 年 1 月至 3 月期间就诊,并有记录的免疫史。乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 系列的完成情况通过诊所类型、人口统计和 SDOH 变量进行分析,包括年龄、性别、种族、民族、保险状况、主要语言、初级保健提供者、HPV 和破伤风 疫苗接种 状态以及合并症。结果 20 个诊所中,422 名患者 (92.5%) 完成了乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 系列。不同诊所类型的完成率差异很大 (p=0.012)。性别、种族和族裔等人口统计因素与 疫苗接种 状态没有显着相关性 (p=0.092、p=0.787、p=0.956)。然而,年轻患者更有可能完成 疫苗 系列 (p<0.001)。与拥有公共或私人保险的患者相比,未参保的患者的完成率较低 (p=0.045)。其他常规 疫苗(HPV 系列、破伤风和破伤风加强疫苗)的完成与乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 的完成密切相关(所有 p<0.001)。拥有初级保健提供者与完成治疗没有显着相关性(p=0.271),并且 HIV 感染等合并症也没有显示出显着关联(p=0.092,p=0.124)。结论 本次队列研究中乙型肝炎疫苗接种发病率明显高于全国平均水平。然而,由于各种 SDOH 因素,差异仍然存在。结构性和访问相关因素,特别是诊所类型和保险覆盖范围,比人口统计特征更能预测 疫苗 的完成情况,这表明结构性因素在确定 疫苗 状态方面比患者属性具有更大的影响力。这些发现强调需要采取系统级干预措施来支持系列的完成,特别是在服务不足的人群中,以支持乙型肝炎预防并减轻与癌症相关的差异。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨不同临床环境中乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 的结构和社会驱动因素,解决 疫苗接种 发病率低的公共卫生问题,尽管 疫苗 可有效预防慢性感染和相关癌症。
设计与对象
使用南佛罗里达大学卫生系统内 20 个诊所的电子健康记录数据进行了回顾性横断面分析,这些诊所包括青少年医学、儿科传染病和社区性健康诊所。该研究纳入了 456 名年龄在 16 岁至 24 岁之间、有免疫史且在 2025 年 1 月至 3 月期间就诊的患者。
核心方法
该研究按诊所类型以及人口统计和健康社会决定因素 (SDOH) 变量分析了乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 系列的完成情况,包括年龄、性别、种族、民族、保险状况、主要语言、初级保健提供者、HPV 和破伤风 疫苗接种 状态以及合并症。
主要结果
在 20 个诊所中,422 名患者 (92.5%) 完成了乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 系列。不同诊所类型的完成率差异很大 (p=0.012)。年轻患者更有可能完成 疫苗 系列 (p<0.001)。与拥有公共或私人保险的患者相比,未参保患者的完成率较低 (p=0.045)。其他常规 疫苗(HPV 系列、破伤风和破伤风加强疫苗)的完成与乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 的完成密切相关(所有 p<0.001)。
解释与创新
作者解释说,结构和访问相关因素,特别是诊所类型和保险范围,比人口特征更能预测 疫苗 的完成情况,表明结构因素对 疫苗接种 状态的影响比患者属性更大。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调需要采取系统级干预措施来支持乙型肝炎系列的完成,特别是在服务不足的人群中,以加强预防工作并减少与癌症相关的差异。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对电子健康记录数据的依赖而受到限制,这可能会引入偏差或缺失信息。由于该研究的观察性质以及存在未测量的混杂因素的可能性,证据强度适中。

Examining Structural and Social Drivers of Hepatitis B Vaccination Across Diverse Clinical Settings

Authors: Alisa Vidwans, Anita Narkhede, Emily Coughlin, Lisa J. Sanders
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Hepatitis B is a major cause of liver cancer in the United States and is also associated with increased risks of non-Hodgkin lymphoma, pancreatic, bile duct, and head and neck cancers. Although the Hepatitis B vaccine is highly effective at preventing chronic infection and subsequent cancer development, vaccination rates remain low: In 2021, Hepatitis B vaccination coverage was 34.2% among adults 19 and older. This study evaluates Hepatitis B vaccination series completion among adolescents and young adults across clinic settings, demographics, and social determinants of health (SDOH) to identify gaps in vaccine coverage and reduce the incidence of Hepatitis B infection and related cancers. Methods We performed a retrospective, cross-sectional analysis using electronic health record data from adolescent medicine, pediatric infectious diseases, internal medicine-pediatrics, and family medicine clinics within the University of South Florida health system, and Ybor Youth Clinic, a community sexual health clinic within the USF pediatrics department. The sample included 456 patients aged 16-24 who were seen between January and March 2025 and had documented immunization histories. Completion of the Hepatitis B vaccination series was analyzed by clinic type and demographic and SDOH variables, including age, gender, race, ethnicity, insurance status, primary language, primary care provider, HPV and tetanus vaccination status, and comorbid conditions. Results Across 20 clinics, 422 patients (92.5%) completed the Hepatitis B vaccination series. Completion rates varied significantly across clinic types (p=0.012). Demographic factors such as gender, race, and ethnicity were not significantly associated with vaccination status (p=0.092, p=0.787, p=0.956). However, younger patients were more likely to have completed the vaccine series (p<0.001). Uninsured patients demonstrated lower completion rates compared to those with public or private insurance (p=0.045). Completion of other routine vaccines (HPV series, tetanus, and tetanus booster) was strongly correlated with Hepatitis B vaccination completion (all p<0.001). Having a primary care provider was not significantly linked to completion (p=0.271), and comorbidities such as HIV infection showed no significant association (p=0.092, p=0.124). Conclusion Hepatitis B vaccination rates in this cohort were substantially higher than national averages. However, disparities persisted based on various SDOH factors. Structural and access-related factors, particularly clinic type and insurance coverage, were more predictive of vaccine completion than demographic characteristics, suggesting structural factors have a greater influence than patient attributes in determining vaccination status. These findings highlight the need for system-level interventions to support series completion, particularly among underserved populations, to support Hepatitis B prevention and mitigate cancer-related disparities.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to examine the structural and social drivers of Hepatitis B vaccination across diverse clinical settings, addressing the public health problem of low vaccination rates despite the vaccine's effectiveness in preventing chronic infection and related cancers.
Design and population
A retrospective, cross-sectional analysis was conducted using electronic health record data from 20 clinics within the University of South Florida health system, including adolescent medicine, pediatric infectious diseases, and community sexual health clinics. The study included 456 patients aged 16-24 who had documented immunization histories and were seen between January and March 2025.
Core methods
The study analyzed Hepatitis B vaccination series completion by clinic type and demographic and social determinants of health (SDOH) variables, including age, gender, race, ethnicity, insurance status, primary language, primary care provider, HPV and tetanus vaccination status, and comorbid conditions.
Main results
Across 20 clinics, 422 patients (92.5%) completed the Hepatitis B vaccination series. Completion rates varied significantly across clinic types (p=0.012). Younger patients were more likely to have completed the vaccine series (p<0.001). Uninsured patients had lower completion rates compared to those with public or private insurance (p=0.045). Completion of other routine vaccines (HPV series, tetanus, and tetanus booster) was strongly correlated with Hepatitis B vaccination completion (all p<0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that structural and access-related factors, particularly clinic type and insurance coverage, were more predictive of vaccine completion than demographic characteristics, indicating that structural factors have a greater influence on vaccination status than patient attributes.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings highlight the need for system-level interventions to support Hepatitis B series completion, especially among underserved populations, to enhance prevention efforts and reduce cancer-related disparities.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on electronic health record data, which may introduce biases or missing information. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational nature of the study and the potential for unmeasured confounding factors.
