海南省2005–2020年报告性乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎的趋势与预测

Trends and forecasting of reported hepatitis B and hepatitis C incidence in Hainan Province, China, 2005–2020
作者： Jiao Wang, Shibing Li, Cexiong Fu, Yi Su, Hanrong Fu, Yuanyuan Tian, Cheng Lan, 苏毅
摘要中文翻译目的 评估 2005 年至 2020 年海南省报告的 发病率(HBV)和丙型肝炎病毒(HCV)发病率 的长期趋势、季节性、预测以及与游客访问的生态关联。方法对海南报告的 HBV 和 HCV 发病率 数据、中国国家全球疾病负担(GBD)估计值以及年度游客访问数据进行分析。连接点回归评估时间趋势,使用黄土评估每月变化的季节趋势分解,季节自回归综合移动平均模型生成预测。斯皮尔曼等级相关分析检查了旅游协会,敏感性分析排除了 2020 年,并使用一阶差分和线性去趋势序列。结果 报告的 HBV 发病率 从 2005 年至 2008 年下降,从 2008 年至 2018 年上升,然后到 2020 年呈非显着下降;总体平均年百分比变化 (AAPC) 为 +3.37%。报告的 HCV 发病率 直到 2015 年一直呈上升趋势,随后下降,总体 AAPC 为 +14.54%。国家 GBD 估计显示,HBV 的总体 AAPC 为 -0.54%,HCV 的总体 AAPC 为 -1.52%。日历月份之间没有发现显着差异。根据历史报告模式,2030 年年度预测报告的 HBV 发病率 为每 10 万人 12.00(95% 预测区间:约 0.00-121.20),在数值上接近研究定义的参考值 12.20。相应的 HCV 估计值为每 100,000 人 22.54 例(约 0.00-39.10),约为研究定义参考值 3.55 的 6.4 倍。较宽的预测区间表明长期预测存在很大的不确定性。2020 年,观察到的平均每月报告的 HBV 和 HCV 发病率 率分别比预测值低 27.3% 和 9.5%。在原始年度分析中,游客访问量与报告的 HBV 和 HCV 发病率 相关(r = 0.871 和 0.879;均 P < 0.001)。经过一阶差分和线性去趋势后,仅对于报告的 HBV 发病率,该关联仍然显着。结论 海南省报告的HBV和HCV 发病率呈现明显的阶段性变化,但月度差异不显着。这些预测代表了历史报告模式下的预测,不应被解释为世卫组织消除目标取得进展的证据。旅游协会是探索性的、生态性的,不支持因果推理。持续的 监测 和加强的 HCV 筛查、诊断 以及与 治疗 的联系仍然很重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估 2005 年至 2020 年中国海南省报告的 发病率 (HBV) 和丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 发病率 的长期趋势、季节性和预测,重点关注与游客访问的生态关联。
设计与对象
该研究分析了海南省报告的 HBV 和 HCV 发病率 数据、中国国家全球疾病负担 (GBD) 估计值以及 2005 年至 2020 年期间的年度游客访问数据。
核心方法
连接点回归用于评估时间趋势,使用黄土进行季节性趋势分解评估每月变化,并使用季节性自回归集成移动平均模型生成预测。斯皮尔曼等级相关分析检查了旅游协会,敏感性分析排除了 2020 年,并使用一阶差分和线性去趋势序列。
主要结果
报告的 HBV 发病率 从 2005 年至 2008 年下降,从 2008 年至 2018 年上升,然后到 2020 年呈现非显着下降;总体平均年百分比变化 (AAPC) 为 +3.37%。报告的 HCV 发病率 直到 2015 年一直呈上升趋势,随后下降,总体 AAPC 为 +14.54%。国家 GBD 估计显示,HBV 的总体 AAPC 为 -0.54%,HCV 的总体 AAPC 为 -1.52%。日历月份之间没有发现显着差异。2020 年,观察到的平均每月报告的 HBV 和 HCV 发病率 率分别比预测值低 27.3% 和 9.5%。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为表明海南报告的 HBV 和 HCV 发病率 存在明显的阶段性变化,没有显着的月度差异。该研究的新颖之处在于其对趋势以及与旅游业的生态关联的全面分析。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为后续的 监测 提供信息,并强调加强 HCV 筛查、诊断 以及与 治疗 联系的重要性。他们还深入了解旅游业对 HBV 发病率 的潜在影响。
局限与证据强度
这些预测代表了历史报告模式下的预测,不应被解释为世卫组织消除目标取得进展的证据。较宽的预测区间表明长期预测存在很大的不确定性。

Trends and forecasting of reported hepatitis B and hepatitis C incidence in Hainan Province, China, 2005–2020

Authors: Jiao Wang, Shibing Li, Cexiong Fu, Yi Su, Hanrong Fu, Yuanyuan Tian, Cheng Lan, 苏毅
Original abstract (English)
Objective To assess long-term trends, seasonality, forecasts, and ecological associations with tourist visits for reported hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis C virus (HCV) incidence in Hainan Province from 2005 to 2020. Methods Reported HBV and HCV incidence data from Hainan, national Global Burden of Disease (GBD) estimates for China, and annual tourist visit data were analyzed. Joinpoint regression assessed temporal trends, seasonal-trend decomposition using loess evaluated monthly variation, and seasonal autoregressive integrated moving average models generated forecasts. Spearman rank correlation analysis examined tourism associations, with sensitivity analyses excluding 2020 and using first-differenced and linearly detrended series. Results Reported HBV incidence declined from 2005 to 2008, increased from 2008 to 2018, and then showed a non-significant decline through 2020; the overall average annual percent change (AAPC) was +3.37%. Reported HCV incidence increased until 2015 and subsequently declined, with an overall AAPC of +14.54%. National GBD estimates showed overall AAPCs of −0.54% for HBV and −1.52% for HCV. No significant differences among calendar months were detected. Under continuation of historical reporting patterns, the annualized projected reported HBV incidence in 2030 was 12.00 per 100,000 population (95% prediction interval: approximately 0.00–121.20), numerically close to the study-defined reference value of 12.20. The corresponding HCV estimate was 22.54 per 100,000 population (approximately 0.00–39.10), approximately 6.4 times the study-defined reference value of 3.55. The wide prediction intervals indicated substantial long-term forecast uncertainty. In 2020, observed mean monthly reported HBV and HCV incidence rates were 27.3% and 9.5% lower than forecasted values, respectively. In raw annual analyses, tourist visits correlated with reported HBV and HCV incidence (r = 0.871 and 0.879; both P < 0.001). After first differencing and linear detrending, the association remained significant only for reported HBV incidence. Conclusion Reported HBV and HCV incidence in Hainan showed distinct phased changes without significant monthly differences. The forecasts represent projections under historical reporting patterns and should not be interpreted as evidence of progress toward WHO elimination targets. Tourism associations were exploratory and ecological and do not support causal inference. Continued surveillance and strengthened HCV screening, diagnosis, and linkage to treatment remain important.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate long-term trends, seasonality, and forecasts for reported hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis C virus (HCV) incidence in Hainan Province, China, from 2005 to 2020, with a focus on ecological associations with tourist visits.
Design and population
The study analyzed reported HBV and HCV incidence data from Hainan Province, national Global Burden of Disease (GBD) estimates for China, and annual tourist visit data spanning the period 2005–2020.
Core methods
Joinpoint regression was used to assess temporal trends, seasonal-trend decomposition using loess evaluated monthly variation, and seasonal autoregressive integrated moving average models generated forecasts. Spearman rank correlation analysis examined tourism associations, with sensitivity analyses excluding 2020 and using first-differenced and linearly detrended series.
Main results
Reported HBV incidence declined from 2005 to 2008, increased from 2008 to 2018, and then showed a non-significant decline through 2020; the overall average annual percent change (AAPC) was +3.37%. Reported HCV incidence increased until 2015 and subsequently declined, with an overall AAPC of +14.54%. National GBD estimates showed overall AAPCs of −0.54% for HBV and −1.52% for HCV. No significant differences among calendar months were detected. In 2020, observed mean monthly reported HBV and HCV incidence rates were 27.3% and 9.5% lower than forecasted values, respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating distinct phased changes in reported HBV and HCV incidence in Hainan without significant monthly differences. The study's novelty lies in its comprehensive analysis of trends and ecological associations with tourism.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform continued surveillance and highlight the importance of strengthening HCV screening, diagnosis, and linkage to treatment. They also provide insights into the potential impact of tourism on HBV incidence.
Limitations and evidence strength
The forecasts represent projections under historical reporting patterns and should not be interpreted as evidence of progress toward WHO elimination targets. The wide prediction intervals indicated substantial long-term forecast uncertainty.

中国南宁市孕妇乙型肝炎病毒感染血清流行病学特征及影响因素(2024-2025年):横断面研究

Seroepidemiological Characteristics and Influencing Factors of Hepatitis B Virus Infection Among Pregnant Women in Nanning City, China (2024–2025): A Cross-Sectional Study
作者： Chang Huang, Tao Lan, Bin Xu, Qiuyun Deng, Chi Tang, Huang Chang, Deng QiuYun
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染仍然是孕妇的主要公共卫生问题,其流行特征的地区差异阻碍了母婴传播的精准预防。本研究旨在了解南宁市孕妇乙型肝炎病毒感染的血清流行病学特征,并确定其相关影响因素,为优化阻断乙型肝炎母婴传播的针对性干预措施提供循证见解。采用分层整群随机抽样策略,招募2024年10月至2025年5月在南宁市定点医疗机构接受常规产前保健的孕妇,采用标准化面对面调查问卷,收集人口学、行为和疾病相关数据。采集静脉血样本,通过化学发光免疫分析法定量检测 5 种血清学 HBV 标志物。采用Kruskal-Wallis检验比较乙型肝炎表面抗体(HBsAb)的几何平均浓度(GMC),采用卡方检验分析5种HBV标志物血清阳性率的组间差异。构建多变量非条件logistic回归模型来探索与乙型肝炎表面抗原(HBsAg)、HBsAb和乙型肝炎核心抗体(HBcAb)血清阳性独立相关的因素。共有 1,935 名符合条件的孕妇入组。HBsAg、HBsAb、乙型肝炎e抗原(HBeAg)、戊型肝炎抗体(HBeAb)和HBcAb总体血清阳性率分别为6.98%、59.48%、1.50%、15.50%和33.02%。HBsAg、HBsAb、HBeAb 和 HBcAb 的血清阳性率呈现出年龄依赖性上升趋势,在 14-19 岁年龄组中观察到最低阳性率,在 40-49 岁年龄组中观察到最高阳性率。与城市居民相比,农村居民的 HBsAg 和 HBeAb 血清阳性率显着较高,但 HBsAb 血清阳性率较低。与过去 5 年内或 ≥ 5 年前接种过疫苗的参与者相比,既往未知或无 HBV 疫苗接种 病史的参与者表现出 HBsAg、HBeAg、HBeAb 和 HBcAb 血清阳性率明显较高,同时 HBsAb 阳性率降低(所有 P < 0.001)。此外,与 5 年内接种疫苗的个体相比,≥5 年前接种疫苗的个体表现出较高的 HBV 标志物血清阳性率和较低的 HBsAb 阳性率(所有 P < 0.001)。多因素Logistic回归显示,高龄产妇、农村居民、合并慢性基础疾病以及与HBV携带者/患者密切接触是HBsAg血清阳性的独立危险因素,而既往HBV 疫苗接种则发挥保护作用。高龄、较高的教育程度和 HBV 疫苗接种 病史独立预测 HBsAb 阳性几率较高;农村居住、慢性合并症和非医疗职业状况是 HBsAb 血清转化不足的危险因素。对于HBcAb血清阳性,高龄、慢性基础疾病和接触HBV感染者是独立危险因素,而高等教育和疫苗接种病史是保护因素。南宁市孕妇乙型肝炎病毒患病率超过全国平均水平,城乡差异突出。高龄产妇和慢性合并症的存在增加了 HBV 感染的脆弱性,而育龄妇女中的 疫苗接种 覆盖率仍然不理想,保护性抗体滴度随着时间的推移逐渐减弱。我们建议针对 HBsAb 水平不足的育龄妇女使用靶向加强型 HBV 传播,以遏制 HBV 感染事件并降低垂直 传播 的风险。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
传播 (HBV) 感染对孕妇构成持续的公共卫生挑战,区域差异使预防母婴 传播 (MTCT) 的工作变得更加复杂。该研究解决了有关该人群乙型肝炎病毒感染的血清流行病学概况和影响因素的知识差距,为有针对性的干预措施提供信息。
设计与对象
一项横断面研究采用分层整群随机抽样方法,在定点医疗机构中招募了2024年10月至2025年5月期间在南宁市接受常规产前检查的1,935名孕妇。
核心方法
标准化问卷收集人口、行为和疾病相关数据。通过化学发光免疫分析对静脉血样本进行五种 HBV 血清学标志物(HBsAg、HBsAb、HBeAg、HBeAb、HBcAb)分析。多变量无条件逻辑回归模型确定了与 HBsAg、HBsAb 和 HBcAb 血清阳性独立相关的因素。
主要结果
总体血清阳性率为 HBsAg 6.98%、HBsAb 59.48%、HBeAg 1.50%、HBeAb 15.50% 和 HBcAb 33.02%。HBsAg、HBsAb、HBeAb 和 HBcAb 的血清阳性率随着年龄的增长而增加,在 40-49 岁年龄组达到峰值。农村居民的HBsAg和HBeAb血清阳性率高于城市居民,但HBsAb血清阳性率低于城市居民。
解释与创新
该研究确定高龄产妇、农村居民、慢性合并症以及与 HBV 携带者的密切接触是 HBsAg 血清阳性的独立危险因素,而既往 HBV 疫苗接种 具有保护作用。新颖之处在于量化城乡差异以及 疫苗 引起的免疫力随着时间的推移而减弱。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调,南宁市需要有针对性的 HBV 传播 策略,包括对 HBsAb 水平不足的育龄妇女进行加强剂量,以减少感染事件并减轻垂直 传播 风险。
局限与证据强度
横截面设计限制了因果推断,并且对自我报告的 疫苗接种 历史的依赖可能会引入回忆偏差。尽管存在这些限制,但大样本量和多变量调整增强了识别风险因素的证据基础。

Seroepidemiological Characteristics and Influencing Factors of Hepatitis B Virus Infection Among Pregnant Women in Nanning City, China (2024–2025): A Cross-Sectional Study

Authors: Chang Huang, Tao Lan, Bin Xu, Qiuyun Deng, Chi Tang, Huang Chang, Deng QiuYun
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a major public health concern among pregnant women, with regional disparities in epidemic characteristics that hinder precise prevention of mother-to-child transmission (MTCT). This study aimed to characterize the seroepidemiological profiles of HBV infection and identify its associated influencing factors among pregnant women in Nanning City, providing evidence-based insights for optimizing targeted interventions to block HBV MTCT. A stratified cluster random sampling strategy was adopted to recruit pregnant women receiving routine antenatal care at designated medical institutions across Nanning between October 2024 and May 2025. Standardized face-to-face questionnaires were administered to collect demographic, behavioral, and disease-related data. Venous blood samples were collected for quantitative detection of five serological HBV markers via chemiluminescence immunoassay. The Kruskal–Wallis test was used to compare the geometric mean concentration (GMC) of hepatitis B surface antibody (HBsAb), while the chi-square test was applied to analyze intergroup differences in the seropositivity rates of the five HBV markers. Multivariate unconditional logistic regression models were constructed to explore factors independently associated with seropositivity of hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg), HBsAb, and hepatitis B core antibody (HBcAb). A total of 1,935 eligible pregnant women were enrolled. The overall seropositivity rates of HBsAg, HBsAb, hepatitis B e-antigen (HBeAg), hepatitis e-antibody (HBeAb), and HBcAb were 6.98%, 59.48%, 1.50%, 15.50%, and 33.02%, respectively. Seropositivity of HBsAg, HBsAb, HBeAb, and HBcAb exhibited an age-dependent ascending trend, with the lowest rates observed in the 14–19 age group and peak rates in the 40–49 age group. Rural residents presented significantly higher HBsAg and HBeAb seropositivity but lower HBsAb seropositivity compared with urban counterparts. Participants with unknown or no prior HBV vaccination history showed markedly higher seropositivity of HBsAg, HBeAg, HBeAb, and HBcAb, alongside reduced HBsAb positivity, relative to those vaccinated within the past 5 years or ≥5 years prior (all P < 0.001). Furthermore, individuals vaccinated ≥5 years ago demonstrated higher HBV marker seropositivity and lower HBsAb positivity than those vaccinated within 5 years (all P < 0.001). Multivariate logistic regression revealed that advanced maternal age, rural residency, comorbid chronic underlying diseases, and close contact with HBV carriers/patients were independent risk factors for HBsAg seropositivity, whereas prior HBV vaccination exerted a protective effect. Advanced age, higher educational attainment, and HBV vaccination history independently predicted higher odds of HBsAb positivity; rural residency, chronic comorbidities, and non-medical occupational status were risk factors for insufficient HBsAb seroconversion. For HBcAb seropositivity, advanced age, chronic underlying diseases, and exposure to HBV-infected individuals were independent risk factors, while higher education and vaccination history served as protective factors. HBV prevalence among pregnant women in Nanning exceeds the national average, accompanied by prominent urban–rural disparities. Advanced maternal age and the presence of chronic comorbidities increase HBV infection vulnerability, while vaccination coverage among childbearing-age women remains suboptimal with progressive attenuation of protective antibody titers over time. We recommend targeted booster HBV vaccination for childbearing-age women with insufficient HBsAb levels to curb incident HBV infections and mitigate the risk of vertical transmission.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection poses a persistent public health challenge among pregnant women, with regional disparities complicating efforts to prevent mother-to-child transmission (MTCT). The study addressed the knowledge gap regarding seroepidemiological profiles and influencing factors of HBV infection in this population to inform targeted interventions.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study recruited 1,935 pregnant women receiving routine antenatal care in Nanning City between October 2024 and May 2025 using stratified cluster random sampling across designated medical institutions.
Core methods
Standardized questionnaires collected demographic, behavioral, and disease-related data. Venous blood samples were analyzed for five HBV serological markers (HBsAg, HBsAb, HBeAg, HBeAb, HBcAb) via chemiluminescence immunoassay. Multivariate unconditional logistic regression models identified factors independently associated with seropositivity of HBsAg, HBsAb, and HBcAb.
Main results
The overall seropositivity rates were 6.98% for HBsAg, 59.48% for HBsAb, 1.50% for HBeAg, 15.50% for HBeAb, and 33.02% for HBcAb. Seropositivity of HBsAg, HBsAb, HBeAb, and HBcAb increased with age, peaking in the 40–49 age group. Rural residents had higher HBsAg and HBeAb seropositivity but lower HBsAb seropositivity than urban residents.
Interpretation and novelty
The study identified advanced maternal age, rural residency, chronic comorbidities, and close contact with HBV carriers as independent risk factors for HBsAg seropositivity, while prior HBV vaccination was protective. Novelty lies in quantifying urban–rural disparities and waning vaccine-induced immunity over time in this population.
Scientific and public-health significance
Findings highlight the need for targeted HBV vaccination strategies, including booster doses for childbearing-age women with insufficient HBsAb levels, to reduce incident infections and mitigate vertical transmission risk in Nanning City.
Limitations and evidence strength
The cross-sectional design limits causal inference, and reliance on self-reported vaccination history may introduce recall bias. Despite these limitations, the large sample size and multivariate adjustment strengthen the evidence base for identifying risk factors.

乙型肝炎 疫苗 阳性反应者和 慢性乙型肝炎 患者的 αβ-TCR 谱的综合评估

Comprehensive evaluation of the αβ-TCR repertoire profile in hepatitis B vaccine-positive responders and patients with chronic hepatitis B
作者： Jiezuan Yang, Dong Yan, Hua Zhang, Linfeng Jin, Haifeng Lu
摘要中文翻译T细胞在乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染中发挥关键作用,既介导保护性免疫,又参与免疫病理过程。通过深度分析T细胞受体(TCR)互补决定区3(CDR3)可为T细胞组成和功能提供重要见解。然而,不同HBV感染状态和乙型肝炎疫苗接种状态个体的TCR α链(TRA)和β链(TRB)库特征仍有待深入解析。本研究采用高通量测序(HTS)技术,对6名乙型肝炎疫苗接种阳性应答者(IPR)、6名慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)患者及5名健康供者(HD)的外周血单个核细胞(PBMC)中TRA和TRB CDR3库进行了表征。系统比较了三组间TRA和TRB CDR3的频率、多样性、相似性及V/J片段使用情况。CHB组的TRA和TRB CDR3的独特序列数量显著低于IPR组和HD组。重叠分析显示,IPR组TRAV的BUB指数显著高于CHB组和HD组(P=0.0073和P=0.0317),而TRBV的BUB指数仅在IPR组与CHB组间显著升高(P=0.0002)。此外,相同TRA CDR3在各组内个体间优先共享,而TRBJ使用在各组间相对稳定。综上,本研究揭示了IPR和CHB循环T细胞CDR3库的不同特征,有助于深入理解对乙型肝炎疫苗接种的保护性应答,并为未来开发基于TRA和TRB的HBV治疗和预防策略提供理论依据。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在解析不同HBV感染状态和乙型肝炎疫苗接种状态个体的TCR库特征,因T细胞在HBV感染中发挥关键作用,既介导保护性免疫,又参与免疫病理过程。
设计与对象
本研究采用横断面研究设计,比较了6名乙型肝炎疫苗接种阳性应答者、6名慢性乙型肝炎患者及5名健康供者的外周血单个核细胞中TCR α链(TRA)和β链(TRB)库特征。
核心方法
采用高通量测序技术对三组受试者PBMC中的TRA和TRB CDR3库进行表征,并系统比较了其频率、多样性、相似性及V/J片段使用情况。
主要结果
慢性乙型肝炎组的TRA和TRB CDR3独特序列数量显著低于疫苗接种阳性应答者组和健康供者组。IPR组TRAV的BUB指数显著高于CHB组和HD组(P=0.0073和P=0.0317),而TRBV的BUB指数仅在IPR组与CHB组间显著升高(P=0.0002)。
解释与创新
本研究揭示了疫苗接种阳性应答者和慢性乙型肝炎患者循环T细胞CDR3库的不同特征,有助于深入理解对乙型肝炎疫苗接种的保护性应答,并为未来开发基于TRA和TRB的HBV治疗和预防策略提供理论依据。
科研与公卫意义
结果可能会增强对 免疫应答 到 HepB 疫苗接种 的理解,并有助于制定基于 TRA 和 TRB 的 HBV 诊断 和预防策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到样本量小和仅摘要证据的限制,这可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。由于缺乏详细的统计分析和控制,证据强度中等。

Comprehensive evaluation of the αβ-TCR repertoire profile in hepatitis B vaccine-positive responders and patients with chronic hepatitis B

Authors: Jiezuan Yang, Dong Yan, Hua Zhang, Linfeng Jin, Haifeng Lu
Original abstract (English)
T cells play a vital role in hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection, mediating both protective immunity and immunopathology. Deep profiling of the T cell receptor (TCR) complementarity-determining region 3 (CDR3) provides critical insights into T cell composition and function. However, the characteristics of the TCR α-chain (TRA) and β-chain (TRB) repertoires in individuals with different HBV infection and vaccination statuses remain incompletely understood. We employed high-throughput sequencing (HTS) to profile the TRA and TRB CDR3 repertoires in peripheral blood mononuclear cells (PBMCs) from 6 inoculation-positive responders (IPR) to hepatitis B (HepB) vaccination, 6 individuals with chronic hepatitis B (CHB), and 5 healthy donors (HD). We systematically compared the frequency, diversity, similarity, and V/J segment usage of TRA and TRB CDR3s across the three groups. The CHB group exhibited significantly fewer unique TRA and TRB CDR3s than both IPR and HD groups. Overlap analysis revealed that the BUB index for TRAV was significantly higher in IPR than in CHB and HD groups ( P = 0.0073 and P = 0.0317, respectively), while for TRBV, a significantly higher BUB index was observed only between IPR and CHB groups ( P = 0.0002). Additionally, identical TRA CDR3s were preferentially shared among individuals within each group, whereas TRBJ usage remained relatively stable across groups. Collectively, our findings delineate distinct CDR3 repertoire characteristics of circulating T cells in IPR and CHB, which may enhance understanding of the protective response to HepB vaccination and inform future investigations into TRA- and TRB-based strategies for HBV diagnosis and prevention.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the TCR repertoire characteristics in individuals with different HBV infection and vaccination statuses, as T cells play a vital role in HBV infection, mediating both protective immunity and immunopathology.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted comparing 6 inoculation-positive responders to HepB vaccination, 6 individuals with chronic hepatitis B, and 5 healthy donors, focusing on the TCR α-chain (TRA) and β-chain (TRB) repertoires in peripheral blood mononuclear cells (PBMCs).
Core methods
High-throughput sequencing (HTS) was used to profile the TRA and TRB CDR3 repertoires in PBMCs from the three groups, with systematic comparisons of frequency, diversity, similarity, and V/J segment usage.
Main results
The CHB group showed significantly fewer unique TRA and TRB CDR3s compared to the IPR and HD groups. The BUB index for TRAV was significantly higher in IPR than in CHB and HD groups (P = 0.0073 and P = 0.0317, respectively), while for TRBV, a significantly higher BUB index was observed only between IPR and CHB groups (P = 0.0002).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings delineate distinct CDR3 repertoire characteristics of circulating T cells in IPR and CHB, offering insights into the protective response to HepB vaccination and informing future investigations into TRA- and TRB-based strategies for HBV diagnosis and prevention.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results may enhance understanding of the immune response to HepB vaccination and contribute to the development of TRA- and TRB-based strategies for HBV diagnosis and prevention.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its small sample size and abstract-only evidence, which may affect the generalisability of the findings. The evidence strength is moderate due to the lack of detailed statistical analyses and controls.

NA 抑制的 慢性乙型肝炎 中的 siRNA daplusiran/tomligisiran 和 DNA 疫苗 JNJ-64300535:OSPREY 研究

siRNA daplusiran/tomligisiran and DNA vaccine JNJ-64300535 in NA-suppressed chronic hepatitis B: the OSPREY study
作者： Stefan Bourgeois, Maria Buti, Edward J. Gane, Kosh Agarwal, Ewa Janczewska, Yao-Chun Hsu, Maurizia Rosanna Brunetto, Pietro Lampertico, Joaquin Cabezas, Gloria Kim
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估在病毒学抑制、HBeAg 阴性 慢性乙型肝炎 参与者中,用 daplusiran/tomligisiran (DAP/TOM) 减少病毒抗原是否可以增强 HBV 特异性 T 细胞和/或 HBsAg 对 DNA 疫苗 JNJ-0535 的反应。
设计与对象
OSPREY 是一项单组、开放标签、1b 期研究,涉及 24 名病毒学受到抑制、HBeAg 阴性 慢性乙型肝炎 的参与者。所有参与者均接受为期 24 周的 DAP/TOM(200 毫克,Q4W)治疗,并从第 14 周至第 26 周接受 4 剂 JNJ-0535。
核心方法
参与者接受核苷类似物 (NA) 治疗直至第 36 周,并在第 48 周继续随访,除非满足停止标准。该研究评估了 HBsAg 减少和 T 细胞对 DNA 疫苗 JNJ-0535 的反应。
主要结果
在入组的 24 名参与者中,有 11 名 (45.8%) 在第 36 周达到了 ≥2 log10 HBsAg 降低的主要终点。7 名参与者根据第 36 周的结果停止了 NA,并且与继续 NA 的参与者相比,在随访第 48 周时有更多针对 ≥2 HBV 抗原的多功能 CD4 T 细胞,并且 HBsAg 平均降低幅度更大(2.42 log10 IU/mL vs 0.96 log10)国际单位/毫升)。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,JNJ-0535 疫苗接种 可能会增强一些参与者的核心/聚合酶特异性 T 细胞反应,在停止 NA 的参与者和继续进行 NA 的参与者之间观察到 HBsAg 减少的显着差异。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果提供了关于将 DAP/TOM 与 DNA 疫苗 相结合以改善 免疫应答 受抑制的 慢性乙型肝炎 患者的潜力的见解,这可以为未来的 疫苗 开发策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究是一项单臂、开放标签试验,样本量较小,限制了研究结果的普遍性。由于缺乏对照组和随访的观察性质,证据是中等的。

siRNA daplusiran/tomligisiran and DNA vaccine JNJ-64300535 in NA-suppressed chronic hepatitis B: the OSPREY study

Authors: Stefan Bourgeois, Maria Buti, Edward J. Gane, Kosh Agarwal, Ewa Janczewska, Yao-Chun Hsu, Maurizia Rosanna Brunetto, Pietro Lampertico, Joaquin Cabezas, Gloria Kim
Original abstract (English)
Abstract OSPREY was a single-arm, open-label, phase 1b study that assessed whether reduction of viral antigens by the small-interfering RNA daplusiran/tomligisiran (DAP/TOM) could enhance HBV-specific T-cell and/or HBsAg responses to the DNA vaccine JNJ-64300535 (JNJ-0535) in participants with virologically suppressed, HBeAg-negative chronic hepatitis B. All participants received DAP/TOM (200 mg, Q4W) for 24 weeks and 4 doses of JNJ-0535 from Week (W)14 to W26. NA was given until W36 and continued to Follow-up W48, unless stopping criteria were met. Of 24 participants enrolled, 11 (45.8%) achieved the primary endpoint of ≥2 log 10 HBsAg reduction at W36. JNJ-0535 vaccination enhanced core/polymerase-specific T-cell responses in 22% of participants by W28, and 39% during follow-up. Seven participants stopped NA based on W36 results and had more polyfunctional CD4 T-cells targeting ≥2 HBV antigens and a greater mean reduction in HBsAg at Follow-up W48 versus those who continued NA (2.42 log 10 IU/mL vs 0.96 log 10 IU/mL).
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess whether reducing viral antigens with daplusiran/tomligisiran (DAP/TOM) could enhance HBV-specific T-cell and/or HBsAg responses to the DNA vaccine JNJ-0535 in participants with virologically suppressed, HBeAg-negative chronic hepatitis B.
Design and population
OSPREY was a single-arm, open-label, phase 1b study involving 24 participants with virologically suppressed, HBeAg-negative chronic hepatitis B. All participants received DAP/TOM (200 mg, Q4W) for 24 weeks and 4 doses of JNJ-0535 from Week 14 to Week 26.
Core methods
Participants received nucleos(t)ide analog (NA) therapy until Week 36 and continued to follow-up at Week 48 unless stopping criteria were met. The study evaluated HBsAg reduction and T-cell responses to the DNA vaccine JNJ-0535.
Main results
Of the 24 participants enrolled, 11 (45.8%) achieved the primary endpoint of ≥2 log10 HBsAg reduction at Week 36. Seven participants stopped NA based on Week 36 results and had more polyfunctional CD4 T-cells targeting ≥2 HBV antigens and a greater mean reduction in HBsAg at Follow-up Week 48 versus those who continued NA (2.42 log10 IU/mL vs 0.96 log10 IU/mL).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that JNJ-0535 vaccination may enhance core/polymerase-specific T-cell responses in some participants, with a notable difference in HBsAg reduction observed between those who stopped NA and those who continued.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide insights into the potential of combining DAP/TOM with DNA vaccines to improve immune responses in patients with suppressed chronic hepatitis B, which could inform future vaccine development strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was a single-arm, open-label trial with a small sample size, limiting the generalisability of the findings. The evidence is moderate due to the lack of a control group and the observational nature of the follow-up.

替诺福韦阿米布芬酰胺在 治疗 初治 慢性乙型肝炎 中的真实有效性和安全性:一项为期 48 周的回顾性 队列研究 研究

Real-world effectiveness and safety of tenofovir amibufenamide in treatment-naïve chronic hepatitis B: a 48-week retrospective cohort study
作者： Jiarui Zheng, Yuxuan Song, Bo Feng, Yandi Xie, 谢艳迪
摘要中文翻译目的 替诺福韦阿米布芬酰胺 (TMF) 是中国新推荐的 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 一线核苷类似物。然而,关于其安全性和有效性的现实证据有限。本研究旨在评估 TMF 在现实世界 队列研究 慢性乙型肝炎个体中 48 周的短期病毒学疗效、生化反应以及安全性。方法 在这项单中心回顾性 队列研究 研究中,129 名 治疗 初治 CHB 患者每天服用 25 mg TMF。在基线以及第 12、24 和 48 周收集临床、病毒学和生化参数以进行评估。主要有效性终点是 治疗 48 周后的病毒学应答(VR、HBV DNA <10 IU/mL)。次要终点包括其他时间点的 VR、丙氨酸转氨酶 (ALT) 正常化以及肾脏参数和血脂水平的变化。结果 第 48 周时,72.9% (94/129) 的患者实现 VR。多变量分析确定较低的基线 HBsAg 水平、HBeAg 阴性状态和非肝硬化状态是实现 VR 的独立预测因素。第 48 周时 ALT 正常化率为 77.5% (100/129)。一名患者实现了 HBsAg 血清清除。关于安全性,没有观察到与药物相关的不良事件。从基线到第 48 周,血清磷、估计肾小球滤过率、血清肌酐或脂质水平(甘油三酯、总胆固醇、高密度脂蛋白和低密度脂蛋白)没有统计学上显着的变化。结论 在这项单臂观察性 队列研究 中,TMF 治疗 在第 48 周时实现了有效的病毒学抑制和高 ALT 正常化率,肾功能和脂质水平保持稳定。由于该研究缺乏比较组,因此这些发现应被视为观察性的。关于 TMF 相对于富马酸替诺福韦二吡呋酯 (TDF) 或替诺福韦艾拉酚胺 (TAF) 的安全性或优越性的比较结论需要专门的头对头研究。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这项研究需要解决有关替诺福韦阿米布酰胺 (TMF) 在中国治疗 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 的安全性和有效性的有限现实证据。该研究旨在探索其短期病毒学功效、生化反应和安全性。
设计与对象
进行了一项单中心回顾性 队列研究 研究,涉及 129 名 治疗 初治 CHB 患者,他们每天服用 25 mg TMF。该研究评估了基线以及第 12、24 和 48 周时的临床、病毒学和生化参数。
核心方法
在基线以及第 12、24 和 48 周收集临床、病毒学和生化参数。主要有效性终点定义为 治疗 48 周后的病毒学应答(VR、HBV DNA <10 IU/mL),次要终点包括其他时间点的 VR、ALT 正常化以及肾脏参数和血脂水平的变化。
主要结果
第 48 周时,72.9% (94/129) 的患者达到病毒学应答。第 48 周时 ALT 正常化率为 77.5% (100/129)。一名患者实现了 HBsAg 血清清除。没有观察到与药物相关的不良事件,并且从基线到第 48 周,肾脏参数或血脂水平没有统计学上的显着变化。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为,到第 48 周时,病毒学抑制有效,并且 ALT 正常化率较高,肾功能和血脂水平稳定。这项研究提供了关于 TMF 在 治疗 初治慢性乙型肝炎患者中的有效性和安全性的新颖的真实世界数据。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为 TMF 作为 慢性乙型肝炎 的一线 治疗 的使用提供信息,特别是在现实环境中,并支持其在肾功能和血脂水平方面的安全性。
局限与证据强度
该研究缺乏比较组,这限制了就 TMF 相对于其他核苷(酸)类似物的安全性或优越性得出比较结论的能力。证据是观察性的,因此强度适中。

Real-world effectiveness and safety of tenofovir amibufenamide in treatment-naïve chronic hepatitis B: a 48-week retrospective cohort study

Authors: Jiarui Zheng, Yuxuan Song, Bo Feng, Yandi Xie, 谢艳迪
Original abstract (English)
Objectives Tenofovir amibufenamide (TMF) is a newly recommended first-line nucleos(t)ide analogues for chronic hepatitis B (CHB) in China. However, real-world evidence on its safety and effectiveness are limited. This study aimed to evaluate the short-term virological efficacy, biochemical response, as well as safety profile of TMF over 48 weeks in a real-world cohort of CHB individuals. Methods In this single-center retrospective cohort study, 129 treatment-naïve CHB patients were administered TMF at 25 mg daily. Clinical, virological, and biochemical parameters were gathered at baseline and at weeks 12, 24, and 48 for evaluation. The primary effectiveness endpoint was virological response (VR, HBV DNA <10 IU/mL) after 48 weeks of treatment. Secondary endpoints included VR at other timepoints, alanine aminotransferase (ALT) normalization, and changes in renal parameters and lipid levels. Results At week 48, 72.9% (94/129) of patients achieved VR. Multivariate analysis identified lower baseline HBsAg levels, HBeAg negative status and non-cirrhotic status as independent predictors for achieving VR. The ALT normalization rate was 77.5% (100/129) at week 48. One patient achieved HBsAg seroclearance. Regarding safety, no adverse events linked to the medication were observed. There were no statistically significant alterations in serum phosphorus, estimated glomerular filtration rate, serum creatinine, or lipid levels (triglycerides, total cholesterol, high-density lipoprotein and low-density lipoprotein) from baseline to week 48. Conclusion In this single-arm observational cohort, TMF treatment achieved effective virological suppression and high rates of ALT normalization by week 48, with renal function and lipid levels remaining stable. Because the study lacked a comparator group, these findings should be regarded as observational. Comparative conclusions about the safety or superiority of TMF relative to tenofovir disoproxil fumarate (TDF) or tenofovir alafenamide (TAF) require dedicated head-to-head studies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study was needed to address the limited real-world evidence on the safety and effectiveness of tenofovir amibufenamide (TMF) for chronic hepatitis B (CHB) in China. The research aimed to explore its short-term virological efficacy, biochemical response, and safety profile.
Design and population
A single-center retrospective cohort study was conducted involving 129 treatment-naïve CHB patients who were administered TMF at 25 mg daily. The study evaluated clinical, virological, and biochemical parameters at baseline and at weeks 12, 24, and 48.
Core methods
Clinical, virological, and biochemical parameters were collected at baseline and at weeks 12, 24, and 48. The primary effectiveness endpoint was defined as virological response (VR, HBV DNA <10 IU/mL) after 48 weeks of treatment, while secondary endpoints included VR at other timepoints, ALT normalization, and changes in renal parameters and lipid levels.
Main results
At week 48, 72.9% (94/129) of patients achieved virological response. The ALT normalization rate was 77.5% (100/129) at week 48. One patient achieved HBsAg seroclearance. No adverse events linked to the medication were observed, and there were no statistically significant changes in renal parameters or lipid levels from baseline to week 48.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as demonstrating effective virological suppression and high rates of ALT normalization by week 48, with stable renal function and lipid levels. This study provides novel real-world data on TMF's efficacy and safety in treatment-naïve CHB patients.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the use of TMF as a first-line treatment for chronic hepatitis B, particularly in real-world settings, and support its safety profile in terms of renal function and lipid levels.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study lacked a comparator group, which limits the ability to draw comparative conclusions about TMF's safety or superiority relative to other nucleos(t)ide analogues. The evidence is observational and thus moderate in strength.

乙型肝炎病毒 X 蛋白中的混杂金属位点结合 Fe-S 簇

Promiscuous Metal Site in Hepatitis B Virus X Protein Binds an Fe–S Cluster
作者： Jiahua Chen, Michelle Langton, Patrick Cao, Avital Aaron, Jackson Ho, Eranthie Weerapana, Deborah L. Perlstein, Alexey Silakov, Daniel W. Bak, Maria-Eirini Pandelia
摘要中文翻译摘要 Fe-S 簇正在成为病毒复制中的重要辅助因子,但由于其 O2 敏感性和基于半胱氨​​酸的配位化学重叠,经常被错误地分配为 Zn。乙型肝炎病毒 调节蛋白 HBx 对于病毒复制和肝癌发生至关重要,但由于其生理相关金属辅因子的不确定性,长期以来在机制上一直难以处理。尽管 HBx 可以结合 Fe-S 簇或 Zn,但其内在无序和广泛的突变耐受性阻碍了对其金属结合环境的精确表征。在这里,我们将化学蛋白质组学与 HYSCORE 光谱法相结合,定义 HBx 中的金属配位配体,并克服与无序蛋白质的常规突变分析相关的局限性。我们排除组氨酸配位,并将 C61、C69、C143 和 C148 确定为 Fe-S 簇结合的主要半胱氨酸配体,C137 作为条件辅助配体。这些残基还支持 Zn 结合,并与 HBx 反式激活和临床相关变体涉及的区域重叠。此外,HBx 参与 宿主 胞质 Fe-S 簇组装机制,并对 Fe-S 靶向试剂表现出敏感性,行为与 Fe-S 簇获取和辅因子不稳定性一致。总之,这些发现表明 HBx 是一种 Fe-S 簇相关病毒蛋白,并扩展了对感染至关重要的病毒 Fe-S 蛋白的不断增长的类别。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在阐明 HBx 的金属辅因子,HBx 是病毒复制和肝癌发生的关键蛋白,由于其生理相关金属结合环境的不确定性,很难对其进行表征。
设计与对象
该研究重点关注 乙型肝炎病毒 调节蛋白 HBx,通过化学蛋白质组学和 HYSCORE 光谱检查其金属结合特性,以克服传统突变分析的局限性。
核心方法
研究人员将化学蛋白质组学与 HYSCORE 光谱法相结合,识别 HBx 中的金属配位配体,解决其内在紊乱和突变耐受性带来的挑战。
主要结果
该研究排除了组氨酸配位,并确定 C61、C69、C143 和 C148 作为 Fe-S 簇结合的主要半胱氨酸配体,C137 作为条件辅助配体。
解释与创新
研究结果表明 HBx 是一种 Fe-S 簇相关病毒蛋白,扩大了对感染至关重要的病毒 Fe-S 蛋白类别。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为了解 HBx 功能的分子机制提供了见解,并可能为未来病毒复制和肝癌发生的研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象级别的证据,并且研究结果基于化学蛋白质组学和 HYSCORE 光谱学,可能无法完全捕捉金属结合环境的生理相关性。

Promiscuous Metal Site in Hepatitis B Virus X Protein Binds an Fe–S Cluster

Authors: Jiahua Chen, Michelle Langton, Patrick Cao, Avital Aaron, Jackson Ho, Eranthie Weerapana, Deborah L. Perlstein, Alexey Silakov, Daniel W. Bak, Maria-Eirini Pandelia
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Fe–S clusters are emerging as important cofactors in viral replication but are frequently misassigned as Zn due to their O2-sensitivity and overlapping cysteine-based coordination chemistry. The Hepatitis B virus regulatory protein HBx, which is essential for viral replication and hepatocarcinogenesis, has long remained mechanistically intractable because of uncertainty surrounding its physiologically relevant metallocofactor. Although HBx can bind either an Fe–S cluster or Zn, its intrinsic disorder and extensive mutational tolerance have hindered precise characterization of its metal-binding environment. Here, we combine chemoproteomics with HYSCORE spectroscopy to define the metal-coordinating ligands in HBx and overcome limitations associated with conventional mutational analysis of disordered proteins. We exclude histidine coordination and identify C61, C69, C143, and C148 as the primary cysteine ligands for Fe–S cluster binding, with C137 functioning as a conditional auxiliary ligand. These residues also support Zn binding and overlap with regions implicated in HBx transactivation and clinically relevant variants. In addition, HBx engages the host cytosolic Fe–S cluster assembly machinery and displays sensitivity to Fe–S-targeting reagents, behavior consistent with Fe–S cluster acquisition and cofactor lability. Together, these findings propose HBx as an Fe–S cluster-associated viral protein and expand the growing class of viral Fe–S proteins that are critical for infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to clarify the metallocofactor of HBx, a critical protein for viral replication and hepatocarcinogenesis, which has been difficult to characterize due to uncertainty about its physiologically relevant metal-binding environment.
Design and population
The study focused on the Hepatitis B virus regulatory protein HBx, examining its metal-binding properties through chemoproteomics and HYSCORE spectroscopy to overcome limitations of conventional mutational analysis.
Core methods
The researchers combined chemoproteomics with HYSCORE spectroscopy to identify the metal-coordinating ligands in HBx, addressing challenges posed by its intrinsic disorder and mutational tolerance.
Main results
The study excluded histidine coordination and identified C61, C69, C143, and C148 as the primary cysteine ligands for Fe–S cluster binding, with C137 functioning as a conditional auxiliary ligand.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings propose HBx as an Fe–S cluster-associated viral protein, expanding the class of viral Fe–S proteins critical for infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide insights into the molecular mechanisms of HBx function and may inform future studies on viral replication and hepatocarcinogenesis.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-level evidence, and the findings are based on chemoproteomics and HYSCORE spectroscopy, which may not fully capture the physiological relevance of the metal-binding environment.

慢性丁型肝炎病毒感染对健康相关生活质量和疲劳的影响:一项真实的国际研究。

The Impact of Chronic Hepatitis D Virus Infection on Health-Related Quality of Life and Fatigue: A Real-World International Study.
作者： Lampertico P, R G Gish, Reau N, Wedemeyer H, Buti M, Kaushik A, Yehoshua A, Burk C, Mirams L, Ellis H
摘要中文翻译由于人们对慢性丁型肝炎病毒(HDV)感染的疾病负担知之甚少,因此本研究检查了不同疾病阶段的健康相关生活质量(HRQoL)和疲劳。这是对德国、意大利、西班牙和美国患者的真实世界定量横断面调查。患者是通过治疗医生招募的。接受过治疗乙型肝炎的 bulevivirtide 或治疗 HDV 的 治疗 的患者被排除在外。211 名患者中,41% 为非肝硬化(F0-F3);20%患有肝硬化但功能正常(代偿);20% 患有肝硬化(F4)且功能受损(失代偿);19% 患有肝细胞癌 (HCC)。平均(标准差;SD)年龄为 54.5(11.5)岁,75% 为男性。方差分析显示,随着疾病阶段恶化和严重程度增加,EQ-5D-3L 视觉模拟量表 (VAS) 评分和指数值显着下降(表明 HRQoL 较差)(所有 p < 0.0001)。肝炎生活质量问卷 v2 的所有领域评分(除了积极的健康状况)随着疾病的进展和症状严重程度的增加而恶化(所有 p ≤ 0.0001)。平均疲劳严重程度量表 (FSS) 总分也增加,表明疲劳程度更大,疾病恶化,症状严重程度增加(均 p < 0.0001)。丁型肝炎病毒感染显着影响患者的 HRQoL 和疲劳,特别是对于疾病后期的患者。需要减缓疾病进展、减轻丁型肝炎病毒感染负担的治疗。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
由于人们对慢性丁型肝炎病毒(HDV)感染的疾病负担知之甚少,因此本研究检查了不同疾病阶段的健康相关生活质量(HRQoL)和疲劳。
设计与对象
这是对德国、意大利、西班牙和美国患者的真实世界定量横断面调查。患者是通过治疗医生招募的,在 211 名患者中,41% 为非肝硬化 (F0-F3);20%患有肝硬化但功能正常(代偿);20% 患有肝硬化(F4)且功能受损(失代偿);19% 患有肝细胞癌 (HCC)。
核心方法
采用方差分析分析不同疾病阶段的 EQ-5D-3L 视觉模拟量表 (VAS) 评分和指数值、肝炎生活质量问卷 v2 领域评分以及疲劳严重程度量表 (FSS) 总分。
主要结果
方差分析显示,随着疾病阶段的恶化和严重程度的增加,EQ-5D-3L VAS 评分和指数值显着下降(所有 p < 0.0001)。肝炎生活质量问卷 v2 的所有领域评分(除了积极的健康状况)随着疾病的进展和症状严重程度的增加而恶化(所有 p ≤ 0.0001)。平均 FSS 总分也随着疾病恶化和症状严重程度增加而增加(均 p < 0.0001)。
解释与创新
丁型肝炎病毒感染显着影响患者的 HRQoL 和疲劳,特别是对于疾病后期的患者。该研究为不同疾病阶段的丁型肝炎病毒感染负担提供了新的现实证据。
科研与公卫意义
需要减缓疾病进展、减轻丁型肝炎病毒感染负担的治疗。研究结果强调了监测和解决 HDV 患者(尤其是晚期疾病患者)的 HRQoL 和疲劳问题的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究是一项横断面调查,限制了因果推断。由于现实世界的设计和大样本量,证据强度中等,但横截面性质和潜在的选择偏差可能会影响普遍性。

The Impact of Chronic Hepatitis D Virus Infection on Health-Related Quality of Life and Fatigue: A Real-World International Study.

Authors: Lampertico P, R G Gish, Reau N, Wedemeyer H, Buti M, Kaushik A, Yehoshua A, Burk C, Mirams L, Ellis H
Original abstract (English)
As little is known about the disease burden of chronic hepatitis D virus (HDV) infection, this study examined health-related quality of life (HRQoL) and fatigue at different disease stages. This was a real-world quantitative cross-sectional survey of patients in Germany, Italy, Spain and the USA. Patients were recruited via their treating physicians. Patients who had received bulevirtide for hepatitis B or treatment licensed for HDV were excluded. Of 211 patients, 41% were noncirrhotic (F0-F3); 20% had liver cirrhosis with normal function (compensated); 20% had liver cirrhosis (F4) with impaired function (decompensated); 19% had hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC). Mean (standard deviation; SD) age was 54.5 (11.5) years, 75% were male. ANOVA showed a significant decrease (indicating poorer HRQoL) in EQ-5D-3L visual analogue scale (VAS) scores and index values with worsening disease stage and increasing severity (all p < 0.0001). All domain scores of the Hepatitis Quality of Life Questionnaire v2 (except positive wellbeing) worsened with progressing disease and increasing symptom severity (all p ≤ 0.0001). Mean Fatigue Severity Scale (FSS) total scores also increased, indicating greater fatigue, with worsening of disease and increasing symptom severity (both p < 0.0001). HDV infection significantly impacts patients' HRQoL and fatigue, particularly for patients at later disease stages. Treatments that slow disease progression, reducing the burden of HDV infection, are needed.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
As little is known about the disease burden of chronic hepatitis D virus (HDV) infection, this study examined health-related quality of life (HRQoL) and fatigue at different disease stages.
Design and population
This was a real-world quantitative cross-sectional survey of patients in Germany, Italy, Spain and the USA. Patients were recruited via their treating physicians, and of 211 patients, 41% were noncirrhotic (F0-F3); 20% had liver cirrhosis with normal function (compensated); 20% had liver cirrhosis (F4) with impaired function (decompensated); 19% had hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC).
Core methods
ANOVA was used to analyze EQ-5D-3L visual analogue scale (VAS) scores and index values, Hepatitis Quality of Life Questionnaire v2 domain scores, and Fatigue Severity Scale (FSS) total scores across different disease stages.
Main results
ANOVA showed a significant decrease in EQ-5D-3L VAS scores and index values with worsening disease stage and increasing severity (all p < 0.0001). All domain scores of the Hepatitis Quality of Life Questionnaire v2 (except positive wellbeing) worsened with progressing disease and increasing symptom severity (all p ≤ 0.0001). Mean FSS total scores also increased with worsening of disease and increasing symptom severity (both p < 0.0001).
Interpretation and novelty
HDV infection significantly impacts patients' HRQoL and fatigue, particularly for patients at later disease stages. The study provides novel real-world evidence on the burden of HDV infection across different disease stages.
Scientific and public-health significance
Treatments that slow disease progression, reducing the burden of HDV infection, are needed. The findings highlight the importance of monitoring and addressing HRQoL and fatigue in HDV patients, especially those with advanced disease.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was a cross-sectional survey, which limits causal inference. The evidence strength is moderate due to the real-world design and large sample size, but the cross-sectional nature and potential selection bias may affect generalizability.

乙型肝炎病毒 Precore RNA 在反向中的非规范作用

Noncanonical Roles of Hepatitis B Virus Precore RNA in Reverse
作者： Mengyin Zhang
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 是一种部分双链 DNA 病毒,通过逆转录进行复制。HBV 核心启动子的转录产生两个 基因组 长度的 RNA:前基因组 RNA (pgRNA) 和前核心 RNA (pcRNA)。pcRNA 包含整个 pgRNA 序列以及 5' 端额外的 32 个核苷酸,其中包括前核心起始密码子,从而能够翻译前核心蛋白,即分泌的 HBV e 抗原 (HBeAg) 的前体。pgRNA 被翻译产生核心和聚合酶,并作为顺式作用 HBV 聚合酶逆转录的模板,而 pcRNA 长期以来被认为无法支持 HBV DNA 合成。虽然预核心对于病毒复制来说是可有可无的,但对于慢性乙型肝炎病毒感染却很重要。临床上,G1896A 突变 引入了一个提前终止密码子,废除了预核心翻译,在 HBeAg 阴性患者中非常普遍,并且与进行性肝病密切相关。尽管具有临床重要性,但 HBV pcRNA 的生物学和功能仍未完全了解,部分原因是传统的基于质粒的系统无法轻易区分 pgRNA 和 pcRNA,并产生高背景 DNA 信号,从而混淆了详细的机制研究。在这里,我采用了基于 HBV RNA 的转染系统来系统地检测野生型 pgRNA 和 pcRNA、废除前核心翻译的工程化 pcRNA 变体以及天然存在的 G1896A 突变 pcRNA。虽然野生型 pcRNA 支持低效的聚合酶生产和最低限度的 HBV DNA 合成,但前核心翻译缺陷突变体和 G1896A 突变体显示出高效的聚合酶生产和 HBV DNA 生产。值得注意的是,从 pcRNA 及其变体翻译而来的聚合酶优先以反式方式发挥作用,以反转录其他含有 ε 的 RNA,而从 pgRNA 翻译而来的聚合酶主要以顺式方式发挥作用。这些发现表明 pcRNA 是一种未被充分认识的具有反式作用的功能性 HBV 聚合酶来源。接下来我研究了这种机制对 HBV pgRNA 剪接变体的影响。HBV 剪接变异因其与慢性 HBV 感染患者肝病进展的相关性而受到越来越多的关注。最丰富的剪接变体 SP1 不支持功能性聚合酶表达,但在患者血清中检测到 SP1 衍生的 DNA,这意味着反式提供的聚合酶可能驱动 SP1 DNA 合成。通过共转染测定,我发现 pcRNA 衍生的聚合酶,特别是来自 G1896A 突变体的聚合酶,可以有效反式反转录 SP1 RNA,产生更多的 SP1 衍生 DNA,并为 SP1 衍生 DNA 的生物发生提供机制解释。最后,我发现在 e 近端区域内终止前核心翻译的 pcRNA 突变体表现出 DNA 生成受损,但反式活性增强,这与终止核糖体封闭 5’e 并限制顺式聚合酶结合的模型一致。为了进一步确定 precore 翻译如何损害 pcRNA 逆转录,我对包含 precore 区域的靶向起始和终止 突变 的 pcRNA 文库进行了系统扫描。该分析允许以氨基酸位置分辨率绘制依赖于翻译的 ε 破坏图。数据支持这样的模型:前核心 ORF 的翻译破坏了聚合酶结合、包装和逆转录起始所需的 5’ε RNA 元件。总而言之,这项工作表明 pcRNA 产生具有催化能力的 HBV 聚合酶,但持续的前核心翻译会破坏 5'e 元件并阻止 HBV 聚合酶顺式结合,从而使 pcRNA 衍生的聚合酶偏向于非经典 5'ε 含有 RNA 的反式作用逆转录。这些发现为 SP1 衍生 DNA 的生物发生提供了机制基础,并为 ε 的翻译耦合重塑如何塑造 HBV RNA 选择以进行包装和逆转录提供了新的见解。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在研究 乙型肝炎病毒 前核心 RNA 在逆转录中的非典型作用及其对病毒复制和疾病进展的影响。这一背景强调了了解 pcRNA 在 HBV 感染中的功能的重要性,特别是与 G1896A 突变 及其与肝病的关系相关的功能。
设计与对象
该研究利用基于 HBV RNA 的转染系统系统地检查野生型 pgRNA 和 pcRNA,以及工程化 pcRNA 变体和天然存在的 G1896A 突变 pcRNA。该研究是利用体外测定来评估聚合酶的产生和 DNA 合成的。
核心方法
该研究采用共转染测定来比较 pcRNA 和 pgRNA 的聚合酶产生和 DNA 合成。分析了跨前核心区域具有靶向起始和终止 突变 的 pcRNA 变体,以确定前核心翻译对逆转录的影响。
主要结果
野生型 pcRNA 支持低效的聚合酶生产和最低限度的 HBV DNA 合成,而前核心翻译缺陷突变体和 G1896A 突变体则显示出高效的聚合酶生产和 HBV DNA 生产。pcRNA 衍生的聚合酶优先以反式方式发挥作用,以逆转录其他含有 ε 的 RNA,这与主要以顺式方式发挥作用的 pgRNA 衍生聚合酶不同。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,pcRNA 是一种未被充分认识的具有反式作用的功能性 HBV 聚合酶来源。这挑战了之前的假设,即 pcRNA 不能支持 HBV DNA 合成,并为 SP1 衍生 DNA 的生物发生提供了机制解释。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果提供了关于前核心翻译如何影响 HBV RNA 包装和逆转录的新见解,这可以为未来病毒复制机制的研究提供信息,并有可能改进慢性 HBV 感染的治疗策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外测定,可能无法完全代表体内条件。证据是强有力的,因为它基于系统转染实验和 突变 分析,支持前核心翻译对逆转录影响的清晰模型。

Noncanonical Roles of Hepatitis B Virus Precore RNA in Reverse

Authors: Mengyin Zhang
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) is a partially double-stranded DNA virus that replicates through reverse transcription. Transcription from the HBV core promoter generates two genome-length RNAs: pregenomic RNA (pgRNA) and precore RNA (pcRNA). pcRNA contains the entire pgRNA sequence plus an additional 32 nucleotides at its 5’ end that include the precore start codon, enabling translation of the precore protein, the precursor of the secreted HBV e antigen (HBeAg). Whereas pgRNA is translated to produce core and polymerase and serves as the template for reverse transcription by cis-acting HBV polymerase, pcRNA has long been considered unable to support HBV DNA synthesis. Although precore is dispensable for viral replication, it is important for chronic HBV infection. Clinically, the G1896A mutation, which introduces a premature stop codon that abolishes precore translation, is highly prevalent among HBeAg-negative patients and is strongly associated with progressive liver disease. Despite this clinical importance, the biology and functions of HBV pcRNA remain incompletely understood, in part because conventional plasmid-based systems cannot readily distinguish pgRNA and pcRNA and generate high background DNA signals that confound detailed mechanistic studies. Here, I adapted an HBV RNA-based transfection system to systematically interrogate wild-type pgRNA and pcRNA, engineered pcRNA variants that abolish precore translation, and the naturally occurring G1896A mutant pcRNA. While wild-type pcRNA supported inefficient polymerase production and minimal HBV DNA synthesis, precore-translation-defective mutants and the G1896A mutant showed efficient polymerase production and HBV DNA production. Notably, polymerase translated from pcRNA and its variants preferentially acted in trans to reverse transcribe other ε-containing RNAs, in contrast to polymerase translated from pgRNA, which functioned predominantly in cis. These findings identify pcRNA as an underappreciated source of functional HBV polymerase with trans-acting activity. I next examined the implications of this mechanism for HBV pgRNA splice variants. HBV splicing variants have garnered increasing attention due to their association with liver disease progression in chronic HBV-infected patients. The most abundant splicing variant, SP1, does not support functional polymerase expression, yet SP1-derived DNA is detected in patient sera, implying that polymerase supplied in trans may drive SP1 DNA synthesis. Using co transfection assays, I showed that pcRNA-derived polymerases, particularly those from the G1896A mutant, efficiently reverse transcribe SP1 RNA in trans, yielding increased SP1-derived DNA and providing a mechanistic explanation for the biogenesis of SP1-derived DNA. Finally, I found that the pcRNA mutant terminating precore translation within the e proximal region exhibited impaired DNA production yet enhanced trans activity, consistent with a model in which a terminating ribosome occludes the 5’ e and limits polymerase binding in cis. To further define how precore translation impairs pcRNA reverse transcription, I performed a systematic scan of the pcRNA library containing targeted start and stop mutations across the precore region. This analysis allows mapping translation-dependent ε disruption at amino-acid position resolution. The data supports the model that translation of the precore ORF disrupts the 5’ ε RNA element required for polymerase binding, packaging, and initiation of reverse transcription. Altogether, this work demonstrates that pcRNA produces catalytically competent HBV polymerase, but that ongoing precore translation disrupts the 5’ e element and prevents HBV polymerase binding in cis, thereby biasing pcRNA-derived polymerase toward trans-acting reverse transcription of non-canonical 5’ ε-containing RNAs. These findings provide a mechanistic basis for the biogenesis of SP1-derived DNA and provide new insights into how translation-coupled remodeling of ε shapes HBV RNA selection for packaging and reverse transcription.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the noncanonical roles of Hepatitis B virus precore RNA in reverse transcription and its implications for viral replication and disease progression. The background highlights the importance of understanding pcRNA's function in HBV infection, particularly in relation to the G1896A mutation and its association with liver disease.
Design and population
The study utilized an HBV RNA-based transfection system to systematically examine wild-type pgRNA and pcRNA, as well as engineered pcRNA variants and the naturally occurring G1896A mutant pcRNA. The research was conducted using in vitro assays to assess polymerase production and DNA synthesis.
Core methods
The study employed co-transfection assays to compare polymerase production and DNA synthesis from pcRNA and pgRNA. pcRNA variants with targeted start and stop mutations across the precore region were analyzed to determine the effects of precore translation on reverse transcription.
Main results
Wild-type pcRNA supported inefficient polymerase production and minimal HBV DNA synthesis, whereas precore-translation-defective mutants and the G1896A mutant showed efficient polymerase production and HBV DNA production. pcRNA-derived polymerases preferentially acted in trans to reverse transcribe other ε-containing RNAs, unlike pgRNA-derived polymerases which functioned predominantly in cis.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings reveal that pcRNA is an underappreciated source of functional HBV polymerase with trans-acting activity. This challenges the previous assumption that pcRNA cannot support HBV DNA synthesis and provides a mechanistic explanation for the biogenesis of SP1-derived DNA.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results offer new insights into how precore translation affects HBV RNA packaging and reverse transcription, which could inform future studies on viral replication mechanisms and potentially lead to improved therapeutic strategies for chronic HBV infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro assays, which may not fully represent in vivo conditions. The evidence is strong, as it is based on systematic transfection experiments and mutation analysis, supporting a clear model of precore translation's impact on reverse transcription.

最初 HBV 血清阴性受者造血干细胞移植后感染事件 乙型肝炎病毒:四例

Incident Hepatitis B Virus Infection after Hematopoietic Stem Cell Transplantation in Initially HBV-Seronegative Recipients: Four Cases
作者： Dangui Chen
摘要中文翻译通常对慢性或已治愈 HBV 感染的受者进行造血干细胞移植 (HSCT) 后与 HBV 相关的并发症进行监测。我们描述了四名 HSCT 受者,他们在移植时 HBsAg 阴性、抗 HBc 阴性和 HBV DNA 阴性,但随后出现 HBsAg 阳性转化,并可检测到 HBV DNA。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查最初乙肝病毒血清阴性但后来在造血干细胞移植后出现乙肝病毒感染的受者的乙肝相关并发症。这很重要,因为通常在慢性或已治愈的 HBV 感染者中监测 HBV 相关并发症,但对血清阴性个体的急性感染了解有限。
设计与对象
这项研究描述了 4 例 HSCT 受者的病例,他们在移植时 HBsAg 阴性、抗 HBc 阴性和 HBV DNA 阴性,但后来出现 HBsAg 阳性转化,并可检测到 HBV DNA。该研究是在临床环境中进行的,重点关注人类受试者。
核心方法
该研究通过血清学检测和 HBV DNA 检测监测 4 名 HSCT 接受者的 HBV 感染状况。受者最初 HBsAg 阴性、抗 HBc 阴性和 HBV DNA 阴性,移植后评估了他们的 HBV 状况。
主要结果
四名最初 HBV 血清阴性的 HSCT 受者在移植后出现 HBsAg 阳性转化,并可检测到 HBV DNA。这表明乙型肝炎病毒感染可能发生在移植时血清阴性的个体中。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,在造血干细胞移植(HSCT)后,最初血清学阴性的受者可能发生乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染,这一潜在风险可能尚未被广泛认知。该发现有助于进一步理解移植环境中HBV相关并发症的发生机制。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果对 监测 和移植受者(尤其是最初血清阴性的受者)的 HBV 感染管理具有影响。他们强调,即使是之前没有接触过乙肝病毒的个体,也需要持续监测。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于四个案例,可能无法代表更广泛的人群。然而,证据是强有力的,因为它是基于对研究个体的 HBV 血清转化和 DNA 检测的直接观察。

Incident Hepatitis B Virus Infection after Hematopoietic Stem Cell Transplantation in Initially HBV-Seronegative Recipients: Four Cases

Authors: Dangui Chen
Original abstract (English)
HBV-related complications after hematopoietic stem cell transplantation (HSCT) are generally monitored in recipients with chronic or resolved HBV infection. We describe four HSCT recipients who were HBsAg-negative, anti-HBc-negative, and HBV DNA-negative at transplantation but subsequently developed HBsAg-positive conversion with detectable HBV DNA.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate HBV-related complications in recipients who were initially HBV-seronegative but later developed HBV infection after hematopoietic stem cell transplantation. This is important because HBV-related complications are generally monitored in recipients with chronic or resolved HBV infection, but there is limited knowledge about acute infections in seronegative individuals.
Design and population
This study describes four cases of HSCT recipients who were HBsAg-negative, anti-HBc-negative, and HBV DNA-negative at transplantation but later developed HBsAg-positive conversion with detectable HBV DNA. The study was conducted in a clinical setting and focused on human recipients.
Core methods
The study involved monitoring four HSCT recipients for HBV infection status through serological testing and HBV DNA detection. The recipients were initially HBsAg-negative, anti-HBc-negative, and HBV DNA-negative, and their HBV status was assessed post-transplantation.
Main results
Four HSCT recipients who were initially HBV-seronegative developed HBsAg-positive conversion with detectable HBV DNA after transplantation. This indicates that HBV infection can occur in individuals who were seronegative at the time of transplantation.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that HBV infection can develop in initially seronegative recipients following HSCT, highlighting a potential risk that may not be widely recognized. This adds to the understanding of HBV-related complications in transplant settings.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results have implications for the surveillance and management of HBV infection in transplant recipients, particularly those who are initially seronegative. They emphasize the need for continued monitoring even in individuals without prior HBV exposure.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to four cases, which may not be representative of the broader population. However, the evidence is strong as it is based on direct observations of HBV seroconversion and DNA detection in the studied individuals.

马尿酸在 慢性乙型肝炎 复杂的 MASLD 中依赖 TLR2 促进 NK 细胞活化

Hippuric acid contributes to NK-cell activation with dependence on TLR2 in chronic hepatitis B complicated by MASLD
作者： Luyuan Zhang, Anqi Li, Ke Xu, Siyang Tao, Meijuan Zheng
摘要中文翻译慢性乙型肝炎 和代谢功能障碍相关的脂肪性肝病 (CHB-MASLD) 的共存与加速纤维化进展和较差的临床结果有关,但免疫病理学机制仍不完全清楚。在本研究中,我们观察到 CHB-MASLD 患者表现出外周自然杀伤 (NK) 细胞频率和激活显着增加,转录组分析显示炎症效应基因特征的富集。值得注意的是,发现 CHB-MASLD 患者的 NK 细胞中 TLR2 表达上调,并且表达 TLR2 的 NK 细胞的频率与代谢参数和肝脏硬度相关。在体外,代谢应激模型增加了 TLR2 表达并增强了 NK 细胞细胞因子的产生。非靶向血浆代谢组学将马尿酸确定为 CHB-MASLD 中富集的候选代谢物,其丰度与代谢参数和肝脏硬度相关。马尿酸参与促进 NK 细胞活化,这种作用可通过 TLR2 阻断部分减弱。此外,与马尿酸刺激的 NK 细胞共培养与 LX-2 细胞中激活相关读数的增加有关,而 TLR2 阻断部分减弱了这种读数。总的来说,这些发现支持 CHB-MASLD 中代谢功能障碍、外周 NK 细胞激活和部分依赖 TLR2 的马尿酸反应之间的免疫代谢联系,为 CHB-MASLD 的免疫病理学提供了新的见解。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究探讨了为什么患有代谢功能障碍相关脂肪肝病 (MASLD) 的 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 患者的临床结果较差,并指出这种合并症背后的免疫病理学机制仍不完全清楚。
设计与对象
该研究检查了患有 MASLD 的 CHB 患者,以表征外周自然杀伤 (NK) 细胞反应和相关代谢因素。
核心方法
对 CHB-MASLD 患者的外周血 NK 细胞进行频率和激活分析,并进行转录组分析以鉴定基因特征。体外代谢应激模型评估了 TLR2 表达和 NK 细胞细胞因子的产生,而非靶向血浆代谢组学则将马尿酸确定为候选代谢物。
主要结果
CHB-MASLD 患者表现出外周 NK 细胞频率增加以及炎症效应基因特征的激活。TLR2 在 NK 细胞中表达上调,并与代谢参数和肝脏硬度相关。马尿酸丰度升高并与这些参数相关。在体外,代谢应激增加了 TLR2 表达和 NK 细胞细胞因子的产生,马尿酸促进了 NK 细胞活化,但 TLR2 阻断部分减弱了这种作用。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,CHB-MASLD 中 MASLD 相关的代谢功能障碍、NK 细胞激活和 TLR2 依赖性马尿酸反应之间存在免疫代谢联系,为这种合并症的免疫病理学提供了新的机制见解。
科研与公卫意义
该研究将马尿酸和 TLR2 确定为潜在的生物标志物或治疗靶点,用于监测 CHB-MASLD 患者的疾病进展和调节 免疫应答。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于摘要报告,没有详细的方法、样本量或统计分析,这限制了结论的强度及其普遍性。

Hippuric acid contributes to NK-cell activation with dependence on TLR2 in chronic hepatitis B complicated by MASLD

Authors: Luyuan Zhang, Anqi Li, Ke Xu, Siyang Tao, Meijuan Zheng
Original abstract (English)
The coexistence of chronic hepatitis B and metabolic dysfunction-associated steatotic liver disease (CHB-MASLD) has been linked to accelerated fibrosis progression and less favorable clinical outcomes, but the immunopathological mechanisms remain incompletely understood. In the present study, we observed that CHB-MASLD patients exhibited significantly increased peripheral natural killer (NK) cell frequency and activation, with transcriptomic analysis revealing enrichment of inflammatory effector gene signatures. Notably, TLR2 was found to be upregulated in NK cells from CHB-MASLD patients and the frequency of TLR2-expressing NK cells correlated with metabolic parameters and liver stiffness. In vitro , metabolic-stress modeling increased TLR2 expression and enhanced NK-cell cytokine production. Untargeted plasma metabolomics identified hippuric acid as a candidate metabolite enriched in CHB-MASLD, and its abundance was associated with metabolic parameters and liver stiffness. Hippuric acid was involved in promoting NK-cell activation, and this effect was partially attenuated by TLR2 blockade. Furthermore, co-culture with hippuric acid-stimulated NK cells was associated with increased activation-related readouts in LX-2 cells, which were partially attenuated by TLR2 blockade. Collectively, these findings support an immunometabolic link among metabolic dysfunction, peripheral NK-cell activation, and a partially TLR2-dependent response to hippuric acid in CHB-MASLD, offering new insights into the immunopathology of CHB-MASLD.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses why chronic hepatitis B (CHB) patients with metabolic dysfunction-associated steatotic liver disease (MASLD) experience worse clinical outcomes, noting that immunopathological mechanisms underlying this comorbidity remain incompletely understood.
Design and population
The study examined patients with CHB complicated by MASLD to characterize peripheral natural killer (NK) cell responses and associated metabolic factors.
Core methods
Peripheral blood NK cells from CHB-MASLD patients were analyzed for frequency and activation, and transcriptomic profiling was performed to identify gene signatures. Metabolic-stress modeling in vitro assessed TLR2 expression and NK-cell cytokine production, while untargeted plasma metabolomics identified hippuric acid as a candidate metabolite.
Main results
CHB-MASLD patients showed increased peripheral NK cell frequency and activation with inflammatory effector gene signatures. TLR2 was upregulated in NK cells and correlated with metabolic parameters and liver stiffness. Hippuric acid abundance was elevated and associated with these parameters. In vitro, metabolic stress increased TLR2 expression and NK-cell cytokine production, and hippuric acid promoted NK-cell activation, partially attenuated by TLR2 blockade.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest an immunometabolic link between MASLD-related metabolic dysfunction, NK-cell activation, and TLR2-dependent responses to hippuric acid in CHB-MASLD, providing new mechanistic insights into the immunopathology of this comorbidity.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study identifies hippuric acid and TLR2 as potential biomarkers or therapeutic targets for monitoring disease progression and modulating immune responses in CHB-MASLD patients.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to an abstract-only report without detailed methods, sample sizes, or statistical analyses, which constrains the strength of the conclusions and their generalizability.

HBV 感染-肝硬化连续过程中的调节性 T 细胞重塑

Regulatory T-cell remodeling across the HBV infection–cirrhosis continuum
作者： Yudan Fu, Yi Wang, Jiqi Ouyang, Wenliang Lv
摘要中文翻译调节性T细胞(Treg)在乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染和肝硬化过程中具有不同的功能。调节性 T 细胞 (Treg) 通常会限制急性 HBV 感染中过度的免疫介导的肝损伤,但也可能抑制 抗病毒药物 免疫。在慢性感染期间,肝内大量富集的Treg细胞会抑制HBV特异性效应T细胞反应,维持抗病毒药物免疫低反应和病毒持续存在,并限制慢性坏死性炎症损伤。随着 HBV 相关肝纤维化程度的进展,Treg 细胞和 Th17 细胞都相对缺乏。Treg细胞不仅能抑制纤维化,还能抑制纤维化。它们还与HSC建立的免疫纤维化微环境相互关联,炎症、HSC激活、细胞外基质沉积和Treg/Th17失衡都是相互关联的。HBV 相关肝硬化中的 Treg 具有高度异质性、区室分布、功能重编程,并加入了肝内免疫抑制纤维化网络,其中包括骨髓细胞、HSC、自然杀伤细胞、CD8+ T 细胞和 Th17 细胞。本文将介绍这些时期逐渐改变过程中Treg丰度、表型、分布和功能的变化,以及微环境因素、Treg/Th17失调和具体治疗方案。因此,在 HBV 相关肝病中调节 Treg 活性的效果是环境依赖性和阶段特异性的,而不是统一的增强或抑制。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究调查了调节性 T 细胞 (Treg) 在 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染发展为肝硬化的过程中的作用,强调需要了解 Tregs 如何促进免疫调节和疾病结果。
设计与对象
该研究检查了 HBV 感染和肝硬化不同阶段 Treg 丰度、表型、分布和功能的变化,重点关注人类 乙型肝炎病毒 及其相关疾病状态。
核心方法
该研究通过免疫表型分析、功能测定以及检查 HBV 感染和肝硬化过程中 Treg 与肝脏微环境中其他免疫细胞和纤维化细胞的相互作用来分析 Treg 特征。
主要结果
随着HBV相关肝纤维化的进展,Treg细胞和Th17细胞都相对缺乏,并且肝硬化中的Treg细胞具有高度异质性、区室分布和功能重编程。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,HBV 相关肝病中的 Treg 活性具有背景依赖性和阶段特异性,这表明它们的作用根据疾病阶段而变化,而不是一致抑制或增强。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果提供了对 HBV 相关肝硬化中免疫纤维化网络的深入了解,这可以为未来的靶向治疗研究和更精确的诊断或监测工具的开发提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅基于抽象证据,这限制了分析的深度,但研究结果基于报告的乙型肝炎病毒感染和肝硬化背景,支持适度的置信度评估。

Regulatory T-cell remodeling across the HBV infection–cirrhosis continuum

Authors: Yudan Fu, Yi Wang, Jiqi Ouyang, Wenliang Lv
Original abstract (English)
Regulatory T cells (Tregs) have varied functions in the course of hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection and cirrhosis. Regulatory T cells (Tregs) generally restrict excessive immune-mediated liver injury in acute HBV infection but may also inhibit antiviral immunity. During chronic infection, expanded and intrahepatically enriched Tregs suppress HBV-specific effector T-cell responses, maintain antiviral immune hyporesponsiveness and viral persistence, and limit chronic necroinflammatory injury. As the extent of HBV-related liver fibrosis progresses, there is a relative deficiency in both Tregs and Th17 cells. Treg cells are not only inhibitory to fibrosis; they are also interconnected with the immune-fibrotic microenvironment established by HSCs, and inflammation, HSC activation, extracellular matrix deposition and Treg/Th17 imbalance are all interrelated. Tregs in HBV-associated cirrhosis are highly heterogeneous, compartmentally distributed, functionally reprogrammed, and have joined an intrahepatic immunosuppressive-fibrotic network that includes myeloid cells, HSCs, natural killer cells, CD8+ T cells and Th17 cells. This paper will introduce modifications in Treg abundance, phenotype, distribution and function during the progressive alteration of these periods, as well as microenvironmental factors, Treg/Th17 dysregulation and specific therapeutic options. Therefore, the effect of regulation of Treg activity in HBV-associated liver disease is context-dependent and stage-specific, not uniform enhancement or suppression.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study investigates the role of regulatory T cells (Tregs) in the progression of hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection to cirrhosis, highlighting the need to understand how Tregs contribute to immune regulation and disease outcomes.
Design and population
The study examines the changes in Treg abundance, phenotype, distribution and function across different stages of HBV infection and cirrhosis, focusing on the human hepatitis B virus and its associated disease states.
Core methods
The research analyzes Treg characteristics through immunophenotyping, functional assays, and examination of their interactions with other immune and fibrotic cells in the liver microenvironment during HBV infection and cirrhosis.
Main results
As HBV-related liver fibrosis progresses, there is a relative deficiency in both Tregs and Th17 cells, and Tregs in cirrhosis are highly heterogeneous, compartmentally distributed, and functionally reprogrammed.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that Treg activity in HBV-associated liver disease is context-dependent and stage-specific, indicating that their role varies depending on the disease phase rather than being uniformly suppressive or enhancing.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide insights into the immune-fibrotic network in HBV-related cirrhosis, which could inform future studies on targeted therapies and the development of more precise diagnostic or monitoring tools.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on abstract-only evidence, which limits the depth of analysis, but the findings are grounded in the reported context of HBV infection and cirrhosis, supporting a moderate confidence assessment.

FBXO11与FBXO32协同激活先天免疫对乙型肝炎病毒的应答,依赖NEDD8的方式

FBXO11 and FBXO32 synergistically activate innate immunity against hepatitis B virus in a NEDD8-dependent manner
作者： Liqiang Li, Shun Tao, Yunfei Zhang, Zihan Zeng, Jun Zhang
摘要中文翻译背景和客观 I 型干扰素 (IFN-I) 信号传导对于 抗病毒药物 先天免疫至关重要。F-box 蛋白介导的泛素化调节该途径。FBXO11 通过 TRAF3 K63 连接的泛素化正向调节 IFN-I 信号传导,但其他 F-box 蛋白在 HBV 感染期间是否具有协同作用仍不清楚。本研究探讨了 FBXO32 在 HBV 感染中的表达和功能,以及 FBXO11 和 FBXO32 是否通过 NEDD8 依赖性机制协同激活先天免疫。方法 HBcAg 和 HBsAg 免疫荧光 (IF) 染色证实 HBV 感染成功。对 HBV 感染的 HepG2-NTCP 细胞中 14 个 FBXO 家族成员进行 qRT-PCR 筛查,发现 FBXO11 和 FBXO32 上调最显着。进行了细胞功能丧失和功能获得研究(单敲除或双敲除以及过表达),然后对 抗病毒药物 基因进行 qRT−PCR 和蛋白质印迹分析。从机制上讲,免疫共沉淀用于评估 FBXO11/FBXO32 与 TRAF3、TBK1 和 IRF3 的相互作用,并应用 NEDD8 抑制剂 MLN4924 来确认 NEDD8 依赖性。在体内,为了区分子代病毒DNA和注射的质粒,进行了对照实验,其中检测到了pAAV/HBV1.2质粒的媒介主链序列以及HBV基因。随后,在单敲除或双敲除或过表达 FBXO11 和 FBXO32 的 C57BL/6 小鼠中进行 pAAV/HBV1.2 质粒的尾静脉注射。感染后 0、6、12、24 和 48 小时收集血清和肝脏样本,以定量 HBV DNA (qPCR)、HBsAg、HBeAg 和 HBcAg (ELISA)。通过 H&E 染色评估肝脏病理学,并对肝组织切片进行 HBcAg、ISG56 和 CXCL10 的免疫组织化学 (IHC)。结果 HBV感染后FBXO11和FBXO32显着上调。双敲除对 抗病毒药物 基因的抑制比单敲除更强烈。从机制上讲,FBXO11和FBXO32协同增强TRAF3依赖NEDD8的K63连接泛素化,促进TRAF3-TBK1-IRF3复合物组装。对照实验证实检测到的 HBV DNA 主要代表子代病毒而不是输入质粒。基于这个经过验证的系统,FBXO11和FBXO32的双重敲除加剧了肝脏病理学并升高了病毒抗原,而双重过表达则减轻了病理学并减少了抗原。结论 FBXO11和FBXO32通过促进NEDD8依赖性K63连接的TRAF3泛素化、抑制HBV复制和减轻免疫病理,协同增强抗病毒药物先天免疫。这些发现揭示了协同抗乙型肝炎轴并提供了潜在的治疗策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨 FBXO11 和 FBXO32 在 抗病毒药物 针对 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 的先天免疫中的作用,以及它们是否通过 NEDD8 依赖性机制发挥协同作用。
设计与对象
该研究在感染 HBV 的 HepG2-NTCP 细胞中进行了细胞功能丧失和功能获得实验,并在对 FBXO11 和 FBXO32 进行了基因修饰的 C57BL/6 小鼠中进行了体内实验。
核心方法
免疫荧光染色证实了 HBV 感染,qRT-PCR 鉴定了上调的 FBXO 家族成员,并使用单或双敲除和过表达进行了功能丧失和功能获得研究。使用免疫共沉淀和 NEDD8 抑制剂 MLN4924 来评估相互作用和 NEDD8 依赖性。
主要结果
HBV 感染显着上调 FBXO11 和 FBXO32。双敲除比单敲除更强烈地抑制 抗病毒药物 基因,而双过表达则减轻了肝脏病理并减少了病毒抗原。
解释与创新
作者解释说,FBXO11和FBXO32通过促进NEDD8依赖性K63连接的TRAF3泛素化来协同增强抗病毒药物先天免疫,从而抑制HBV复制并减轻免疫病理。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现提供了对 抗病毒药物 抗 HBV 免疫的分子机制的见解,并提出了针对 FBXO11 和 FBXO32 的潜在治疗策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外和体内模型,证据基于经过验证的系统,支持适度的置信度评估。

FBXO11 and FBXO32 synergistically activate innate immunity against hepatitis B virus in a NEDD8-dependent manner

Authors: Liqiang Li, Shun Tao, Yunfei Zhang, Zihan Zeng, Jun Zhang
Original abstract (English)
Background and objective Type I interferon (IFN-I) signaling is crucial for antiviral innate immunity. F-box protein-mediated ubiquitination regulates this pathway. FBXO11 positively regulates IFN-I signaling via TRAF3 K63-linked ubiquitination, but whether other F-box proteins act synergistically during HBV infection remains unknown. This study investigates FBXO32 expression and function in HBV infection, and whether FBXO11 and FBXO32 synergistically activate innate immunity through a NEDD8-dependent mechanism. Methods Immunofluorescence (IF) staining for HBcAg and HBsAg confirmed successful HBV infection. qRT-PCR screening of 14 FBXO family members in HBV-infected HepG2-NTCP cells identified FBXO11 and FBXO32 as the most markedly upregulated. Cellular loss- and gain-of-function studies (single or double knockout and overexpression) were performed, followed by qRT−PCR and western blot analysis of antiviral genes. Mechanistically, co-immunoprecipitation was used to assess FBXO11/FBXO32 interactions with TRAF3, TBK1, and IRF3, and the NEDD8 inhibitor MLN4924 was applied to confirm NEDD8 dependence. In vivo , to distinguish progeny viral DNA from the injected plasmid, a control experiment was performed in which the vector backbone sequence of the pAAV/HBV1.2 plasmid was detected alongside the HBV gene. Subsequently, hydrodynamic tail-vein injection of the pAAV/HBV1.2 plasmid was performed in C57BL/6 mice with single or double knockout or overexpression of FBXO11 and FBXO32.Serum and liver samples were collected at 0, 6, 12, 24, and 48 hours post-infection to quantify HBV DNA (qPCR), HBsAg, HBeAg and HBcAg (ELISA). Liver pathology was evaluated by H&E staining, and immunohistochemistry (IHC) for HBcAg, ISG56 and CXCL10 was performed on liver tissue sections. Results HBV infection markedly upregulated FBXO11 and FBXO32. Double knockout suppressed antiviral genes more strongly than single knockout. Mechanistically, FBXO11 and FBXO32 cooperatively enhanced NEDD8-dependent K63-linked ubiquitination of TRAF3, promoting TRAF3-TBK1-IRF3 complex assembly. Control experiments confirmed that the detected HBV DNA predominantly represented progeny virus rather than input plasmid. Based on this validated system, double knockout of FBXO11 and FBXO32 exacerbated liver pathology and elevated viral antigens, whereas double overexpression alleviated pathology and reduced antigens. Conclusion FBXO11 and FBXO32 synergistically enhance antiviral innate immunity by promoting NEDD8-dependent K63-linked TRAF3 ubiquitination, suppressing HBV replication and alleviating immunopathology. These findings reveal a synergistic anti-HBV axis and provide a potential therapeutic strategy.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the role of FBXO11 and FBXO32 in antiviral innate immunity against hepatitis B virus (HBV) and whether they act synergistically through a NEDD8-dependent mechanism.
Design and population
The study used cellular loss- and gain-of-function experiments in HepG2-NTCP cells infected with HBV, and in vivo experiments in C57BL/6 mice with genetic modifications to FBXO11 and FBXO32.
Core methods
Immunofluorescence staining confirmed HBV infection, qRT-PCR identified upregulated FBXO family members, and loss- and gain-of-function studies were conducted using single or double knockout and overexpression. Co-immunoprecipitation and NEDD8 inhibitor MLN4924 were used to assess interactions and NEDD8 dependence.
Main results
HBV infection significantly upregulated FBXO11 and FBXO32. Double knockout suppressed antiviral genes more strongly than single knockout, while double overexpression alleviated liver pathology and reduced viral antigens.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that FBXO11 and FBXO32 synergistically enhance antiviral innate immunity by promoting NEDD8-dependent K63-linked TRAF3 ubiquitination, which suppresses HBV replication and alleviates immunopathology.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings provide insights into the molecular mechanisms of antiviral immunity against HBV and suggest potential therapeutic strategies targeting FBXO11 and FBXO32.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro and in vivo models, and the evidence is based on a validated system, supporting a moderate confidence assessment.

喀麦隆 患病率 感染的 患病率:系统回顾和荟萃分析。

Prevalence of Hepatitis B Virus Infection in Cameroon: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis.
作者： Iya Maliki, Edward J D Webb, Rose Armelle Ada, Bryony Dawkins, Aaron G Lim
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本研究旨在提供喀麦隆 HBV 患病率 的最新估计值。该综述的重点是人类 乙型肝炎病毒 感染、基因型和疾病,重点是一般人群和亚人群中的 患病率。核心问题是确定喀麦隆当前 HBV 感染的 患病率 并确定导致 患病率 率变异的因素。
证据基础与方法
证据基础包括报告一般人群和亚人群中 HBV 表面抗原 (HBsAg) 患病率 的研究。使用随机效应荟萃分析生成 HBsAg 患病率 总体和人群亚组的汇总估计值。使用异质性指数(I 2 )评估异质性。使用乔安娜布里格斯研究所关键评估工具评估质量。在 1236 条记录中,荟萃分析纳入了 92 篇论文和 100 项研究。
共识与结论
喀麦隆 HBV 总体 患病率 为 9.98%(95% 置信区间 [CI] 9.00-11.02),异质性较高 (I 2 = 98.10%)。相对较低风险和较高风险人群的 患病率 分别为 8.94% (95% CI 7.84-10.11) 和 12.70% (95% CI 10.28-15.32)。总体而言,喀麦隆的 HBV 患病率 仍然很高。
争议与缺口
在亚组分析中,我们发现性别、地理区域(地区)、人群风险群体和研究设计是异质性的主要来源。所提供的证据并未报告喀麦隆 HBV 的具体基因型或其在亚人群中的分布。所提供的证据并未报告喀麦隆 HBV 高 患病率 的潜在机制。
科研与实践启示
我们的研究结果表明,迫切需要采取健康干预措施来阻止疾病的传播,特别需要关注男性和 患病率 最高的地区。所提供的证据并未报告喀麦隆应对乙型肝炎的具体研究重点或政策建议。

Prevalence of Hepatitis B Virus Infection in Cameroon: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis.

Authors: Iya Maliki, Edward J D Webb, Rose Armelle Ada, Bryony Dawkins, Aaron G Lim
Original abstract (English)
Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection is a global public health problem, and many regions of Sub-Saharan Africa are disproportionately affected, including Cameroon, where prevalence has previously been shown to be high. This study aimed to provide up-to-date estimates of HBV prevalence in Cameroon. We searched Medline, Global Health, Embase, Web of Science, Cochrane Library, Africa Index Medicus and the Journal of Health Science and Disease at the University of Yaounde. We included studies reporting HBV surface antigen (HBsAg) prevalence in the general population and subpopulations. A random-effects meta-analysis was used to generate pooled estimates of HBsAg prevalence, overall and for population subgroups including those considered at relatively higher-risk and relatively lower-risk of HBV infection. Heterogeneity was assessed using the index of heterogeneity (I 2 ). Quality was assessed using the Joanna Briggs Institute critical appraisal tool. Out of 1236 records, 92 papers with 100 studies were included in the meta-analysis. Overall prevalence of HBV in Cameroon was 9.98% (95% confidence interval [CI] 9.00-11.02) with high heterogeneity (I 2 = 98.10%). The prevalences for relatively lower-risk and higher-risk populations were8.94% (95% CI 7.84-10.11) and 12.70% (95% CI 10.28-15.32), respectively. In subgroup analyses, we found that sex, geographical area (regions), population risk groups and study design were the main sources of heterogeneity. Overall, HBV prevalence remains high in Cameroon. Our findings suggest an urgent need for health interventions to halt the spread of the disease, with particular attention needed for males and regions with the highest prevalence.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This study aimed to provide up-to-date estimates of HBV prevalence in Cameroon. The review focused on human hepatitis B virus infection, genotypes and disease, with an emphasis on prevalence in the general population and subpopulations. The central question was to determine the current prevalence of HBV infection in Cameroon and identify factors contributing to variability in prevalence rates.
Evidence base and method
The evidence base included studies reporting HBV surface antigen (HBsAg) prevalence in the general population and subpopulations. A random-effects meta-analysis was used to generate pooled estimates of HBsAg prevalence, overall and for population subgroups. Heterogeneity was assessed using the index of heterogeneity (I 2 ). Quality was assessed using the Joanna Briggs Institute critical appraisal tool. Out of 1236 records, 92 papers with 100 studies were included in the meta-analysis.
Consensus and conclusions
Overall prevalence of HBV in Cameroon was 9.98% (95% confidence interval [CI] 9.00-11.02) with high heterogeneity (I 2 = 98.10%). The prevalences for relatively lower-risk and higher-risk populations were 8.94% (95% CI 7.84-10.11) and 12.70% (95% CI 10.28-15.32), respectively. Overall, HBV prevalence remains high in Cameroon.
Controversies and gaps
In subgroup analyses, we found that sex, geographical area (regions), population risk groups and study design were the main sources of heterogeneity. The supplied evidence does not report on the specific genotypes of HBV in Cameroon or their distribution across subpopulations. The supplied evidence does not report on the mechanisms underlying the high prevalence of HBV in Cameroon.
Research and practice implications
Our findings suggest an urgent need for health interventions to halt the spread of the disease, with particular attention needed for males and regions with the highest prevalence. The supplied evidence does not report on specific research priorities or policy recommendations for addressing HBV in Cameroon.
