乙型肝炎的障碍 治疗:加拿大乙型肝炎网络进行的多中心横断面登记和调查研究。

Barriers to Hepatitis B Treatment: A Multicentre Cross-Sectional Registry and Survey Study by the Canadian Hepatitis B Network.
作者： Hin Hin Ko, Jordyn Vienneau, Curtis L Cooper, Pam Crotty, Stephen E Congly, Mayur Brahmania, Alnoor Ramji, Erin Kelly, Angela M Crawley, Karen Doucette
摘要中文翻译背景与目的:慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 仍然是全世界肝硬化和肝细胞癌 (HCC) 的主要原因,但即使在资源丰富的环境中,治疗 的摄取也不是最佳的。这项泛加拿大研究旨在量化加拿大乙型肝炎网络 (CanHepB) 注册中心内的 治疗 差距,比较符合 治疗 资格但未经治疗的患者与正在接受治疗的患者的特征,并评估患者和专家对 治疗 障碍的看法。方法:对 2018 年 1 月至 2024 年 11 月期间加拿大 18 个乙型肝炎专科诊所的 1983 名成人慢性乙型肝炎患者进行横断面回顾性分析。治疗 资格是根据 2018 年加拿大肝脏研究协会指南确定的。一项匿名调查记录了患者报告的障碍和观点 (n = 191),而另一项调查评估了专家的看法 (n = 54)。结果:在 881 名未经治疗的患者中,46.4% (n = 409) 符合 治疗 资格。与 治疗 组 (n = 1087) 相比,符合 治疗 条件但未接受治疗的患者更年轻(中位年龄 47.3 岁 vs. 54.5 岁),女性更常见(54.2% vs. 39.7%),并且更有可能是黑人/非洲/加勒比裔(18.2% vs. 他们的 患病率 较低)肝硬化(2.4% vs. 20.3%)、肝癌(0.7% vs. 9.3%)和高血压(17.1% vs. 22.9%),但脂肪变性肝病(36.4% vs. 24.6%)和血脂异常(14.7% vs. 未治疗的受访者)的 患病率 较高。在未治疗的受访者中,担忧包括副作用、需要长期治疗以及缺乏治疗。相反,专家认为成本是主要障碍,而副作用是最不令人担忧的。这项全国性研究揭示了慢性乙型肝炎护理中存在显着的 治疗 差距,这是由人口差异以及患者关注点和临床医生认知之间的不匹配造成的。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述检查了资源丰富的环境下成人中的人类 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (HBV) 感染,重点关注 治疗 资格、未经治疗的患者之间的人口统计差异,以及患者报告的障碍与专家对 HBV 治疗的看法之间的不匹配。
证据基础与方法
该研究对 2018 年 1 月至 2024 年 11 月期间在加拿大 18 个专科诊所入组的 1983 名患有 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 的成年人进行了横断面回顾性分析,使用 2018 年加拿大肝脏研究协会指南来确定 治疗 的资格。
共识与结论
在 881 名未经治疗的患者中,46.4% (n=409) 符合 治疗 条件,与接受治疗的患者相比,未经治疗的 治疗 患者更年轻,女性居多,而且更有可能是黑人/非洲/加勒比裔。未经治疗的患者肝硬化、肝癌和高血压的 患病率 较低,但脂肪肝疾病和血脂异常的 患病率 较高。
争议与缺口
患者对副作用、长期治疗和缺乏治疗选择的担忧与专家将成本确定为主要障碍和副作用作为最不关心的问题形成鲜明对比,凸显了对 HBV 治疗 的认知障碍的不匹配。
科研与实践启示
该研究强调需要采取有针对性的干预措施来解决 HBV 治疗 摄取的人口差异,并协调患者和专家对改善资源丰富环境中慢性乙型肝炎护理障碍的看法。

Barriers to Hepatitis B Treatment: A Multicentre Cross-Sectional Registry and Survey Study by the Canadian Hepatitis B Network.

Authors: Hin Hin Ko, Jordyn Vienneau, Curtis L Cooper, Pam Crotty, Stephen E Congly, Mayur Brahmania, Alnoor Ramji, Erin Kelly, Angela M Crawley, Karen Doucette
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND & AIMS: Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) remains a leading cause of cirrhosis and hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) worldwide, yet treatment uptake is suboptimal even in high-resource settings. This pan-Canadian study aimed to quantify treatment gaps within the Canadian Hepatitis B Network (CanHepB) Registry, compare characteristics of treatment-eligible but untreated patients to those receiving therapy, and assess patient and specialist perspectives on barriers to treatment. METHODS: A cross-sectional, retrospective analysis of 1983 adults with CHB followed in 18 Canadian hepatitis B specialty clinics between January 2018 and November 2024. Treatment eligibility was determined using the 2018 Canadian Association for the Study of the Liver guidelines. An anonymous survey captured patient-reported barriers and perspectives (n = 191), while a separate survey evaluated specialist perceptions (n = 54). RESULTS: Among 881 untreated patients, 46.4% (n = 409) were treatment eligible. Compared with the treatment group (n = 1087), the treatment-eligible but untreated patients were younger (median 47.3 vs. 54.5 years), more frequently female (54.2% vs. 39.7%), and more likely to be of Black/African/Caribbean origin (18.2% vs. They had a lower prevalence of cirrhosis (2.4% vs. 20.3%), HCC (0.7% vs. 9.3%), and hypertension (17.1% vs. 22.9%), but a higher prevalence of steatotic liver disease (36.4% vs. 24.6%) and dyslipidemia (14.7% vs. Among untreated respondents, concerns included side effects, the need for long-term therapy, and lack of curative options. Conversely, specialists identified cost as the primary barrier and side effects as the least concerning. CONCLUSION: This nationwide study reveals a significant treatment gap in CHB care, driven by demographic disparities and a mismatch between patient concerns and clinician perceptions.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines human chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection in adults within a high-resource setting, focusing on treatment eligibility, demographic disparities among untreated patients, and the mismatch between patient-reported barriers and specialist perceptions regarding HBV therapy.
Evidence base and method
The study is a cross-sectional, retrospective analysis of 1983 adults with chronic hepatitis B (CHB) enrolled in 18 Canadian specialty clinics from January 2018 to November 2024, using the 2018 Canadian Association for the Study of the Liver guidelines to determine treatment eligibility.
Consensus and conclusions
Among 881 untreated patients, 46.4% (n=409) were treatment eligible, with untreated treatment-eligible patients being younger, more frequently female, and more likely to be of Black/African/Caribbean origin compared to treated patients. Untreated patients had lower prevalence of cirrhosis, HCC, and hypertension but higher prevalence of steatotic liver disease and dyslipidemia.
Controversies and gaps
Patient concerns about side effects, long-term therapy, and lack of curative options contrasted with specialists' identification of cost as the primary barrier and side effects as the least concerning, highlighting a mismatch in perceived barriers to HBV treatment.
Research and practice implications
The study underscores the need for targeted interventions to address demographic disparities in HBV treatment uptake and to align patient and specialist perspectives on barriers to improve CHB care in high-resource settings.

乙型肝炎患者接受英国二级护理的情况:范围界定审查。

Attendance Among People With Hepatitis B in UK Secondary Care: A Scoping Review.
作者： Emma Victoria Shiel, Bini George, Arpana Verma, Norina Gasteiger
摘要中文翻译慢性乙型肝炎 疾病需要终身监测,以预防肝硬化和肝细胞癌等并发症。尽管有结构化的护理途径,英国二级护理中的缺勤仍然具有挑战性。本次范围界定审查旨在综合影响不参加二级护理预约的因素的现有证据,以及提高 慢性乙型肝炎 患者预约依从性的策略。资格标准:经过同行评审的资料来源检查了英国二级护理服务中患有乙型肝炎的成年人的就诊情况。灰色文献被排除在外。证据来源:在 CINAHL Ultimate、Medline、Embase、Scopus 和 APA PsycINFO 中进行检索。使用结构化数据提取形式绘制数据图表并进行叙述性综合。十一项研究符合纳入标准。已确定的障碍包括与患者相关的因素(例如,有限的健康素养、污名化、相互竞争的优先事项)、系统层面的挑战(例如,预约安排和可及性)以及文化或语言障碍。心理因素,包括对 诊断 和 治疗 副作用的恐惧,也有报道。报告的策略包括患者教育、根据文化量身定制的干预措施、提醒系统(例如短信和电话)以及涉及社区外展的综合护理模式。慢性乙型肝炎 患者未接受二级护理的情况受到个人、社会和系统因素复杂相互作用的影响。解决多重障碍的干预措施,特别是那些纳入文化敏感方法和提醒系统的干预措施,可能会提高参与度。需要进一步研究与受影响社区共同设计和评估干预措施。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述调查了英国 慢性乙型肝炎 成人不参加二级护理预约的情况,重点关注影响预约依从性的因素和提高参与度的策略。核心问题解决了英国二级护理途径中患者、社会和系统性障碍对就诊和干预方法的相互作用。
证据基础与方法
该范围界定综述检索了 CINAHL Ultimate、Medline、Embase、Scopus 和 APA PsycINFO,查找有关接受英国二级护理服务的成人乙型肝炎患者的同行评审研究,不包括灰色文献。其中包括十一项研究,并使用结构化提取形式和叙述综合来绘制数据图表。
共识与结论
缺勤的原因包括患者相关因素(例如,有限的健康素养、污名化、相互竞争的优先事项)、系统层面的挑战(例如,日程安排和可及性)、文化或语言障碍以及心理因素,例如对 诊断 和 治疗 副作用的恐惧。患者教育、针对文化的干预措施、提醒系统(例如短信和电话)以及涉及社区外展的综合护理模式等策略可能会提高参与度。
争议与缺口
该审查没有报告干预措施的汇总估计或比较有效性,从而使提醒系统或文化定制方法等策略的影响程度存在不确定性。此外,证据基础仅限于 11 项研究,并且综合并未解决干预设计或人群特征的异质性。
科研与实践启示
优先研究应与受影响社区共同设计和评估干预措施,以解决多因素障碍。监测 和实践应优先考虑文化敏感的方法和提醒系统,同时将社区外展纳入二级护理途径,以提高预约依从性。

Attendance Among People With Hepatitis B in UK Secondary Care: A Scoping Review.

Authors: Emma Victoria Shiel, Bini George, Arpana Verma, Norina Gasteiger
Original abstract (English)
Chronic Hepatitis B disease requires lifelong monitoring to prevent complications such as cirrhosis and hepatocellular carcinoma. Despite structured care pathways, non-attendance within UK secondary care remains challenging. This scoping review aimed to synthesise existing evidence on factors influencing non-attendance at secondary care appointments and strategies to improve appointment adherence among people living with Chronic Hepatitis B. Eligibility Criteria: Peer-reviewed sources examining adults with Hepatitis B attendance at UK secondary care services. Grey literature was excluded. Sources of Evidence: Searches were performed in CINAHL Ultimate, Medline, Embase, Scopus and APA PsycINFO. Data were charted using a structured data extraction form and synthesised narratively. Eleven studies met the inclusion criteria. Identified barriers included patient-related factors (e.g., limited health literacy, stigmatisation, competing priorities), system-level challenges (e.g., appointment scheduling and accessibility) and cultural or language barriers. Psychological factors, including fear of diagnosis and treatment side effects, were also reported. Reported strategies included patient education, culturally tailored interventions, reminder systems (e.g., SMS and telephone calls) and integrated care models involving community outreach. Non-attendance among people with chronic Hepatitis B in secondary care is influenced by a complex interplay of personal, social and systemic factors. Interventions that address multiple barriers, particularly those that incorporate culturally sensitive approaches and reminder systems, may improve engagement. Further research is needed to co-design and evaluate interventions with affected communities.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines non-attendance at secondary care appointments among adults with chronic hepatitis B in the UK, focusing on factors influencing appointment adherence and strategies to improve engagement. The central question addresses the interplay of patient, social, and systemic barriers to attendance and intervention approaches within UK secondary care pathways.
Evidence base and method
This scoping review searched CINAHL Ultimate, Medline, Embase, Scopus, and APA PsycINFO for peer-reviewed studies on adults with hepatitis B attending UK secondary care services, excluding grey literature. Eleven studies were included, and data were charted using a structured extraction form with narrative synthesis.
Consensus and conclusions
Non-attendance is driven by patient-related factors (e.g., limited health literacy, stigmatisation, competing priorities), system-level challenges (e.g., scheduling and accessibility), cultural or language barriers, and psychological factors such as fear of diagnosis and treatment side effects. Strategies like patient education, culturally tailored interventions, reminder systems (e.g., SMS and telephone calls), and integrated care models involving community outreach may improve engagement.
Controversies and gaps
The review does not report pooled estimates or comparative effectiveness of interventions, leaving uncertainty about the magnitude of impact for strategies such as reminder systems or culturally tailored approaches. Additionally, the evidence base is limited to 11 studies, and the synthesis does not address heterogeneity in intervention design or population characteristics.
Research and practice implications
Priority research should co-design and evaluate interventions with affected communities to address multifactorial barriers. Surveillance and practice should prioritise culturally sensitive approaches and reminder systems, while integrating community outreach into secondary care pathways to enhance appointment adherence.

尼日利亚医护人员对乙型肝炎 疫苗 的认识、知识、接受度和吸收率:范围界定审查

Awareness, knowledge, acceptance, and uptake of hepatitis B vaccines among healthcare workers in Nigeria: a scoping review
作者： Idowu Olapemi Makinde, Charles Chibuisi Ehiemere, Efosa Ekhator, Kenneth Egwuda, Ishaku Adamu Akyala
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎疫苗自20世纪起已获准公开使用;然而,尼日利亚的许多医护工作者仍未意识到这一重要科学发现的重要性。本范围审查旨在评估尼日利亚医护工作者对乙型肝炎疫苗的认知、知识、接受度及接种情况。本范围审查依据《系统综述与荟萃分析扩展报告规范(PRISMA-ScR)》指南进行报告。相关同行评议研究(发表时间为2021年1月至2025年9月)通过医学主题词(MeSH)术语、关键词、截断词及布尔运算符从五个公共数据库中检索。研究采用人群-概念-背景(PCC)框架设计纳入标准。从每篇论文中提取相关数据,并进行主题分析以总结研究结果。本审查共纳入22项研究,总样本量为5,537例,均为横断面研究设计。研究主要来自尼日利亚南部地区。9项研究报告了医护工作者(HCWs)对乙型肝炎疫苗的认知情况,认知率在阿布贾为50.7%,在卡杜纳州为100%。16项研究报告了至少接种一剂乙型肝炎疫苗的情况,接种率在阿南布拉州为19.9%,在索科托州为80%。有关医护工作者对乙型肝炎疫苗的知识及接受度的证据不足,仅有两项研究分别报告了这两个主题。综上所述,尼日利亚许多医护工作者已了解乙型肝炎有效疫苗,但部分地区的疫苗接种率仍不尽如人意。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该评价调查了尼日利亚医护人员 (HCW) 对 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 疫苗 的认识、知识、接受度和采用情况,重点关注这一特定人群和环境中 疫苗 相关行为和决定因素的核心问题。
证据基础与方法
此次范围界定审查遵循 PRISMA-ScR 指南,并使用 MeSH 术语、关键词、截断和布尔运算符搜索了 2021 年 1 月至 2025 年 9 月的五个公共数据库。纳入标准是使用人口、概念、背景 (PCC) 框架设计的,并进行了主题分析,总结了 22 项横断面研究的结果,总样本量为 5,537 名医护人员。
共识与结论
该审查报告称,尼日利亚医护人员对 HBV 疫苗 的认知存在很大差异,各州的认知度从 50.7% 到 100% 不等,而某些地区的 疫苗 知晓率并不令人满意,比率低至 19.9%,高至 80%。结论是,虽然存在对 HBV 疫苗 的认识,但某些地区的吸收率仍然不理想。
争议与缺口
证据基础显示,尼日利亚医护人员对 HBV 疫苗 的了解和接受程度的数据不足,只有两项研究报告了每个主题。此外,该审查强调了地理不平衡,因为大多数纳入的研究都来自尼日利亚南部,导致证据存在巨大的区域差距。
科研与实践启示
该审查强调需要采取有针对性的干预措施,以提高尼日利亚医护人员对 HBV 疫苗 的采用率,特别是在采用率较低的地区,并呼吁开展进一步研究,以解决知识和接受评估方面的关键差距。

Awareness, knowledge, acceptance, and uptake of hepatitis B vaccines among healthcare workers in Nigeria: a scoping review

Authors: Idowu Olapemi Makinde, Charles Chibuisi Ehiemere, Efosa Ekhator, Kenneth Egwuda, Ishaku Adamu Akyala
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B vaccines have been licensed for public use since the 20th century; however, many health workers in Nigeria are yet to recognise the importance of this notable scientific discovery. This scoping review aimed to assess the awareness, knowledge, acceptance, and uptake of hepatitis B vaccines among healthcare workers in Nigeria. The scoping review was reported using the Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic Reviews and Meta-Analyses extension for Scoping Reviews (PRISMA-ScR) guidelines. Relevant peer-reviewed studies published from January 2021 to September 2025 were searched from five public databases using Medical Subject Headings (MeSH) terms, keywords, truncations and Boolean operators. The Population, Concept, Context (PCC) framework was used to design the inclusion criteria. Relevant data were extracted from each paper, and thematic analysis was done to summarise the findings. Twenty-two studies with a total sample size of 5,537 and all cross-sectional designs were included in the review. The studies were mainly from Southern Nigeria. The awareness of hepatitis B virus (HBV) vaccines among healthcare workers (HCWs) was described in nine studies and it ranged from 50.7% in Abuja to 100% in Kaduna State. The uptake of at least one dose of the vaccine was an outcome in sixteen studies and it ranged from 19.9% in Anambra State to 80% in Sokoto State. There was insufficient evidence on the knowledge and acceptance of HBV vaccines among HCWs in Nigeria, as only two studies reported on each theme. In conclusion, many healthcare workers in Nigeria were aware of effective vaccines for HBV, however, the uptake of these vaccines remained unsatisfactory in some regions.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines awareness, knowledge, acceptance, and uptake of hepatitis B virus (HBV) vaccines among healthcare workers (HCWs) in Nigeria, focusing on the central question of vaccine-related behaviors and determinants in this specific population and setting.
Evidence base and method
This scoping review followed PRISMA-ScR guidelines and searched five public databases from January 2021 to September 2025 using MeSH terms, keywords, truncations, and Boolean operators. Inclusion criteria were designed using the Population, Concept, Context (PCC) framework, and thematic analysis was performed to summarize findings from 22 cross-sectional studies with a total sample size of 5,537 HCWs.
Consensus and conclusions
The review reports high variability in HBV vaccine awareness among HCWs in Nigeria, ranging from 50.7% to 100% across states, and unsatisfactory vaccine uptake in some regions, with rates as low as 19.9% and as high as 80%. It concludes that while awareness of HBV vaccines is present, uptake remains suboptimal in certain areas.
Controversies and gaps
The evidence base shows insufficient data on knowledge and acceptance of HBV vaccines among HCWs in Nigeria, with only two studies reporting on each theme. Additionally, the review highlights a geographic imbalance, as most included studies were from Southern Nigeria, leaving substantial regional gaps in evidence.
Research and practice implications
The review underscores the need for targeted interventions to improve HBV vaccine uptake among HCWs in Nigeria, particularly in regions with low uptake, and calls for further research to address critical gaps in knowledge and acceptance assessments.

慢性丁型肝炎感染的外周生物标志物:综述。

Peripheral Biomarkers in Chronic Hepatitis D Infection: A Review.
作者： Zillah Cargill, James Lok, Geoffrey Dusheiko, Ivana Carey
摘要中文翻译慢性丁型肝炎是最严重的病毒性肝炎,与乙型肝炎单一感染相比,晚期纤维化、肝硬化和肝细胞癌的发生率更高。它是由丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 引起的,这是一种有缺陷的 RNA 病毒,需要与乙型肝炎 (HBV) 共同感染才能进入肝细胞并传播。使用当前可用的血清学生物标志物准确预测肝病后遗症和 治疗 反应仍然具有挑战性。本文综述了 HDV 的外周生物标志物,包括 HBV 活性的新型生物标志物,探讨了其潜在的临床用途和未来发展的领域。外周生物标志物能够对 HDV 患者进行风险分层、监测或预测 治疗 反应并阐明感染的自然史(包括与 HBV 的复杂关系),将能够实现个体化护理并早期识别那些罹患晚期肝病风险较高的患者。在现有的 治疗 和新兴的 抗病毒药物 疗法的背景下,确定这些生物标志物在更大的、种族多样化的人群中的作用将是未来的当务之急。

范围与问题
慢性丁型肝炎是最严重的病毒性肝炎,与乙型肝炎单一感染相比,与晚期纤维化、肝硬化和肝细胞癌的发生率更高有关。
证据基础与方法
它是由丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 引起的,这是一种有缺陷的 RNA 病毒,需要与乙型肝炎 (HBV) 共同感染才能进入肝细胞并传播。
共识与结论
使用当前可用的血清学生物标志物准确预测肝病后遗症和 治疗 反应仍然具有挑战性。
争议与缺口
本文综述了 HDV 的外周生物标志物,包括 HBV 活性的新型生物标志物,探讨了其潜在的临床用途和未来发展的领域。
科研与实践启示
在现有的 治疗 和新兴的 抗病毒药物 疗法的背景下,确定这些生物标志物在更大的、种族多样化的人群中的作用将是未来的当务之急。

Peripheral Biomarkers in Chronic Hepatitis D Infection: A Review.

Authors: Zillah Cargill, James Lok, Geoffrey Dusheiko, Ivana Carey
Original abstract (English)
Chronic hepatitis D represents the most severe form of viral hepatitis and is associated with higher rates of advanced fibrosis, cirrhosis and hepatocellular carcinoma than hepatitis B monoinfection. It is caused by the hepatitis D virus (HDV), a defective RNA virus that requires co-infection with hepatitis B (HBV) for hepatocyte entry and propagation. Accurate prediction of liver disease sequelae and treatment response using currently available serological biomarkers remains challenging. This article reviews peripheral biomarkers in HDV, including novel biomarkers of HBV activity, exploring their potential clinical utility and areas for future development. Peripheral biomarkers capable of risk stratifying patients with HDV, monitoring or predicting treatment response and elucidating the natural history of infection (including the complex relationship with HBV), would enable individualised care and early identification of those at greater risk of developing advanced liver disease. Establishing the role of these biomarkers in larger, ethnically diverse populations both on existing treatments and in the context of emerging antiviral therapies will be imperative going forward.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Chronic hepatitis D represents the most severe form of viral hepatitis and is associated with higher rates of advanced fibrosis, cirrhosis and hepatocellular carcinoma than hepatitis B monoinfection.
Evidence base and method
It is caused by the hepatitis D virus (HDV), a defective RNA virus that requires co-infection with hepatitis B (HBV) for hepatocyte entry and propagation.
Consensus and conclusions
Accurate prediction of liver disease sequelae and treatment response using currently available serological biomarkers remains challenging.
Controversies and gaps
This article reviews peripheral biomarkers in HDV, including novel biomarkers of HBV activity, exploring their potential clinical utility and areas for future development.
Research and practice implications
Establishing the role of these biomarkers in larger, ethnically diverse populations both on existing treatments and in the context of emerging antiviral therapies will be imperative going forward.

乙型肝炎病毒 血清流行率、乌干达艾滋病毒感染者的相关因素和肝纤维化负担:系统评价

Hepatitis B virus seroprevalence, associated factors, and liver fibrosis burden among people living with HIV in Uganda: a systematic review
作者： Henry Kenric Kinene, Raha Zihindula, Tambwe Patrick Rodrigue, Bigabwa Baharanyi Dominique, Tambwe Patrick
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 和 HIV 合并感染仍然是撒哈拉以南非洲地区肝脏相关发病率和 死亡率 的重要原因。乌干达承担着这两种感染的沉重负担,但有关 HBV 血清流行率、相关因素和 HIV 感染者 (PLHIV) 肝纤维化的证据尚未全面综合。本系统综述旨在总结乌干达关于 HBV 血清流行率、HBV 感染的相关性以及 PLHIV 肝纤维化负担的现有证据。方法 本系统评价是根据系统评价和荟萃分析的首选报告项目 (PRISMA) 2020 声明进行和报告的。我们检索了 PubMed/MEDLINE、Scopus、Web of Science、African Journals Online (AJOL)、WHO Global Index Medicus、Google Scholar 和 medRxiv/bioRxiv,从开始到 2025 年 12 月,以查找在乌干达针对 PLHIV 报告 HBsAg 血清流行率、与 HBV 感染相关的因素或肝纤维化结果进行的研究。合格的研究包括观察性设计。两名评审员独立筛选研究、提取数据并使用乔安娜·布里格斯研究所 (JBI) 关键评估工具评估方法学质量。调查结果以叙述方式综合。结果 共纳入 14 项研究。大多数研究都是横断面的,是在乌干达北部、乌干达中部、乌干达西南部和维多利亚湖捕鱼社区进行的。报告的艾滋病毒感染者中的 HBsAg 血清流行率 因环境而异,乌干达北部和渔业社区的估计值普遍高于一些城市诊所人群的估计值。经常报告的与 HBV 感染相关的因素包括男性、年龄较大、CD4 细胞计数较低以及居住在农村或高风险地区。评估肝纤维化的研究表明,与 HIV 单一感染人群相比,HBV-HIV 合并感染者的显着纤维化负担更大,尽管不同研究中纤维化的定义和诊断方法存在很大差异。结论 现有证据表明,HBV 感染仍然是乌干达 PLHIV 中的一个重要合并症,不同地理区域的 血清流行率 存在显着差异。男性、年龄较大、CD4 细胞计数较低以及居住在高负担环境中是最常报告的相关因素。研究还表明,与 HIV 单一感染人群相比,HBV-HIV 合并感染者的肝纤维化负担更大。然而,对研究结果的解释受到方法异质性和横断面研究的主导地位的限制。系统审评注册PROSPERO CRD420261349477。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述调查了乌干达艾滋病毒感染者 (PLHIV) 中的人类 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染情况,重点关注 血清流行率、相关人口和临床因素以及肝纤维化负担。核心问题涉及乙型肝炎病毒合并感染的程度、其相关因素及其对该人群肝病的影响。
证据基础与方法
本系统评价遵循 PRISMA 2020 指南,并检索了 PubMed/MEDLINE、Scopus、Web of Science、African Journals Online、WHO Global Index Medicus、Google Scholar 和 medRxiv/bioRxiv,从开始到 2025 年 12 月。符合条件的研究是在乌干达 PLHIV 中进行的观察性设计,报告 HBsAg 血清流行率、相关因素或肝纤维化结果。两名评审员独立筛选研究、提取数据并使用 JBI 关键评估工具评估方法学质量。纳入了十四项研究,并对研究结果进行了叙述性综合。
共识与结论
HBV 感染仍然是乌干达 PLHIV 中的一个重要合并症,血清流行率 在不同地理环境中存在很大差异。与一些城市诊所人群相比,乌干达北部和渔业社区的 HBsAg 血清流行率 普遍较高。经常报告的与 HBV 感染相关的因素包括男性、年龄较大、CD4 细胞计数较低以及居住在农村或高风险地区。评估肝纤维化的研究表明,与 HIV 单一感染人群相比,HBV-HIV 合并感染者的显着纤维化负担更大,尽管定义和诊断方法各不相同。
争议与缺口
研究结果的解释受到方法学异质性的限制,包括横断面研究的主导地位以及纤维化定义和诊断方法的可变性。证据基础缺乏评估肝纤维化和相关因素的标准化方法,这限制了结论的可比性和稳健性。
科研与实践启示
需要标准化诊断标准和纵向研究设计来准确量化 HBV 血清流行率、识别危险因素并评估乌干达 PLHIV 患者的肝纤维化负担。监测 计划应优先考虑乌干达北部和渔业社区等高风险地区,以有效地进行干预。

Hepatitis B virus seroprevalence, associated factors, and liver fibrosis burden among people living with HIV in Uganda: a systematic review

Authors: Henry Kenric Kinene, Raha Zihindula, Tambwe Patrick Rodrigue, Bigabwa Baharanyi Dominique, Tambwe Patrick
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Hepatitis B virus (HBV) and HIV coinfection remains an important cause of liver-related morbidity and mortality in sub-Saharan Africa. Uganda carries a substantial burden of both infections, yet evidence on HBV seroprevalence, associated factors, and liver fibrosis among people living with HIV (PLHIV) has not been comprehensively synthesized. This systematic review aimed to summarize available evidence from Uganda on HBV seroprevalence, correlates of HBV infection, and liver fibrosis burden among PLHIV. Methods This systematic review was conducted and reported in accordance with the Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic Reviews and Meta-Analyses (PRISMA) 2020 statement. We searched PubMed/MEDLINE, Scopus, Web of Science, African Journals Online (AJOL), WHO Global Index Medicus, Google Scholar, and medRxiv/bioRxiv from inception to December 2025 for studies conducted in Uganda among PLHIV reporting HBsAg seroprevalence, factors associated with HBV infection, or liver fibrosis outcomes. Eligible studies included observational designs. Two reviewers independently screened studies, extracted data, and assessed methodological quality using the Joanna Briggs Institute (JBI) critical appraisal tools. Findings were synthesized narratively. Results A total of 14 studies were included. Most studies were cross-sectional and were conducted in Northern Uganda, Central Uganda, South-Western Uganda, and Lake Victoria fishing communities. Reported HBsAg seroprevalence among PLHIV varied across settings, with higher estimates generally observed in Northern Uganda and fishing communities than in some urban clinic-based populations. Frequently reported factors associated with HBV infection included male sex, older age, lower CD4 cell count, and rural or high-risk residence. Studies assessing liver fibrosis suggested a greater burden of significant fibrosis among HBV-HIV coinfected individuals compared with HIV-monoinfected populations, although fibrosis definitions and diagnostic methods varied substantially across studies. Conclusions Available evidence indicates that HBV infection remains a significant comorbidity among PLHIV in Uganda, with substantial variation in seroprevalence across geographic settings. Male sex, older age, lower CD4 cell count, and residence in higher-burden settings were among the most frequently reported associated factors. Studies also suggest a greater burden of liver fibrosis among HBV-HIV coinfected individuals than among HIV-monoinfected populations. However, interpretation of findings is limited by methodological heterogeneity and the predominance of cross-sectional studies. Systematic review registration PROSPERO CRD420261349477.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines human hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection among people living with HIV (PLHIV) in Uganda, focusing on seroprevalence, associated demographic and clinical factors, and liver fibrosis burden. The central question addresses the extent of HBV coinfection, its correlates, and its impact on liver disease in this population.
Evidence base and method
This systematic review followed PRISMA 2020 guidelines and searched PubMed/MEDLINE, Scopus, Web of Science, African Journals Online, WHO Global Index Medicus, Google Scholar, and medRxiv/bioRxiv from inception to December 2025. Eligible studies were observational designs conducted in Uganda among PLHIV, reporting HBsAg seroprevalence, associated factors, or liver fibrosis outcomes. Two reviewers independently screened studies, extracted data, and assessed methodological quality using JBI critical appraisal tools. Fourteen studies were included, with narrative synthesis of findings.
Consensus and conclusions
HBV infection remains a significant comorbidity among PLHIV in Uganda, with seroprevalence varying substantially across geographic settings. Higher HBsAg seroprevalence was generally observed in Northern Uganda and fishing communities compared to some urban clinic-based populations. Frequently reported factors associated with HBV infection included male sex, older age, lower CD4 cell count, and rural or high-risk residence. Studies assessing liver fibrosis suggested a greater burden of significant fibrosis among HBV-HIV coinfected individuals compared with HIV-monoinfected populations, though definitions and diagnostic methods varied.
Controversies and gaps
Interpretation of findings is limited by methodological heterogeneity, including the predominance of cross-sectional studies and variability in fibrosis definitions and diagnostic methods. The evidence base lacks standardized approaches to assess liver fibrosis and associated factors, which constrains comparability and robustness of conclusions.
Research and practice implications
Standardized diagnostic criteria and longitudinal study designs are needed to accurately quantify HBV seroprevalence, identify risk factors, and assess liver fibrosis burden among PLHIV in Uganda. Surveillance programs should prioritize high-risk settings such as Northern Uganda and fishing communities to target interventions effectively.

评估实现世界卫生组织 2030 年消除乙型病毒性肝炎目标的现状和障碍:范围界定审查

Assessing the Status of and Impediments to Achieving the WHO Goal to Eliminate Viral Hepatitis B by 2030: A Scoping Review
作者： BERABOSE Charles, Ralf Clemens, Tatiana De Noronha, Sue Ann Costa Clemens
摘要中文翻译该项目登记了一项范围审查,审查世界卫生组织 (WHO) 到 2030 年消除作为公共卫生威胁的病毒性乙型肝炎 (HBV) 目标的进展情况。2016 年,世界卫生组织设定的目标是相对于 2015 年基线,新发慢性感染减少 90%,死亡率 减少 65%;然而,尽管 疫苗 和强效 抗病毒药物 有效,病毒性肝炎 死亡率 仍在继续上升。该综述评估了 2030 年 HBV 消除目标的全球和区域状况和障碍,并将 HBV 与丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 消除轨迹进行了比较。它是按照 Joanna Briggs Institute (JBI) 的方法进行的,并根据 PRISMA 范围界定扩展扩展 (PRISMA-ScR) 进行报告。使用 JBI 人口-概念-背景 (PCC) 框架,在五个数据库和多个机构存储库中检索了 2015 年至 2025 年间发表的同行评审文献、全球和区域 监测 报告以及灰色文献。图表数据根据世卫组织六个区域的世卫组织影响指标(发病率、死亡率、患病率)和服务覆盖指标(HepB3 和及时出生剂量 疫苗接种、诊断、治疗)进行绘制。具体目标是: (i) 确定世卫组织 2030 年乙肝消除目标的全球和区域状况;(ii) 评估 HBV 疫苗接种 策略(HepB3 和出生剂量)的有效性、实施情况和差距及其对 HBV 流行病学 的影响;(iii) 比较乙肝病毒和丙肝病毒的消除工作,以确定不同的挑战和可转移的机会。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该审查重点关注实现世卫组织到 2030 年消除作为公共卫生威胁的乙型病毒性肝炎 (HBV) 目标的现状和障碍。审查审查了实现这一目标的进展情况,评估了 HBV 疫苗接种 策略的有效性,并将乙型肝炎病毒消除工作与丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 消除工作进行了比较。
证据基础与方法
范围界定审查是按照 Joanna Briggs Institute (JBI) 的方法进行的,并根据 PRISMA 范围界定审查扩展 (PRISMA-ScR) 进行报告。它使用 JBI 人口-概念-背景 (PCC) 框架,在五个数据库和多个机构存储库中检索了同行评审文献、全球和区域 监测 报告以及 2015 年至 2025 年间发表的灰色文献。
共识与结论
尽管有有效的 疫苗 和强效 抗病毒药物,病毒性肝炎 死亡率 的数量仍在继续上升。该审查根据世卫组织影响指标(发病率、死亡率、患病率)和服务覆盖指标(HepB3 和及时出生剂量 疫苗接种、诊断、治疗)绘制了世卫组织六个区域的数据,以评估实现 2030 年消除目标的进展情况。
争议与缺口
该审查强调了 HBV 疫苗接种 战略及其实施方面的差距,以及实现世卫组织目标的挑战。它还指出,与丙肝病毒相比,需要对乙肝病毒的消除工作进行更详细的分析,特别是在确定不同的挑战和可转移的机会方面。
科研与实践启示
该审查确定了研究的具体优先事项、监测、诊断、治疗 和政策,以应对实现世卫组织到 2030 年消除乙型肝炎的目标所面临的持续挑战。其中包括提高 疫苗接种 覆盖率、增强诊断和 治疗 的可及性以及加强 监测 系统。

Assessing the Status of and Impediments to Achieving the WHO Goal to Eliminate Viral Hepatitis B by 2030: A Scoping Review

Authors: BERABOSE Charles, Ralf Clemens, Tatiana De Noronha, Sue Ann Costa Clemens
Original abstract (English)
This project registers a scoping review examining progress toward the World Health Organization (WHO) goal of eliminating viral hepatitis B (HBV) as a public health threat by 2030. In 2016, the WHO set targets of a 90% reduction in new chronic infections and a 65% reduction in mortality relative to a 2015 baseline; yet, despite effective vaccines and potent antivirals, viral hepatitis mortality continues to rise. The review assesses the global and regional status of, and impediments to, the 2030 HBV elimination goal, and compares HBV with hepatitis C virus (HCV) elimination trajectories. It was conducted following the Joanna Briggs Institute (JBI) methodology and reported in accordance with the PRISMA extension for Scoping Reviews (PRISMA-ScR). Peer-reviewed literature, global and regional surveillance reports, and grey literature published between 2015 and 2025 were searched across five databases and multiple institutional repositories, using the JBI Population–Concept–Context (PCC) framework. Charted data were mapped against WHO impact indicators (incidence, mortality, prevalence) and service-coverage indicators (HepB3 and timely birth-dose vaccination, diagnosis, treatment) across the six WHO regions. The specific objectives are: (i) to determine the global and regional status against the WHO 2030 HBV elimination targets; (ii) to evaluate the effectiveness, implementation, and gaps of HBV vaccination strategies (HepB3 and birth dose) and their impact on HBV epidemiology; and (iii) to compare HBV and HCV elimination efforts to identify divergent challenges and transferable opportunities.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the status of and impediments to achieving the WHO goal of eliminating viral hepatitis B (HBV) as a public health threat by 2030. It examines progress toward this goal, evaluates the effectiveness of HBV vaccination strategies, and compares HBV elimination efforts with those for hepatitis C virus (HCV).
Evidence base and method
The scoping review was conducted following the Joanna Briggs Institute (JBI) methodology and reported in accordance with the PRISMA extension for Scoping Reviews (PRISMA-ScR). It searched peer-reviewed literature, global and regional surveillance reports, and grey literature published between 2015 and 2025 across five databases and multiple institutional repositories, using the JBI Population–Concept–Context (PCC) framework.
Consensus and conclusions
Despite the availability of effective vaccines and potent antivirals, viral hepatitis mortality continues to rise. The review maps data against WHO impact indicators (incidence, mortality, prevalence) and service-coverage indicators (HepB3 and timely birth-dose vaccination, diagnosis, treatment) across the six WHO regions to assess progress toward the 2030 elimination targets.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights gaps in HBV vaccination strategies and their implementation, as well as challenges in achieving the WHO targets. It also notes the need for more detailed analysis of HBV elimination efforts compared to HCV, particularly in identifying divergent challenges and transferable opportunities.
Research and practice implications
The review identifies specific priorities for research, surveillance, diagnosis, treatment, and policy to address the ongoing challenges in achieving the WHO goal of eliminating HBV by 2030. These include improving vaccination coverage, enhancing diagnostic and treatment access, and strengthening surveillance systems.

传染性生殖器软疣患者的 HIV、乙型肝炎、丙型肝炎和梅毒血清学筛查:一项回顾性横断面研究

Serological Screening for HIV, Hepatitis B, Hepatitis C, and Syphilis in Patients with Genital Molluscum Contagiosum: A Retrospective Cross-Sectional Study
作者： Zuhal Erçin, Mehtap Toprak, Yasin Tiryaki
摘要中文翻译(1)背景:关于传染性生殖器软疣患者性传播感染的现有文献有限。在本研究中,我们旨在评估传染性生殖器软疣患者中人类免疫缺陷病毒、乙型肝炎病毒、丙型肝炎病毒和梅毒等性传播感染的血清学检测结果和筛查率。(2)方法:在这项回顾性横断面研究中,我们回顾了2016年10月至2025年12月在我院皮肤科门诊诊断的年龄≥18岁传染性生殖器软疣患者的病历。记录了年龄、性别以及抗人类免疫缺陷病毒、乙型肝炎表面抗原、抗丙型肝炎病毒和性病研究实验室/快速血浆反应素的血清学检测结果。(3)结果:528例患者中202例(38.3%)进行了血清学筛查,其中11例(5.45%)至少一种性传播感染检测结果呈阳性。(4) 结论:我们的研究结果支持对传染性生殖器软疣患者进行性传播感染的常规筛查,因为这些患者中很大一部分没有接受血清学筛查。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本研究旨在评估生殖器传染性软疣患者中性传播感染的血清学检测结果及筛查率,包括人类免疫缺陷病毒、乙型肝炎病毒、丙型肝炎病毒及梅毒。
证据基础与方法
本回顾性横断面研究纳入了2016年10月至2025年12月在皮肤科门诊诊断为生殖器传染性软疣且年龄≥18岁的患者的医疗记录。记录了年龄、性别及抗人类免疫缺陷病毒、乙型肝炎病毒表面抗原、抗丙型肝炎病毒、性病研究实验室试验/快速血浆反应素的血清学检测结果。
共识与结论
38.3%的患者接受了血清学筛查,5.45%的患者至少一项性传播感染检测结果为阳性。研究结果支持对生殖器传染性软疣患者进行常规性传播感染筛查,因为相当比例的此类患者未接受血清学筛查。
争议与缺口
该研究未提供乙型肝炎病毒感染的患病率的详细信息,亦未说明其对生殖器传染性软疣患者的具体影响。此外,该研究未涉及针对乙型肝炎病毒的靶向干预措施的有效性或必要性。
科研与实践启示
亟需进一步研究以明确乙型肝炎病毒感染在生殖器传染性软疣患者中的患病率及影响。此外,应探索针对该人群的乙型肝炎病毒靶向干预措施及改进筛查方案。

Serological Screening for HIV, Hepatitis B, Hepatitis C, and Syphilis in Patients with Genital Molluscum Contagiosum: A Retrospective Cross-Sectional Study

Authors: Zuhal Erçin, Mehtap Toprak, Yasin Tiryaki
Original abstract (English)
(1) Background: The available literature on sexually transmitted infections in patients with genital molluscum contagiosum is limited. In this study, we aimed to evaluate the serological test results and screening rate for sexually transmitted infections, including human immunodeficiency virus, hepatitis B virus, hepatitis C virus, and syphilis, among patients with genital molluscum contagiosum. (2) Methods: In this retrospective cross-sectional study, we reviewed the medical records of patients aged ≥18 years diagnosed with genital molluscum contagiosum at our dermatology outpatient clinic between October 2016 and December 2025. Age, sex, and serological test results for anti-human immunodeficiency virus, hepatitis B surface antigen, anti-hepatitis C virus, and Venereal Disease Research Laboratory/Rapid Plasma Reagin were recorded. (3) Results: Serological screening was performed in 202 of 528 patients (38.3%), and 11 (5.45%) had positive test results for at least one sexually transmitted infection. (4) Conclusions: Our findings support routine screening for sexually transmitted infections in patients with genital molluscum contagiosum, given that a substantial proportion of these patients do not undergo serological screening.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The study aimed to evaluate the serological test results and screening rate for sexually transmitted infections, including human immunodeficiency virus, hepatitis B virus, hepatitis C virus, and syphilis, among patients with genital molluscum contagiosum.
Evidence base and method
In this retrospective cross-sectional study, the medical records of patients aged ≥18 years diagnosed with genital molluscum contagiosum at the dermatology outpatient clinic between October 2016 and December 2025 were reviewed. Age, sex, and serological test results for anti-human immunodeficiency virus, hepatitis B surface antigen, anti-hepatitis C virus, and Venereal Disease Research Laboratory/Rapid Plasma Reagin were recorded.
Consensus and conclusions
Serological screening was performed in 38.3% of patients, and 5.45% had positive test results for at least one sexually transmitted infection. The findings support routine screening for sexually transmitted infections in patients with genital molluscum contagiosum, given that a substantial proportion of these patients do not undergo serological screening.
Controversies and gaps
The study does not provide detailed information on the prevalence of hepatitis B virus infection or its specific impact on patients with genital molluscum contagiosum. It also does not address the effectiveness or necessity of targeted interventions for hepatitis B virus.
Research and practice implications
There is a need for further research to determine the prevalence and impact of hepatitis B virus infection in patients with genital molluscum contagiosum. Additionally, targeted interventions and improved screening protocols for hepatitis B virus should be explored in this population.

血清HBcrAg对抗病毒药物治疗慢性乙型肝炎的预测价值

Predictive value of serum HBcrAg in antiviral therapy for chronic hepatitis B
作者： Shuojie Wang, Hongxiao Hao, Wen Deng, Lu Zhang, Weihua Cao, Ziyu Zhang, Xinxin Li, Yaqin Zhang, Xin Wei, Linmei Yao
摘要中文翻译慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 仍然是一个重大的全球公共卫生挑战。其抗病毒药物治疗的目标正逐渐从长期病毒抑制转向实现更安全的治疗停药、更准确的风险分层和更深入的功能性治愈。传统的血清学标志物,如 HBV DNA 和 HBsAg,在慢性乙型肝炎中仍然具有重要的临床意义。然而,在长期核苷类似物(NA)治疗后,其反映残留病毒活性和长期结果的能力受到限制,特别是对于持续病毒学抑制或HBsAg明显下降或清除的患者。近年来,乙型肝炎核心相关抗原(HBcrAg)由于其能够反映肝内共价闭合环状DNA(共价闭合环状DNA)的转录活性和残留病毒储存宿主的状态而出现作为 CHB 领域值得注意的新型生物标志物。与此同时,随着聚乙二醇化干扰素 (Peg-IFN) 附加策略和新型联合治疗方法的发展,HBcrAg 在选择最佳患者群体和监测治疗效果方面的潜力日益得到认可。本综述重点介绍 HBcrAg 的生物学基础及其在 CHB 抗病毒药物 治疗中的主要预测价值。并根据目前的研究进展对其临床应用潜力和存在的挑战进行了总结和展望。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述研究了血清乙型肝炎核心相关抗原(HBcrAg)作为 抗病毒药物 治疗人类 慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)生物标志物的作用,重点关注其对 治疗 反应、患者分层和治疗效果监测的预测价值。
证据基础与方法
该综述综合了HBcrAg的最新研究,强调了其生物学基础和在CHB 抗病毒药物治疗中的临床应用潜力,包括Peg-IFN附加策略和新型联合治疗方法。
共识与结论
HBcrAg 因其反映肝内 共价闭合环状DNA 转录活性和残留病毒 储存宿主 状态的能力而受到认可,在接受长期核苷(酸)类似物治疗的患者中,与 HBV DNA 和 HBsAg 等传统标记物相比,其预测价值更高。
争议与缺口
该综述强调了传统血清学标志物(HBV DNA、HBsAg)反映长期 NA 治疗后残留病毒活性和长期结果的能力有限,强调需要进一步验证 HBcrAg 的临床效用。
科研与实践启示
该综述建议优先研究 HBcrAg 在选择 Peg-IFN 附加策略和新型联合疗法的最佳患者群体中的作用,以及将其整合到监测方案中以实现 CHB 的 功能性治愈。

Predictive value of serum HBcrAg in antiviral therapy for chronic hepatitis B

Authors: Shuojie Wang, Hongxiao Hao, Wen Deng, Lu Zhang, Weihua Cao, Ziyu Zhang, Xinxin Li, Yaqin Zhang, Xin Wei, Linmei Yao
Original abstract (English)
Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) remains a significant global public health challenge. The goals of its antiviral therapy are gradually shifting from long-term viral suppression toward achieving safer treatment discontinuation, more accurate risk stratification, and deeper functional cure. Traditional serological markers, such as HBV DNA and HBsAg, remain clinically important in CHB. However, their ability to reflect residual viral activity and long-term outcomes is limited after long-term nucleos(t)ide analog (NA) therapy, especially in patients with sustained virological suppression or marked HBsAg decline or clearance.In recent years, hepatitis B core-related antigen (HBcrAg), due to its capacity to reflect transcriptional activity of intrahepatic covalently closed circular DNA(cccDNA) and the state of the residual viral reservoir, has emerged as a notable novel biomarker in the field of CHB. Concurrently, with the development of pegylated interferon (Peg-IFN) add-on strategies and novel combination cure approaches, the potential of HBcrAg in selecting optimal patient populations and monitoring therapeutic efficacy is increasingly recognized. This review focuses on the biological basis of HBcrAg and its main predictive values in CHB antiviral therapy. It also summarizes and prospects its clinical application potential and existing challenges based on current research progress.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines the role of serum hepatitis B core-related antigen (HBcrAg) as a biomarker in antiviral therapy for chronic hepatitis B (CHB) in humans, focusing on its predictive value for treatment response, patient stratification, and monitoring of therapeutic efficacy.
Evidence base and method
The review synthesizes current research on HBcrAg, emphasizing its biological basis and clinical application potential in CHB antiviral therapy, including Peg-IFN add-on strategies and novel combination cure approaches.
Consensus and conclusions
HBcrAg is recognized for its capacity to reflect intrahepatic cccDNA transcriptional activity and the state of the residual viral reservoir, offering improved predictive value over traditional markers like HBV DNA and HBsAg in patients undergoing long-term nucleos(t)ide analog therapy.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights the limited ability of traditional serological markers (HBV DNA, HBsAg) to reflect residual viral activity and long-term outcomes after prolonged NA therapy, underscoring the need for further validation of HBcrAg's clinical utility.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests prioritizing research on HBcrAg's role in selecting optimal patient populations for Peg-IFN add-on strategies and novel combination therapies, as well as its integration into monitoring protocols for achieving functional cure in CHB.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

如何推进乙型肝炎消除:Helen Nde,公共卫生硕士 - 美国管理护理杂志

What It Will Take to Advance Hepatitis B Elimination: Helen Nde, MPH - The American Journal of Managed Care
新闻简报2026 年 8 月 30 日,《美国管理式医疗杂志》发表了一篇题为“如何推进乙型肝炎消除”的评论,作者是公共卫生专业人士海伦·恩德 (Helen Nde),公共卫生硕士。文章没有提供新的流行病学数据、病例数或 暴发 调查;相反,它概述了实现全球消除乙型肝炎所需的战略和政策考虑。它强调了获取、筛查和 治疗 方面的差距,并呼吁付款人、提供者和公共卫生机构之间协调努力。作为一篇透视文章,它为利益相关者提供指导,而不是报告特定的健康事件。源材料中没有提供有关受影响人群、地点或数量影响的详细信息。

B routine priorityPublic-health news

What It Will Take to Advance Hepatitis B Elimination: Helen Nde, MPH - The American Journal of Managed Care

News Brief
On August 30, 2026, the American Journal of Managed Care released a commentary called “What It Will Take to Advance Hepatitis B Elimination,” written by public‐health professional Helen Nde, MPH. The article does not present new epidemiological data, case counts, or outbreak investigations; instead, it outlines strategic and policy considerations needed to achieve global hepatitis B elimination. It emphasizes gaps in access, screening, and treatment, and calls for coordinated efforts among payers, providers, and public‐health agencies. As a perspective piece, it serves as guidance for stakeholders rather than reporting a specific health event. No details on affected populations, locations, or quantitative impact are provided in the source material.

新闻简报GSK(伦敦证券交易所股票代码:GSK)已在日本获得全球首个 Hibsago(bepirovirsen)作为治疗 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒感染的 功能性治愈 的监管批准。此次批准是在 III 期试验数据之后进行的,该数据表明 功能性治愈 在 慢性乙型肝炎 患者中的发病率显着。该决定是 GSK 管道的重大发展,可能会影响 慢性乙型肝炎 的全球 治疗 标准。然而,文章强调,葛兰素史克仍必须展示贝匹罗韦森的准入、定价和后续适应症与 抗病毒药物 市场中的辉瑞和默克等竞争对手相比如何。此次批准符合葛兰素史克在 疫苗 和特种药物领域的更广泛战略,强化了其对高利润、耐用产品线的关注。文章还指出,虽然批准是积极的一步,但并没有完全解决公司更广泛的风险,包括专利到期和合法现金流出。

B routine priorityPublic-health news

GSK (LSE:GSK) Wins First Global Approval For Chronic Hepatitis B Functional Cure

News Brief
GSK (LSE:GSK) has received the world's first regulatory approval for Hibsago (bepirovirsen) as a functional cure for chronic hepatitis B virus infection in Japan. This approval follows Phase III trial data that demonstrated significant functional cure rates in patients with chronic hepatitis B. The decision is a major development for GSK's pipeline and could influence global treatment standards for chronic hepatitis B. However, the article highlights that GSK must still show how bepirovirsen's access, pricing, and follow-on indications compare with competitors like Pfizer and Merck in antiviral markets. The approval aligns with GSK's broader strategy in vaccines and specialty medicines, reinforcing its focus on high-margin, durable product lines. The article also notes that while the approval is a positive step, it does not fully resolve the company's broader risks, including patent expiries and legal cash outflows.
Original source text (English)

Hibsago approval leans into GSK's vaccines and specialty-medicine Narrative The investment story for GSK rests on the company using its vaccines and specialty-medicine strength to build steadier, higher-margin cash flows from areas with clear unmet need. A first-in-class functional cure for chronic hepatitis B in Japan slots directly into that Narrative. "GSK is well-positioned to benefit from the global rise in demand for vaccines and specialty medicines, driven by an aging population and higher healthcare access in emerging markets, as evidenced by robust ongoing growth in Shingrix, meningitis vaccines, and double-digit expansion in specialty medicines, which supports sustained revenue growth and greater resilience in future cash flows..." Read the full GSK narrative to see the case behind these numbers. This approval supports the part of the GSK thesis that depends on specialty-infectious-disease products complementing vaccines. A functional cure option for hepatitis B broadens the infectious-disease portfolio beyond prevention and treatment of acute illness, which tends to reinforce the idea of more durable, less cyclical product lines compared with some general medicines. Where it leaves the story open is execution and breadth. GSK still needs to show how bepirovirsen access, pricing and follow-on indications stack up against peers like Pfizer and Merck in antiviral markets, and whether this single asset can materially offset risks analysts flagged around patent expiries, legal cash outflows and pressure on older segments. 慢性乙型肝炎 治疗期间接受基于弹性的护理与标准护理的 慢性乙型肝炎 患者的抑郁和药物依从性:一项回顾性 队列研究 研究 - Frontiers

Depression and Medication Adherence among Chronic Hepatitis B Patients Receiving Resilience-Based Care versus Standard Care During Antiviral Therapy: A Retrospective Cohort Study - Frontiers
新闻简报这项回顾性 队列研究 研究于 2026 年 8 月 30 日发表在《医学前沿》上,调查了基于复原力的护理对接受 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 治疗的 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 患者的影响。该研究于 2022 年 7 月至 2025 年 6 月在中国合肥市第三人民医院进行,纳入了 200 名慢性乙型肝炎患者,分为两组:100 名患者在接受标准护理的同时接受了以恢复力为重点的项目,另外 100 名患者仅接受标准护理。观察组的抑郁评分较低(6.78±3.45 vs. 10.23±4.12),药物依从性较高(0.92±0.08 vs. 0.78±0.15),病毒学和生化结果也有所改善,例如较高的病毒学应答率(85.0% vs. 70.0%)和 ALT 正常化率(80.0% vs. 70.0%)。65.0%)。该研究使用多变量回归模型来调整混杂因素,并得出结论,基于复原力的护理与更好的健康结果相关。各组之间的不良事件相似。这些发现基于历史记录,与任何特定的 暴发 或公共卫生事件无关。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该研究于2022年7月至2025年6月期间进行,并进行了6个月的随访。发表日期为2026-08-30。
地点与对象
该研究在中国合肥市合肥市第三人民医院、安徽医科大学第三临床医院进行。涉及 200 名诊断为 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 并在肝病诊所接受治疗的患者。
事件
一项回顾性 队列研究 研究评估了接受 慢性乙型肝炎 治疗的 慢性乙型肝炎 患者除了标准护理外还接受以恢复力为中心的计划与抑郁症状、药物依从性和病毒学结果之间的关联。该研究由《医学前沿》报道。
规模、影响与风险
该研究纳入了 200 名 慢性乙型肝炎 患者。与对照组(100 名患者)相比,观察组(100 名患者)表现出较低的抑郁评分、更高的药物依从性以及更有利的病毒学和生化结果。没有报告具体病例或死亡人数。
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究是一项回顾性队列研究研究,研究结果基于历史记录。使用多变量回归模型对结果进行分析,研究得出结论,基于复原力的护理与改善结果相关。来源状态已满,该研究发表在《医学前沿》上。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Depression and Medication Adherence among Chronic Hepatitis B Patients Receiving Resilience-Based Care versus Standard Care During Antiviral Therapy: A Retrospective Cohort Study - Frontiers

News Brief
This retrospective cohort study, published in Frontiers in Medicine on August 30, 2026, investigated the impact of resilience-based care on patients with chronic hepatitis B (CHB) undergoing antiviral therapy. Conducted between July 2022 and June 2025 at Hefei Third People's Hospital in China, the study included 200 CHB patients divided into two groups: 100 who received resilience-focused programs alongside standard care and 100 who received standard care only. The observation group showed lower depression scores (6.78 ± 3.45 vs. 10.23 ± 4.12), higher medication adherence (0.92 ± 0.08 vs. 0.78 ± 0.15), and improved virological and biochemical outcomes, such as a higher virological response rate (85.0% vs. 70.0%) and ALT normalization rate (80.0% vs. 65.0%). The study used multivariable regression models to adjust for confounding factors and concluded that resilience-based care was associated with better health outcomes. Adverse events were similar between groups. The findings are based on historical records and are not associated with any specific outbreak or public health event.

Hepatobiliary Diseases Depression and Medication Adherence among Chronic Hepatitis B Patients Receiving Resilience-Based Care versus Standard Care During Antiviral Therapy: A Retrospective Cohort Study Xiao Yang 1 PZ Ping Zhao 2 LZ Lei Zhang 1 CF Chunhua Fang 1 1. Hefei Third People's Hospital, Third Clinical Hospital of Anhui Medical University, Hefei, China 2. Nursing Department of Hefei Third People's Hospital, Third Clinical Hospital of Anhui Medical University, Hefei, China Article metrics View details 488 Views The final, formatted version of the article will be published soon. Notify me Select one of your emails You have multiple emails registered with Frontiers: Notify me on publication Please enter your email address: Email If you already have an account, please login You don't have a Frontiers account ? You can register here Abstract Abstract Objective: To evaluate the association between receiving a resilience-focused program in addition to standard care and depressive symptoms and medication adherence among patients with CHB receiving antiviral therapy. Methods: In this retrospective cohort study, 200 patients diagnosed with CHB and treated at our hospital's hepatology clinic between July 2022 and June 2025 were included. Based on historical records, 100 patients who voluntarily completed a structured 12-week resilience promotion program in addition to standard care formed the observation group, and 100 patients who received standard care only formed the control group. Primary outcomes at the 6-month follow-up were depressive symptom severity measured by the Patient Health Questionnaire-9 (PHQ-9) and medication adherence measured by the Medication Possession Ratio (MPR). Secondary outcomes included resilience (Connor-Davidson Resilience Scale-10, CD-RISC-10), virological response, alanine aminotransferase (ALT) normalization, quality of life (SF-36), adverse events, and healthcare utilization. To account for potential confounding by indication and baseline imbalances, multivariable linear, logistic, and Poisson regression models were used, with adjusted effect estimates and 95% confidence intervals reported. Results: At the 6-month follow-up, the observation group had lower depression scores ( 6.78 ± 3.45 vs. 10.23 ± 4.12), higher MPR (0.92 ± 0.08 vs. 0.78 ± 0.15), and higher resilience scores (32.45 ± 5.67 vs. 26.78 ± 6.34). Virological response (85.0% vs. 70.0%; adjusted OR 2.46, 95% CI 1.35 to 4.48) and ALT normalization (80.0% vs. 65.0%; adjusted OR 2.01, 95% CI 1.10 to 3.67) were more frequent in the observation group. SF-36 physical and mental component scores were also higher in the observation group. Adverse event incidence was similar between groups. The observation group had fewer unplanned outpatient visits (P = 0.045), and Poisson regression confirmed fewer outpatient visits in the observation group (adjusted rate ratio 0.72, 95% CI 0.56 to 0.93). However, because allocation was non-randomized, these associations may be affected by self-selection and residual confounding. Conclusion: In this retrospective cohort study, receiving a resilience-focused program in addition to standard care was associated with lower depressive symptoms, better medication adherence, and more favorable virological and biochemical outcomes in patients with CHB. Summary Keywords Chronic hepatitis B, Depression, Resilience intervention, Retrospective study, Treatment Adherence Received 16 March 2026 Accepted 30 August 2026 Copyright © 2026 Yang, Zhao, Zhang and Fang. B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

怎样才能推进消除乙型肝炎:Helen Nde,公共卫生硕士

What It Will Take to Advance Hepatitis B Elimination: Helen Nde, MPH
新闻简报The article, published on August 29, 2026, discusses the ongoing efforts to advance hepatitis B elimination, as reflected by Helen Nde, MPH, of the Center for Disease Analysis Foundation. Nde emphasizes that the core issue is the perception of hepatitis B among stakeholders, highlighting the disease's significant global impact, with over 800,000 annual deaths. She argues that effective tools, such as a vaccine and treatment, already exist to prevent and manage the disease, yet it remains a major public health challenge in the US and globally. The article outlines the need for expanded vaccination access, particularly the birth-dose vaccine in regions where it is not standard, and screening and treatment of pregnant women and adults in endemic areas to prevent mother-to-child transmission and manage chronic infections. Nde also stresses the importance of education and destigmatization to encourage screening and care, noting that stigma, especially among foreign-born populations, is a barrier to these efforts. The discussion frames elimination targets as aspirational goals rather than rigid deadlines, with a focus on making progress toward these objectives.
查看新闻五要素
时间
该文发表于2026-08-29。所讨论的事件被定位为推进乙型肝炎消除目标的持续努力与反思,未提供具体事件日期。
地点与对象
讨论的范围是全球性的,重点是美国和世界各地流行地区的乙型肝炎消除工作。它提到了在耻辱感较高的社区,特别是在美国出生的外国人群中解决该疾病的重要性。
事件
Helen Nde,公共卫生硕士,讨论了扩大 疫苗接种、筛查、治疗 和教育以推进乙型肝炎消除的必要性。她强调,核心问题是利益相关者对乙型肝炎的看法,并强调了预防和管理该疾病的有效工具的可用性。
规模、影响与风险
乙型肝炎每年在全球导致超过800,000人死亡。这是一个重要的公共卫生问题,其主要风险在于慢性感染及进展为严重肝病。尽管现有解决方案,文章指出该疾病在美国和全球仍是一个重要问题。
应对、状态与不确定性
文章概述了采取干预措施的必要性,例如扩大 疫苗接种 的使用范围、对流行地区的孕妇进行筛查以及改进教育和消除污名化工作。它将消除乙型肝炎的现状反映为理想目标,而不是严格的最后期限,没有提及具体的调查或测试状态。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

What It Will Take to Advance Hepatitis B Elimination: Helen Nde, MPH

News Brief
The article, published on August 29, 2026, discusses the ongoing efforts to advance hepatitis B elimination, as reflected by Helen Nde, MPH, of the Center for Disease Analysis Foundation. Nde emphasizes that the core issue is the perception of hepatitis B among stakeholders, highlighting the disease's significant global impact, with over 800,000 annual deaths. She argues that effective tools, such as a vaccine and treatment, already exist to prevent and manage the disease, yet it remains a major public health challenge in the US and globally. The article outlines the need for expanded vaccination access, particularly the birth-dose vaccine in regions where it is not standard, and screening and treatment of pregnant women and adults in endemic areas to prevent mother-to-child transmission and manage chronic infections. Nde also stresses the importance of education and destigmatization to encourage screening and care, noting that stigma, especially among foreign-born populations, is a barrier to these efforts. The discussion frames elimination targets as aspirational goals rather than rigid deadlines, with a focus on making progress toward these objectives.
Original source text (English)
Hepatitis B elimination requires expanded vaccination, screening, treatment, and education, Helen Nde, MPH, said. In the fourth and final segment of her interview with The American Journal of Managed Care ® , Helen Nde, MPH, of the Center for Disease Analysis Foundation, reflected on hepatitis B elimination targets and what fundamentally needs to shift in how the disease is addressed. Part 1: Best Practices to Reengage Lost Hep B Patients: Helen Nde, MPH Part 2: Hepatitis B Re-Engagement Efforts Underreported in Lower-Resource Settings: Helen Nde, MPH Part 3: Hepatitis B Re-Engagement Program Highlights Data, Access Gaps: Helen Nde, MPH Nde framed global elimination targets as aspirational benchmarks rather than rigid deadlines. She said it works better for her sense of hope and possibility to view them as goals to reach toward, ideally within a set timeline, while recognizing the value of getting as close as possible even if the exact target is missed. Nde argued that the core problem is how stakeholders perceive hepatitis B relative to other chronic and infectious diseases. She emphasized the disconnect between the scale of harm the disease causes, as it kills over 800,000 people annually, and the fact that highly effective tools already exist to prevent it: a vaccine and an effective, though not curative, treatment that stops progression to severe liver disease and death. In her view, there is no real justification for hepatitis B remaining as significant a problem as it is, in the US or anywhere else, because the solutions are already well understood. Nde also outlined the specific interventions needed, including expanding vaccination access , particularly the birth-dose vaccine in regions where it is not yet standard, while preserving what already works well in the US. Efforts should also include screening and treating pregnant women in endemic areas to prevent mother-to-child transmission, along with screening and treating the broader adult population with chronic hepatitis B. She discussed the clinical rationale for prioritizing early intervention, explaining that adults who contract hepatitis B typically clear the virus, whereas children who acquire it face a much higher risk of developing chronic infection and progressing to serious liver disease by their 30s or 40s. Lastly, Nde stressed the importance of education and destigmatization. She said she wants patients to understand that hepatitis B is manageable and not something to fear, noting that stigma, particularly tied to foreign-born populations in the US and in communities globally, remains a barrier to people seeking screening and care. “Investing in what we already know works, and making sure people have the information that they have, I think that that's the main thing that needs to happen here,” she concluded

韩国乙型肝炎患者戒烟可降低肝癌风险 - CHOSUNBIZ - Chosunbiz

Quitting smoking cuts liver cancer risk in Korea hepatitis B patients - CHOSUNBIZ - Chosunbiz
新闻简报A study published on 2026-09-03 by Chosunbiz found that quitting smoking is associated with a 22% lower risk of hepatocellular carcinoma in Koreans with chronic hepatitis B. The research, conducted by Samsung Medical Center and Sungkyunkwan University, analyzed 127,196 patients with chronic hepatitis B using National Health Insurance data from 2018 to 2023. Participants were categorized into three groups: those who continued smoking conventional cigarettes (86,338), those who quit (19,521), and those who switched to e-cigarettes (21,337). The median follow-up period was 4.93 years, during which hepatocellular carcinoma occurred in 4,184 individuals. After adjusting for factors like age and sex, the quit-smoking group had an adjusted hazard ratio (aHR) of 0.78, indicating a 22% lower risk compared to the smoking group. The e-cigarette switch group also showed a similar aHR of 0.78, but the study noted that only 12% of those who switched later quit completely, while 61% of initial quitters remained abstinent. The researchers emphasized that complete cessation should be prioritized over switching to e-cigarettes for managing smoking in patients with chronic hepatitis B. The study does not establish a causal relationship between smoking cessation and reduced cancer risk, as it was retrospective in nature.
查看新闻五要素
时间
The study was published on 2026-09-03, and the median follow-up period was 4.93 years, with data spanning from 2018 to 2023.
地点与对象
该研究重点关注 127,196 名患有 慢性乙型肝炎 的韩国人,特别是那些基线时有吸烟史的人,并使用国民健康保险数据进行了分析。
事件
一项回顾性 队列研究 研究发现,戒烟与 慢性乙型肝炎 患者患肝细胞癌的风险降低 22% 相关,但并未建立因果关系。该研究还指出,改用电子烟也显示出类似的风险降低,但戒烟的一致性较差。
规模、影响与风险
在127,196名参与者中,随访期间4,184人发生肝细胞癌。戒烟组的调整风险比(aHR)为0.78,表明其肝细胞癌风险较继续吸食传统卷烟者降低22%。转用电子烟组的aHR同样为0.78,显示风险降低程度相似。
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究为回顾性队列分析,无法证明戒烟或转用电子烟与降低肝细胞癌风险之间存在直接因果关系。研究结果已发表于国际期刊《Preventive Medicine》。

Quitting smoking cuts liver cancer risk in Korea hepatitis B patients - CHOSUNBIZ - Chosunbiz

Original source text (Korean)
Quitting smoking cuts liver cancer risk in Korea hepatitis B patients - CHOSUNBIZ 2026년 9월 4일(금) KR JP 이코노미조선 KR JP Industry Finance Real Estate IT Retail Science Policy Society International Entertainment Culture Sports EnglishScience Quitting smoking cuts liver cancer risk in Korea hepatitis B patients Study of 127,196 Koreans with hepatitis B links quitting smoking to markedly lower hepatocellular carcinoma risk By Heo Ji-yoon Published 2026.09.03. 10:12 Prefer Chosunbiz EN on Google A domestic retrospective cohort study finds that among patients with chronic hepatitis B, those who quit smoking have a 22% lower risk of developing hepatocellular carcinoma than those who continue to smoke conventional cigarettes./Courtesy of pixabay A study found that among people with chronic hepatitis B, those who quit smoking had a 22% lower risk of developing hepatocellular carcinoma than those who kept smoking conventional cigarettes. Hepatocellular carcinoma is the most common primary liver cancer that arises in the liver, and chronic hepatitis B is one of the main risk factors. In this study, even those who switched from conventional cigarettes to e-cigarettes had a 22% lower risk of hepatocellular carcinoma. However, the risk in the e-cigarette switch group was not lower than in the quit-smoking group. Researchers at Samsung Medical Center and Sungkyunkwan University analyzed the association between changes in smoking status and the risk of hepatocellular carcinoma in people with chronic hepatitis B using National Health Insurance data, and published the findings in the international journal "Preventive Medicine." The study included Song Byung-geun, assistant professor; Kwak Geum-yeon, professor; and Shin Dong-hyun, professor, in the Division of Gastroenterology at Samsung Medical Center, and Professors Lee Myung-chul, Cho Ju-hee, and Kang Dan-bi in the Department of Clinical Research Design and Evaluation at Sungkyunkwan University. The team analyzed 127,196 people with chronic hepatitis B who had a history of smoking at baseline, drawn from National Health Insurance data from 2018 to 2023. Participants were classified into 86,338 who continued smoking conventional cigarettes, 19,521 who quit, and 21,337 who switched from conventional cigarettes to e-cigarettes. The median follow-up was 4.93 years, during which hepatocellular carcinoma occurred in 4,184 people. After adjusting for relevant factors such as age, sex, and smoking amount, the risk of hepatocellular carcinoma was 22% lower in the quit-smoking group compared with the group that continued smoking conventional cigarettes. The adjusted hazard ratio (aHR) was 0.78. The adjusted hazard ratio compares disease risk between two groups after accounting for other factors; 0.78 means the group's risk is 0.78 times that of the reference group, whose risk is set at 1. 2026 Major Banks Launch 5 Year Jumbo CD Rates (Take A Look)AdvisorBooth | Jumbo CD Learn More The adjusted hazard ratio in the e-cigarette switch group was also 0.78, indicating a 22% lower risk of hepatocellular carcinoma than in the group that continued smoking conventional cigarettes. However, because this was a retrospective cohort study, it does not prove a causal relationship that quitting smoking or switching to e-cigarettes directly reduces the risk of hepatocellular carcinoma. The researchers conducted an additional analysis of 83,540 people who maintained their smoking behavior consistently. In this analysis, those who consistently remained abstinent had a 36% lower risk of hepatocellular carcinoma than the group that continued smoking conventional cigarettes (aHR 0.64), and those who consistently remained switched to e-cigarettes had a 27% lower risk (aHR 0.73). Although the risk reduction was greater in the quit-smoking group, the difference between the two groups was not statistically significant. There was a difference in maintaining smoking behavior. Only 12% of those who switched to e-cigarettes later quit completely, but 61% of those who initially quit remained abstinent at follow-up. About 37% of those who switched to e-cigarettes later returned to conventional cigarettes. The researchers said, "In smoking management for people with chronic hepatitis B, complete cessation should be prioritized over switching to e-cigarettes." 乙型肝炎母亲为了保护婴儿而与成本和恐惧作斗争 - Streamlinefeed.co.ke

Hepatitis B Mothers Fight Cost And Fear To Protect Babies - streamlinefeed.co.ke
新闻简报This news report highlights the challenges faced by Kenyan mothers living with Hepatitis B, including the high cost of viral load testing and social stigma. Despite these obstacles, county programs in Baringo are successfully preventing mother-to-child transmission through early identification, treatment, and the administration of birth dose vaccines within 24 hours of delivery. The report cites Kenya's pooled Hepatitis B prevalence at 7.8 percent, with hotspots like Baringo exceeding 10 percent. In Baringo, 66 pregnant and breastfeeding women are on treatment, and all babies tested at nine months have remained negative. Health officials emphasize the importance of universal antenatal screening and free birth dose immunoprophylaxis to eliminate the virus. The report also notes that the cost of viral load testing, at Sh20,000, is a major barrier for many women, but the protocol works effectively when this cost is removed. The story underscores the need for accessible healthcare and the impact of early intervention in reducing the risk of transmission.
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布于 2026-08-30。事件日期没有明确说明,但故事指的是 2026 年 8 月 31 日在 Kawangware 产前护理期间制作的 诊断。
地点与对象
肯尼亚,特别是 Kawangware 诊所和巴林戈县。该人群包括患有乙型肝炎的孕妇及其新生儿。
事件
肯尼亚的乙型肝炎母亲面临病毒载量检测和 Z​​XQTERMVQXZ 的高昂费用和耻辱,但县计划通过早期识别、治疗 和出生剂量 疫苗 成功预防母婴 传播。该报告和肯尼亚卫生当局的官方声明证实了这一事件。
规模、影响与风险
Kenya's pooled Hepatitis B prevalence is 7.8 percent, with hotspots like Baringo above 10 percent. Untreated pregnant women risk transmitting the virus to their children. In Baringo, 66 pregnant and breastfeeding women are on treatment, and all babies tested at nine months have remained negative. The risk of transmission is mitigated with proper intervention.
应对、状态与不确定性
County programs in Baringo are actively delivering virus-free babies through early testing, treatment, and birth dose vaccines. The response is confirmed by the report and health officials. The cost barrier of Sh20,000 for viral load testing is a significant challenge, but the protocol is effective when the cost is removed.

Skip to main content Global editionThursday, September 3, 2026 Streamline Search⌘KWriteSign inJoin Streamline StreamlineGlobal edition LatestWorldBusinessPoliticsTechnologyScienceCultureSportDirectoryHouse HuntingPeopleHistoryMore LatestWorldBusinessPoliticsTechnologyScienceCultureSport Back to News/World News World News Hepatitis B Mothers Fight Cost And Fear To Protect Babies Kenyan mothers living with Hepatitis B confront a Sh20,000 viral load test and stigma, yet county programmes are delivering virus-free babies. Streamline Feed OfficialVerified Aug 31, 20263 min read Follow on Google News Try askingWhy does this matter? Advertisement from Jabali Towers Apartments. The first 10 seconds cannot be skipped. Ad playing Ad playing. Learn more Build your reading streak0/3 today·Counts at 25% Firdaus Ismail sat alone on a cooling bench in a Kawangware clinic, convinced the nurse had bad news about HIV. The diagnosis was Hepatitis B, and it changed her pregnancy forever. Her story, told to Nation Africa, is the human face of a silent epidemic. Kenya's pooled Hepatitis B prevalence sits at 7.8 percent in systematic review data, with hotspots like Baringo above 10 percent, and every untreated pregnant woman carries a risk of passing the virus to her child at birth. A Diagnosis Mistaken For HIV Ismail, 30, recalled watching other mothers leave the antenatal queue while she waited, certain she had tested positive for HIV. When clinicians explained that the antiviral drugs used to suppress Hepatitis B are similar to HIV medicines, she refused to take them home that day, terrified of the stigma waiting outside the clinic door. The fear is clinical as well as social. Without intervention, a mother with a high viral load can transmit the virus during delivery, and the newborn's only shield is a birth dose vaccine within 24 hours plus, where indicated, maternal antiviral therapy. The 20,000 Shilling Barrier Doctors referred Ismail to Kenyatta National Hospital for a viral load test, then discovered the obstacle that decides outcomes for thousands of women: the test costs Sh20,000, about 155 US dollars, and she could not afford it. For a family already funding a pregnancy, that single line item can sever the clinical pathway WHO recommends. Kenya pooled HBV prevalence: 7.8 percent, per a Frontiers systematic review Pregnant women prevalence: 6.5 percent pooled, above 10 percent in hotspots like Baringo, per KEMRI Viral load test cost at referral: Sh20,000 Protection window: birth dose vaccine within 24 hours of delivery Baringo's Quiet Success In Baringo, one of Kenya's hotspots, more than 700 people are on Hepatitis B treatment, including 66 pregnant and breastfeeding women, county Prevention of Mother-to-Child Transmission coordinator Gladys Tuitoek said. More than 30 of those mothers have delivered, and every baby tested at nine months has remained negative, a result Tuitoek attributes to first visit identification, treatment where needed and the birth dose within 24 hours. Baringo Health Executive Dr Solomon Sirma says every pregnant woman should know her status, because with proper treatment and follow-up, women living with Hepatitis B can deliver children free from the virus. The county's data is small but exact, and it proves the protocol works when the cost barrier is removed. The Birth Dose Gap WHO guidance is unambiguous: a monovalent birth dose within 24 hours, followed by the full infant series, prevents transmission that no later vaccine can undo. KEMRI scientists argue that expanding timely birth dose access is the cornerstone of elimination, yet Kenya's routine schedule has historically started the pentavalent vaccine at six weeks, leaving the first weeks of life uncovered. The gap is where money meets medicine. Viral load testing at Sh20,000, transport to referral hospitals and the birth dose logistics for home deliveries, still nearly half of births in some counties, are the three doors a mother must pass through, and each one has a price. For readers in London or Atlanta, the contrast is instructive. Universal antenatal screening and free birth dose immunoprophylaxis made mother-to-child Hepatitis B rare in high income health systems, the same playbook Baringo is running on a county budget. Ismail delivered safely, and her newborn tested negative. Her advice from the same bench where the fear began is now simple: never skip the clinic, because early testing turned what she once believed was a death sentence into a healthy child. Event
Hepatitis B mothers in Kenya face high costs and stigma for viral load testing and treatment, yet county programs are successfully preventing mother-to-child transmission through early identification, treatment, and birth dose vaccines. The event is confirmed by the report and official statements from Kenya's health authorities.

Kenya's pooled Hepatitis B prevalence is 7.8 percent, with hotspots like Baringo above 10 percent. Untreated pregnant women risk transmitting the virus to their children. In Baringo, 66 pregnant and breastfeeding women are on treatment, and all babies tested at nine months have remained negative. The risk of transmission is mitigated with proper intervention.
Response, status and uncertainty
County programs in Baringo are actively delivering virus-free babies through early testing, treatment, and birth dose vaccines. The response is confirmed by the report and health officials. The cost barrier of Sh20,000 for viral load testing is a significant challenge, but the protocol is effective when the cost is removed.

Peak Bodies Welcome Hepatitis B Drug Access Boost
新闻简报On 01 Sep 2026, Mirage News reported that Australian clinicians will no longer require specialist accreditation to prescribe hepatitis B antiviral medications. This change aims to improve access to treatment for nearly 230,000 people living with chronic hepatitis B in Australia. The reform is expected to reduce reliance on specialist services, as currently over 75% of hepatitis B treatment in Australia is written by specialists. The report highlights that only one in four people with chronic hepatitis B receive guideline-recommended care, and less than half of those eligible for treatment are receiving it. By allowing more general practitioners and nurse practitioners to prescribe, the goal is to bring care closer to home, reduce pressure on specialist clinics, and ensure more Australians can access the treatment and monitoring they need. The change is part of broader efforts to achieve the 2030 hepatitis B elimination targets in Australia.

B routine priorityPublic-health news

Peak Bodies Welcome Hepatitis B Drug Access Boost

News Brief
On 01 Sep 2026, Mirage News reported that Australian clinicians will no longer require specialist accreditation to prescribe hepatitis B antiviral medications. This change aims to improve access to treatment for nearly 230,000 people living with chronic hepatitis B in Australia. The reform is expected to reduce reliance on specialist services, as currently over 75% of hepatitis B treatment in Australia is written by specialists. The report highlights that only one in four people with chronic hepatitis B receive guideline-recommended care, and less than half of those eligible for treatment are receiving it. By allowing more general practitioners and nurse practitioners to prescribe, the goal is to bring care closer to home, reduce pressure on specialist clinics, and ensure more Australians can access the treatment and monitoring they need. The change is part of broader efforts to achieve the 2030 hepatitis B elimination targets in Australia.
Original source text (English)
Mirage News Mirage News Mirage News Health 01 Sep 2026 3:32 pm AEST Date Time Hepatitis Australia - ASHM From today, Australian clinicians will no longer need to obtain specialist accreditation to prescribe hepatitis B antiviral prescriptions. This change comes after years of advocacy from peak bodies and patient advocacy groups to improve access to treatments for people living with hepatitis B. Hepatitis B is the most common blood-borne virus in Australia with nearly 230,000 people in Australia living with chronic hepatitis B. If not managed properly, it can lead to serious liver disease or liver cancer. The change will mean all medical and authorised nurse practitioners are able to prescribe PBS-subsidised antiviral medicines for eligible patients, reducing barriers to care. Currently, only one in four people living with chronic hepatitis B are receiving guideline-recommended clinical care and less than half of those eligible for treatment are receiving it [i] . Expanding prescribing to all medical and nurse practitioners will remove barriers to treatment, improve access to life-saving care, and support Australia's efforts to achieve the 2030 hepatitis B elimination targets. ASHM CEO, Alexis Apostolellis, is optimistic the change will strengthen the role of primary care in hepatitis B management. With specialists currently writing more than 75% of Australia's hepatitis B treatment in 2021 1 , he said the reform will reduce reliance on specialist services. "While we have seen growing involvement from primary care in the management of chronic hepatitis B, care is still largely concentrated in specialist settings where some hospitals have waitlists that exceed two years," said Mr Apostolellis. "By allowing more GPs and nurse practitioners to prescribe hepatitis B treatments, we can bring care closer to home, reduce pressure on specialist clinics, and help more Australians access the treatment and monitoring they need." Timely access to hepatitis B antiviral treatment is critical to preventing liver flares, liver damage and other serious health complications. There are devastating consequences that can result when continuity of treatment is disrupted. Hepatitis Australia CEO Lucy Clynes said that this change will enable people living with hepatitis B to access care from the providers they trust. "With only one in four people with hepatitis B receiving the care they need, making access simple is essential. Many people with hepatitis B rely on GPs who speak their language or are close to home. This change means they can access care through the providers they already know and trust, just as they would for any other chronic condition," said Ms Clynes. "The first functional cures are on the horizon, but they will be most effective for people whose hepatitis B is well managed." Removing the accreditation requirement will help ensure that more clinicians can confidently prescribe and continue treatment, strengthening access to care and helping prevent avoidable illness and deaths among people living with chronic hepatitis B. ASHM will continue to offer clinical education, support and expert guidance to support hepatitis B prescribing in primary care. /Public Release. 干血斑可靠地量化 HBV DNA 并检测 HBeAg - Bioengineer.org

Dried blood spots reliably quantify HBV DNA and detect HBeAg - Bioengineer.org
新闻简报在埃塞俄比亚进行的一项研究表明,通过简单的手指刺破获得的干血斑可以可靠地定量 HBV DNA 并检测 HBeAg,这是 乙型肝炎病毒 感染的两个关键标志物。由 Asegedech Asmamaw 及其同事领导的这项研究于 2026 年 9 月 3 日由 Bioengineer.org 发表,将干血斑与 Sebeta 健康中心 143 名 HBV 感染者的血浆样本进行了比较。研究结果表明,干血斑在所有 89 个样本中均检测出了 HBV DNA,其中血浆病毒载量超过了 WHO 治疗 阈值 3.3 log10 IU/ml,两种方法之间具有很强的相关性(0.90)。从干燥样本中检测 HBeAg 的灵敏度为 85.7%,特异性为 92.9%,表明性能良好,但并不完美。该研究强调了干血斑在资源有限的环境中改善乙型肝炎监测的潜力,因为它们消除了冷藏、专门静脉切开术和快速运输的需要。然而,作者建议,对于某些应用,尤其是在检测截止点附近,验证性血浆测试可能仍然是必要的。该研究是使用 Xpert HBV 病毒载量测定和 Alinity i HBeAg 免疫测定进行的,这两种方法均已在低收入国家用于结核病和 HIV 检测。结果支持干血斑用于 HBV DNA 定量和 HBeAg 检测的可靠性,但建议在现实条件下进行进一步验证。
